# Common Mistakes

_mistakes, traps and misconceptions explicitly called out._

Evidence base: 1207 extracted items from 702 videos by #7Phantom, 1Coal, 1_Bruce, Alex Builds Games, AllThingsHow (guide), AlvinBlox, Amora, ArTheDev, Arow, B Ricey, BloxxedDev, BrawlDev, Business of Creators, ByteBlox, CORSO, CaberJaber, CallMeDogey, Creators on the Blox, Crusherfire, Cumin, Daiorg, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Emancyphur, Erthlord, GDC Festival of Gaming, Game Maker's Toolkit, GameMakers, Generalist Programmer, GnomeCode, GosuGamers, ItsZyonic, JustDucksey, Last Level Studios, Leif, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, Matt The Method, MonarchDev, MoseyTube, Mulch, Perstile, PineappleDev, Poke, PolarBear, Possible Panda, Quez, Raul, RoWatcher (tracker blog), Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox Investor Relations, Roox4, RoyalDev, Seibo, SimTek Game Development, SmartyRBX, Suphi Kaner, ThatGuyEvan, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevKing, ThePurpleGuy, Tizzy, VuukStudios, Xaiyne, Yann, anomalousDucky, bubDEV, eGamers, janisjanis01, kra_DeZ, rasdev, tef, waveshine, {EISABOOS} "RBX" (2015-01-03 to 2026-10-30). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Creators and Roblox staff converge on recurring traps. Design: over-complex or dream first projects, feature creep, bloat, cloning or template reskins, chasing trends. Monetisation: pay-to-win, too many offers, fake urgency, RNG rewards, abusable purchases. Growth: ads to fix a weak game, over-hype, release-and-walk-away. Data: reading experiments too early, ignoring novelty effects. UX: cluttered, inconsistent or non-mobile UI, ignoring console and controller. Compliance: IP, the maturity questionnaire, ad and notification standards, account security. Technical: unanchored parts, unbounded pet counts, client-side purchase checks, static configs, rollback sound effects. Business: partnerships, contracts, scams. Mechanics statements from [OFFICIAL] sources override indie claims. Indie revenue and CCU figures are self-reported and unverified.

## Rules and findings
- **Do not make a huge dream or complex game as your first project** (multiple-creators): Start small and build up gradually. Large first projects (camping simulator, custom systems, handcrafted assets) were never finished or got 0-2 players. Even "small" projects can balloon through feature creep (one grew into a 200-day build). Newer developers tend to over-complicate. Sources: [SmartyRBX 2023-10 0:37](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=37), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 23:49](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1429), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 1:17](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=77), [SmartyRBX 2025-06 19:46](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=1186), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 23:49](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1429). Newest source 2026-03-14.
- **Do not spend months polishing before launch; validate demand and ship** (multiple-creators): Release something you're proud of, then iterate on feedback; avoid feature creep and endless polish. Real data arrives only after players do. Building only for what you find fun is the common failure of unplayed polished games. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:02:57](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3777), [Tizzy 2026-07 13:39](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=819), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=0), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 1:17](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=77), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=0). Newest source 2026-07-06.
- **Do not rely on ads or Robux spend to rescue a weak game** (multiple-creators): Ad spikes without retention burn budget and players fall back to zero. Fix the game loop first. Self-reported examples: about 3,135 Robux spent, 182 recovered; 3,000 Robux of ads earned 28 Robux. Unverified promo services produced almost nothing. Tizzy says not updating the game is why ad spend does not get you on the algorithm. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2025-04 3:45](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=225), [TheDankDeveloper 2024-07 8:57](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=537), [ItsZyonic 2024-07 2:14](https://youtu.be/Cd6mpD4doGs?t=134), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Tizzy 2026-05 18:04](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=1084). Newest source 2026-05-16.
- **Avoid pay-to-win, over-monetisation and stacked offers** (multiple-creators): Players call P2W and leave. Free players quitting makes paying players churn too, leaving a dead game. Prioritise gameplay over monetisation; don't focus on monetisation before traction. Recover trust by lowering overpriced items and adding free content. Extra cheap offers distract from best sellers; one-time game passes need constant new players. If conversion is low, check price, shop visibility and purchase flow. See disagreements for the over-restraint risk. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902), [GameMakers 2020-03 12:18](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=738), [Roblox Creators 2022-11 0:43](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=43), [Roblox Creators 2023-01 0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0), [Roblox Creators 2023-01 0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't use fake urgency, pushy language, deceptive notifications or spam** (multiple-creators): No inaccurate stock claims and no inaccurate or auto-restarting countdowns. With minors use 'View Item', 'See Price', 'Open Shop' instead of 'GET IT NOW'. No disguised ads, bait-and-switch rewards or pre-loaded purchases. Avoid purely informational notifications; prompt an action. Clickbait ads damage trust. Spamming ads, users, groups or pop-ups drives players away; test rewarded ad flows in Studio and tune ad load carefully. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications), [Roblox 2018-07 22:51](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1371). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Rewarded ads: deterministic, account-bound, no RNG** (multiple-creators): Under 2026 standards state the exact reward ('Watch to receive [Specific Item]'); no 'Random Legendary Item'. Tickets for a later draw are allowed. Rewards must be developer products, not Robux. Tradable ad currency enables alt farming. Don't let ads hurt the character. Not allowed in free-form creation or AI-interaction experiences. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-08 17:45](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1065), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't clone, template or reskin hits, or chase trends** (multiple-creators): Near-duplicate experiences with similar metadata or place files rank lower. Tizzy's $600 template flopped and a reskin of a top game failed. Almost every game is derivative, so differentiate on fantasy and mechanic. Zeal regrets Pressure resembling Doors. Trends fade by the time copies appear (cleaning sims); trend-of-the-week updates make games hollow; constant identity changes stop audiences forming. Don't copy successful studios' process. Republishing uncopylocked games breaks rules. Sources: [Tizzy 2026-06 1:21](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=81), [Tizzy 2026-07 4:33](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=273), [Tizzy 2026-06 1:14](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=74), [Tizzy 2026-09 13:02](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=782), [Tizzy 2026-09 13:02](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=782). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep scope on the core loop; don't bolt on off-core features** (multiple-creators): Don't add community requests that don't fit (pet update, housing rejected). Don't add a second mode to a horror game. Don't cram many systems at once. Avoid bloat and near-identical content loops. Model maintenance cost of classes and dungeons before expanding. Don't implement every suggestion; it can break the economy. One game broke after a combat system was added. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-11 17:37](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1057), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 24:10](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1450), [kra_DeZ 2026-10 6:47](https://youtu.be/dE0njfPNsdY?t=407), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 5:55](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=355), [Roblox 2025-09 34:07](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=2047). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Don't release and walk away; keep updating wisely** (multiple-creators): The first two weeks decide whether a game gets players. Neglecting updates kills games, even ones making money; one game died after updates stopped. Without LiveOps, players lose interest. Don't release variations of the same content weekly (burnout), or cosmetic-only updates. But don't hotfix without research. Sources: [MoseyTube 2026-08 5:41](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=341), [Tizzy 2026-08 15:57](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=957), [kra_DeZ 2026-10 1:37](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=97), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-essentials), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 9:34](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=574). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't judge or abandon a quiet game before promoting it and checking quality** (multiple-creators): Most failures come from nobody knowing it exists; good games can flop at launch and blow up later; items can have zero sales then trend weeks later. Conversely many quitters simply have weak games. Don't blame players, the algorithm or ads. Don't sell early on a mid-metric game (one recovered from 300 CCU, self-reported). Sources: [kra_DeZ 2026-09 9:06](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=546), [GameMakers 2021-01 34:49](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2089), [Roblox 2024-09 7:26](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=446), [Roblox 2024-09 21:07](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=1267), [waveshine 2025-08 6:36](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=396). Newest source 2026-09-14.
- **Don't over-hype or over-promote early** (multiple-creators): Over-promoting 6-8 months out loses interest and raises expectations; overhyping makes an MVP-then-iterate release harder. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:15:27](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4527), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:07](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=127), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:07](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=127). Newest source 2023-04-27.
- **Don't act on experiment or stats results too early** (multiple-creators): Don't peek after 24 hours; novelty effects mislead. Wait the planned duration, ideally two weeks and until numbers are static. Results swinging between significant and not are unreliable. Segment new vs veteran players (Adopt Me's trade-delay test looked negative because bots/alts were concentrated in control). Don't use the Details tab for decisions. Green overview stats fall once benchmarked against similar games. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-12 14:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=872), [Roblox Creators 2025-12 14:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=872), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox 2025-09 9:03](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=543). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Configure experiments and configs correctly** (multiple-creators): Configs do nothing unless code calls GetValue; snapshots are static, so reload on update events. Conditional values must match the default type; rule order is global. Use GetConfigForPlayerAsync for targeting and experiments, not GetConfigAsync. Delay GetValue until the player reaches the feature. Avoid content changes and overlapping experiments mid-test. Test even obvious changes. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Design UI mobile-first and avoid clutter** (multiple-creators): Mobile is over 70% of users (another creator says ~80%, unverified). Consolidate buttons; avoid left-side movement area and the top chat area; test on a phone; design for phones then scale up. Landscape, not portrait, for new games, but test portrait layouts, which collapse. Avoid HUD text overload and oversized close buttons. Don't prioritise PC for non-combat games. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 35:34](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=2134), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:13:34](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4414), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 22:41](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1361), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 32:04](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1924), [Tizzy 2026-06 13:41](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=821). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep UI consistent, clear and accessible** (multiple-creators): One palette and theme, few colours used for meaning only, consistent padding, alignment, hierarchy, icons and button positions. Contrast frames and buttons; don't stylise until unreadable; clarity over theme. Don't rely on hover-only tooltips. Allow for longer translated text. Colour blindness (over 5%) and disability (over 26%) shares are cited. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 10:43](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=643), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 5:37](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=337), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 6:18](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=378), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 6:59](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=419), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 7:39](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=459). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Support all input devices; don't hard-code device assumptions** (multiple-creators): Don't fork control scripts per platform, write keyboard-only code, or infer device from capabilities. Don't skip console at launch (Wonder Works regretted it). Disable chat window and set maturity info for console games. Research genre and device conventions. Test your UI against core Roblox UI. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 3:59](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=239), [Roblox 2018-07 3:59](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=239), [Roblox 2025-09 27:27](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1647), [Roblox 2025-09 3:46](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=226), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 22:40](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=1360). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't port games from elsewhere or assume other audiences transfer** (multiple-creators): Roblox is social and synchronous and closer to YouTube than the App Store. Slim upfront tutorials. Paper-fun can be a false positive; retest on the real platform. Older audiences notice poor quality, and kid-focused branding repels them. Sources: [GameMakers 2020-03 29:03](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=1743), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/roblox-user-base), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 5:24](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=324), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Answer the maturity questionnaire accurately and in order** (multiple-creators): Base answers on the most mature content players can encounter. Don't add 17+ or Moderate/Restricted content before the label is granted. Any depicted blood is at least light; heavy blood is 17+; implied violence is mild; realistic consequences are moderate+. Inaccurate answers risk removal, 13+ restriction and moderation. Resubmitting removes ads eligibility during review. Unrated experiences are treated as 13+. Kids/Select needs 2FA, ID verification and the full questionnaire. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 16:48](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=1008), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Never use third-party IP without permission** (multiple-creators): Violations can remove the whole experience and repeat reports can end your account. Not monetising, crediting, free downloads or believing fair use do not protect you. AI-modifying someone's work still infringes. Respond within 7 days to IP scanner flags and 10 business days to content standards deviations. No knowingly false notices (DMCA 512(f)). Don't use the Roblox brand in UGC items. Link a verified email. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 2:57](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=177), [Roblox 2025-09 9:37](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=577), [Roblox 2025-09 11:00](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=660), [Roblox 2025-09 14:24](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=864), [Roblox 2025-09 24:40](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=1480). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Do not manipulate impressions, teleports, alts or bots** (multiple-creators): Roblox may deduct or reclaim Robux and suspend games or accounts. Don't encourage alt accounts, fake ad impressions, misclassify metadata, or force or mislead players through portals. Creator Rewards removes the AFK/long-session advantage. Use Enum.VerifiedLevel.Low for everyday features. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox DevForum 2025-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards), [Roblox 2023-09 30:36](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1836). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Protect rewards, referrals and purchases from exploits** (multiple-creators): Reward each invitee once (DataStore flag), add inviter cooldowns, watch for mutual-invite rings. Rate-limit generic systems. Before UGC events rename remotes and place bait coins. Bound pet/egg counts. Heavy-spend products can be spammed and break servers. Use server authority rather than custom cheat detection alone. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2024-11 2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122), [Roblox Creators 2024-11 2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122), [Roblox Creators 2024-11 2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/referral-system), [Roblox 2022-06 35:12](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2112). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Handle purchase APIs, paid access and subscriptions correctly** (multiple-creators): PromptProductPurchaseFinished and PromptCommerceProductPurchaseFinished only mean the prompt closed; use receipts or order events. Don't return Processed early. Check subscription status on the server. Use MarketplaceService for prices (regional pricing). Cross-game sales disabled from May 30, 2026. Paid access shrinks players, has no refunds, bars private servers and copies; broken games may be quarantined. Subscriptions aren't auto-refunded; state benefits; changing private server price cancels subscriptions. Plan monetisation early. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/robux-transfers), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't ship dead, broken or untested features** (multiple-creators): No VIP badges or shop items that do nothing, no 'join event' boards without events. Fix bugs before release; prioritise bugs that make players quit. Don't ship untested updates or wipe data. Disabled badges can't be earned. Save the Item catalog tab. Schedule updates for peak hours. A broken D1 (6-8%) hurt scaling (self-reported). Use Publish As Overwrite for start place changes. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2021-01 8:54](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=534), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 4:03](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=243), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 57:59](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3479), [Roblox Creators 2026-09 34:41](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2081). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Avoid art, level and theme mistakes** (multiple-creators): Avoid free models, unanchored parts, repetitive layouts, and tool visuals that don't match function. Keep level art cohesive with movement and don't spread the world far from gameplay or across too many worlds. Social lobby spaces matter. Don't change default Roblox mechanics heavily. Bright colours can read as danger. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2021-01 4:03](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=243), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-10 1:30](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=90), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-10 1:30](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=90), [SmartyRBX 2024-02 12:54](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=774), [SmartyRBX 2024-02 3:08](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=188). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Thumbnails, metadata and discovery checks; AI and localisation shortcuts** (multiple-creators): Default thumbnails are ineligible for Home recommendations. Avoid ambiguous imagery and misleading metadata. Rejected thumbnails can cause bans. No social URLs or invite codes in-game. Tags alone don't drive Discovery. Ads don't penalise RFY. Games are not permanently quarantined. AI auto-translation overrides manual translations; AI moderation, code and UI need checking; avoid concatenated sentences and idioms. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq), [CallMeDogey 2025-01 5:58](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=358), [CallMeDogey 2026-04 5:29](https://youtu.be/SjjPGduApZU?t=329). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Protect account, ownership, contracts and partners** (multiple-creators): Don't rely on one account (~100,000 Robux of progress lost, self-reported). Don't leave a group that owns your game. Secure accounts and keys. No company partnerships (separate LLCs plus MOU). Get contracts before a sale and sell when stable. Don't form a team before a playable game. Vet clients and editors, cap revisions, don't pay upfront ($70 lost). Track partner deadlines; prefer smaller collab partners. Never email tax forms. Sources: [waveshine 2025-08 1:35](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=95), [Creators on the Blox 2025-12 20:33](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=1233), [GameMakers 2021-01 38:55](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2335), [Roblox DevForum 2017-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 4:34](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=274). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Mind economy design: sinks, pacing, events** (multiple-creators): Plan sinks before big drops, estimate event economic impact (Gold surplus), pace content so it isn't consumed day one, don't assume full-season play, don't round intermediate balance values. Avoid rebirths that trivialise progress and scarce or effort-heavy rewards. Avoid exclusive units in hyper-casual games. UGC events bring collectors who don't spend. Avoid quests inviting join-leave griefing. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 11:51](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=711), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:10:16](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4216), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:01:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3671), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:04:48](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3888). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Handle feedback, safety and prediction carefully** (multiple-creators): Don't ban critics; all feedback can be applied. Explain unpopular changes. Moderate player-hosted parties; suggest chat rephrases rather than blocking. Avoid flashing red lights. Don't play sounds or particles inside bind-to-simulation (use task.defer); keep predicted instances small (~10,000 lagged); stress-test extremes; keep places under 100 MB. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 59:15](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=3555), [Creators on the Blox 2026-06 55:46](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=3346), [Creators on the Blox 2026-06 55:46](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=3346), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 37:51](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2271), [Daiorg 2025-08 12:03](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=723). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **UGC, commerce and ad-admin pitfalls** (one-creator-repeated): Set uploads to Restricted before selling; group assets can't be sold; sales are USD only; no obfuscated code or remote require; research demand before uploading UGC. Shopify price changes take bundles off sale; deleting imported items deletes products; refunds don't revoke developer products. Ad campaigns can be Inactive, Rejected or Pending; no PII in ads; don't set alert duration to 1; dashed crash charts mean low sample. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2026-04 0:41](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=41), [Roblox Creators 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=0), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Remember only nil and false are falsy in Luau** (multiple-creators): 0, empty strings and empty tables are truthy, so 'if count then' runs for zero. Compare explicitly (count > 0, value ~= nil) when zero or empty must fail. Also check and/or fallbacks, since 0 or '' will not fall through. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/expressions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/expressions). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use pairs for sparse tables; ipairs stops at the first nil** (multiple-creators): ipairs halts at the first nil gap, so entries after it are skipped. pairs visits every key but its order is not guaranteed. Use an array with ipairs when order matters (leaderboards); use pairs for tables with gaps. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/pairs-and-ipairs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Do not force garbage collection; free memory by releasing references and destroying instances** (multiple-creators): collectgarbage('collect') errors in Roblox and GC timing is undocumented. Clearing a variable or Parent to nil does not free an Instance while another strong reference exists. Clear all references and call Instance:Destroy(). Disconnect event connections too: they are held by the engine, so Lua GC will not free instances they reference. Numbers and strings are not reliable leak culprits. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/nil). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use task-based scheduling instead of spawn()** (one-creator-repeated): spawn() has reported delays up to 15 seconds. Prefer task.spawn, task.delay or coroutines. A FastSpawn BindableEvent trick runs in the main thread's error scope, so an error inside its body can affect the main thread. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683), [Roblox DevForum 2020-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683). Newest source 2020-04-25.
- **Follow BindableFunction rules exactly** (one-creator-repeated): With no OnInvoke set, the invoking script yields forever. Only the last OnInvoke assigned runs. Omitting return gives nil. Pass pure arrays or pure dictionaries with no nil values; non-string keys become strings; tables are copied (identity checks fail) and metatables are lost. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Guard Touched handlers against silent misfires and repeats** (one-creator-repeated): Touched and TouchEnded fire only from physical movement, not when you set Position or CFrame into an overlap. Models cannot connect Touched directly; loop over child BaseParts. Use a boolean or attribute debounce so repeated touches do not restart timers or fade loops. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-a-health-pickup), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify gameplay results on the server and filter player text** (multiple-creators): Malicious clients can fake hits or remove walls unless the server verifies the raycast path. Client-sided anticheat is not worth the time; false positives are inevitable, so design mild punishments. Decide trust per feature; Unknown is not untrusted. Filter all player-visible text via GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync in pcall. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958), [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/rate-limit-public-text-inputs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Guard against streaming in and out** (one-creator-repeated): ChildAdded, ChildRemoved and CollectionService signals also fire on stream in/out, so guard effects with a Spawned attribute. Touched, ProximityPrompt, ClickDetector and DragDetector do not work for players who don't have the part streamed in; use persistent models. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Handle join and leave timing before the player is ready** (multiple-creators): Players can collect coins before PlayerAdded finishes and be reset to zero; freeze the character until setup completes. Never wait for player data inside PlayerRemoving: one wait left a plot system stuck and kicked over 700 players. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards), [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:23](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=863), [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:18](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=858). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Anchor placed map parts and check placement** (multiple-creators): Unanchored parts fall, drift, or cause heavy lag and get fixed late. Anchor on placement and re-check positions after building. Anchoring more than one part in an assembly splits it and deactivates welds between the parts, so check that too. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 4:29](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=269), [janisjanis01 2026-02 11:20](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=680), [janisjanis01 2026-06 5:18](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=318), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Profile before blaming the Humanoid or replacing it** (one-creator-repeated): Analyze your code before blaming the default Humanoid for poor performance. The default is refined; only build a custom one if you won't need most of its features. Do not use Humanoid.Running as WalkSpeed: it reports HumanoidRootPart velocity including vertical momentum, so falling triggers false positives. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801), [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801), [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298). Newest source 2022-01-25.
- **Design network efficiency in from the start** (multiple-creators): Entity and remote efficiency is hard to retrofit; designing it early, like security, avoids large rewrites. Remote limits are in kB/s (50 kB/s, not 50 kilobits). Automatic-mode remote parameters reach other clients with a delay of one send interval plus latency. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078), [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Playtest, test exploits and progression before launch** (multiple-creators): Repeat actions (respawning cows, tapping for farming) and check stacking limits; one player stacked hundreds of turrets on one tile. Patches can create new exploits: a punishing spawn was itself farmed. Playtest and balance early, walk progression to the end for impossible points (a wave-76 wall came from a missing config), and test multiplayer before promoting. Sources: [Game Maker's Toolkit 2022-04 0:01](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=1), [Game Maker's Toolkit 2022-04 7:30](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=450), [janisjanis01 2026-08 16:23](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=983), [tef 2026-10 13:25](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=805), [janisjanis01 2026-02 12:07](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=727). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Watch concurrent players for days after updates; verify release hygiene** (multiple-creators): Players bounce when updates launch and CCU can drop sharply (near zero overnight in one case). Watch CCU over several days before judging, check logs, and roll back quickly if an update is game-breaking. After publishing a video, verify it is Public, since Unlisted videos got almost no views. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-06 11:27](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=687), [Duckable 2026-05 12:37](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=757), [janisjanis01 2025-09 13:05](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=785), [janisjanis01 2025-08 7:53](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=473). Newest source 2026-06-06.
- **Stabilise and check ad performance before scaling spend** (multiple-creators): Bugs wasted a 32,000 Robux ad spend and killed retention on earlier games. Check click-through early: about 1.9% CTR means replace the creative, not add spend. Scale only after a stable build. Sources: [Duckable 2026-05 10:09](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=609), [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:23](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=863), [janisjanis01 2026-04 2:42](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=162), [janisjanis01 2026-05 9:19](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=559), [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:18](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=858). Newest source 2026-05-31.
- **Keep Studio collaboration and versions safe** (one-creator-repeated): Disable Team Create before reverting a version, or other editors' auto-saves can overwrite it. Restore does not publish; call publish with the returned draftHash. Don't have several people sync the same script, and Stop Sync before deleting a top-level synced instance. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Do not edit Build games in Studio; expect 16+ default** (one-creator-repeated): Editing a Build game in Studio permanently makes it a standard Studio game and it loses Build status; make changes in the Build chat. Build games default to a 16+ rating; younger audiences need Roblox Select with content review. Refused requests: rephrase to be more original. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Asset permissions are one-way; set them deliberately** (one-creator-repeated): Open Use cannot go back to Restricted; game grants are permanent; removing a friend does not revoke asset permission. A Decal's Image must be Open Use first. Avatar items need dependent textures and meshes Open Use before upload. A restricted asset used in a script needs game permission or it is silent. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check fees, policy and asset IDs before uploading** (one-creator-repeated): Upload fees (80 Robux; 500 for emissive) are not refunded if moderation rejects the item. Free Limited items cost a per-unit fee Roblox keeps: 200 hats at 100 Robux each costs 20,000 Robux. Never use others' AssetIDs or assets pending moderation; Roblox moderates again after upload. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-fees-and-commissions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-fees-and-commissions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/moderation), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-dcc). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Respect avatar cage, SLIM and rig specs** (one-creator-repeated): Keep inner and outer cages distinct and non-overlapping, with no missing limbs; keep clothing inside the outer cage, not the inner; do not delete cage vertices or alter UVs. Keep modesty layers over sensitive regions. SLIM excludes Humanoid-containing, zero-scale or animated models, R6 and NPCs. Publish animations via the Animation Editor; makeup needs R15 MeshPart heads. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use data stores for persistence; respect data limits and API errors** (one-creator-repeated): Memory stores have a 45-day maximum lifetime, so use data stores for longer persistence; use configs for frequent single-key reads. Separate data store instances keep their own caches. Unsupported types may save as nil; preview with HttpService:JSONEncode. Parse v1 and v2 error shapes and wrap calls in pcall. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/save-player-data). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set pathfinding costs and limits** (one-creator-repeated): Default shortest paths route through swamp water if geometrically shorter; set material costs. Keep requests under 3,000 studs straight-line; large mazes above 20,000 nodes can fail. Set AgentHeight and AgentCanJump to match the agent, or computations fail when a jump is required. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Do not yield in chat callbacks; use native chat correctly and avoid parallel assumptions** (one-creator-repeated): Don't yield inside TextChatService callbacks; yielding blocks chat. Avoid duplicate TextChannel names. With platform-integrated chat, custom chat must use native systems. Members without a stated thread safety level are Unsafe for parallel access. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep educational and child-facing content within preset limits** (one-creator-repeated): Presets cannot simulate free-form chat, back-and-forth dialogue, question/answer structures or alphabet wheels; avoid slang, real-world contact and emotional pressure. Avoid direct violence in classroom games (use cartoonish conflict). Open-ended Quick Words get rejected. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/quick-words). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Read the exact error line; catch beginner syntax traps first** (multiple-creators): Most beginner errors are capitalization and typos; a single missing word cost hours. Define functions before use, don't quote variable names, keep keywords lowercase, put loop code between while...do and end, and add wait() in loops to avoid hangs. Arithmetic on non-numeric strings errors. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618), [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618), [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/intro-to-scripting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep code readable, centralised and not copy-pasted** (multiple-creators): Avoid copy-pasting repeated blocks per mode, and centralise logic instead of copying scripts into each part. Model AI behaviour as explicit states and transitions. Comment why, not what. Avoid recursive require chains. Use modules instead of stuffing everything into _G. Sources: [Duckable 2026-05 3:38](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=218), [B Ricey 2021-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/Vi2FckNJv9w?t=0), [B Ricey 2023-04 0:43](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=43), [B Ricey 2022-09 16:33](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=993), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use CFrame correctly and disable seating before teleporting** (multiple-creators): Vectors encode orientation only; position is CFrame.p alone. Avoid the CFrameValue plus SetPrimaryPartCFrame legacy pattern, which is fragile. Disable seating before teleporting, or a CFrame change while seated fails. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2015-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743), [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/supporting-systems). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set realistic expectations and build what players want** (multiple-creators): Do not assume your first game succeeds; one took 3 months and lost money as a learning experience. A 5-day build rarely goes viral. You do not need 10,000 active players to earn around $1,000. Build what players already want, not what you think is cool; pretty games with low player counts don't keep players. Sources: [Yann 2026-05 0:41](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=41), [janisjanis01 2023-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=0), [bubDEV 2025-08 6:51](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=411), [rasdev 2026-07 3:24](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=204), [rasdev 2026-07 3:24](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=204). Newest source 2026-07-20.
- **Do not rely on free models, tutorial copy-paste or AI one-shots as a shortcut** (multiple-creators): Free models may contain malicious scripts; review code first. Copy-pasted tutorials and free models you don't understand stall learning and leave no sense of progress. AI tools may return pre-made marketplace templates, paid trials pressure you, and one-prompt games look unpolished. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838), [Duckable 2026-05 5:51](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=351), [Duckable 2026-05 2:57](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=177), [Duckable 2026-09 14:12](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=852), [Duckable 2026-09 11:30](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=690). Newest source 2026-10-24.
- **Verify uploads are Public and audio levels after publishing** (one-creator-repeated): Two videos left Unlisted got almost no views; verify visibility right after publishing. Check audio levels before publishing. Don't rely on joke banners to drive subscribes (5 subs from 6,000 views) or on a single sure-hit idea. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-08 7:53](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=473), [janisjanis01 2025-08 2:24](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=144), [janisjanis01 2025-05 6:21](https://youtu.be/sM9btPAvb28?t=381), [janisjanis01 2025-08 9:47](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=587). Newest source 2025-08-24.
- **Design anchor features before monetisation** (single-mention): Spend early time on the core mechanics that define the game, not purchase systems. Monetisation designed after the core loop is fun avoids bolting offers onto an unproven game. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838). Newest source 2017-11-29.
- **Write team roles, royalty splits and exit terms before work starts** (multiple-creators): Cutting royalties later causes disputes, so fix compensation up front. Don't hire a friend to avoid conflict or keep someone falling behind, since it damages the team. Expect early scams and avoid percentage-only deals from unproven teams, many of which never release. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475), [VuukStudios 2023-12 7:35](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=455), [VuukStudios 2024-07 3:06](https://youtu.be/NfXvHlAdIsI?t=186), [VuukStudios 2023-12 10:49](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=649). Newest source 2024-07-23.
- **Decide discovery and reach before building; don't rely on one channel or server** (multiple-creators): Build, upload and then wonder why nobody plays is backwards. Settle discovery channels (search, thumbnails, promo, community) before opening Studio. A secondary YouTube channel gave no players within two days and a small account's meme posts got about 100 views, so measure any single channel and widen reach, e.g. several hiring servers. Sources: [Matt The Method 2026-08 2:37](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=157), [Matt The Method 2026-08 2:37](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=157), [Raul 2026-06 2:30](https://youtu.be/dAL8ohgzN28?t=150), [Erthlord 2026-01 14:45](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=885), [VuukStudios 2026-02 5:43](https://youtu.be/JQqhUWZUGDQ?t=343). Newest source 2026-08-26.
- **Expect many launches before traction; don't read a peak as success** (multiple-creators): One creator released about 10 games before reaching 2–3 CCU. Another's 5K CCU game fell to about 500 once hype ended, and featuring alone did not help small games. Judge the game on retention after the spike, not on peak CCU. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-09 9:09](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=549), [VuukStudios 2026-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=688), [anomalousDucky 2020-01 2:44](https://youtu.be/R5LnkhhJtno?t=164), [ArTheDev 2026-06 1:42](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=102). Newest source 2026-09-13.
- **Hold a quality bar and polish the feel of the core mechanic** (multiple-creators): Don't ship a game you are not proud of; one creator held back a bland game and lost a day. Very fast finishes with little content can signal thin gameplay. Earlier janky mechanics came from unpolished feel, so tune movement, lighting and visuals before release. Sources: [Raul 2026-07 3:05](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=185), [Raul 2026-07 5:56](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=356), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865). Newest source 2026-07-29.
- **Remove dead-end UI and never kick on death** (multiple-creators): Don't ship windows with no content, vanity info or credits buttons that do nothing, or 'coming soon' settings; add buttons only when their content exists. Never kick players on death ('please restart'); it forces a full rejoin and cuts replayability. Reset the player in-session instead. Sources: [GnomeCode 2021-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=0), [GnomeCode 2021-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=41), [GnomeCode 2021-06 9:11](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=551). Newest source 2021-06-14.
- **Gate score by alive state and make time rewards persist** (one-creator-repeated): A score that keeps counting while dead undermines the game; gate accrual with a per-player alive flag. Default team scoring for round games ties points to current players and drops them on disconnect, so don't rely on it. Time-reward progress resets to 0 each session by default, so persist it if rewards should accumulate. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Never gate core gameplay, movement or camera behind purchases or age checks** (multiple-creators): ByteBlox says gating basic movement or camera behind purchases breaks Roblox ToS and risks suspension. AgeChecked cannot be completed on console or VR, so gate only optional rewards or zones, never core gameplay. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-11 16:06](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=966), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091). Newest source 2026-04-30.
- **Keep policy-safe rules and reward wording; treat scams as scams** (multiple-creators): Experience rules must follow Community Standards and apply evenly; excluding users by protected traits violates policy. Write an explicit no-ableism rule. Feeds must not imply rewards for ongoing viewing. Roblox staff never ask for passwords, no legitimate source gives free Robux, and instant Robux-to-cash sites are scams, so report them. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-ban-api-and-alt-account-detection/3039740), [Roblox DevForum 2021-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919), [Roblox DevForum 2021-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't rely on client-side or name-based anti-exploit checks** (multiple-creators): FilteringEnabled only stops unvalidated replication. Exploiters still read client code, can delete client-only checks, and window-name detection fails because exploits randomise names. Validate on the server, avoid unfair server exploits such as remote crashes, and design abuse to yield no advantage. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594), [Roblox DevForum 2020-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594), [Roblox DevForum 2020-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594), [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/defensive-design). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Under rollback, don't trust Touched or cached AnimationTracks; don't publish Server Authority yet** (multiple-creators): Rollbacks can fire BasePart.Touched/TouchEnded and InputAction.StateChanged inaccurately; use GetTouchingParts() and InputAction:GetState(). Under server authority, don't cache AnimationTrack objects; store IDs and fetch via GetTrackByAnimationId. Server Authority games don't work for player clients yet, so don't publish them. Expect mispredictions and keep corrections small. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority/techniques), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Migrate deprecated APIs before cutoffs; don't assume new systems backport** (multiple-creators): CameraAction stops synchronizing; migrate to Player:GetCameraState(). Migrate FilterDescendantsInstances before it triggers analysis warnings. The legacy PD opt-out flips to Enabled in Phase 2 (H2 2026) and Phase 3 removes the property. The new avatar controller targets only new experiences, and joint upgrades apply only to players spawned via HumanoidDescription flows, not existing NPCs. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575), [Roblox DevForum 2026-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561), [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211), [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904). Newest source 2026-07-09.
- **Plan the platform-integrated chat migration before May 2027** (one-creator-repeated): Platform-integrated chat becomes required for all games from May 2027, and unsupported chat APIs will be deprecated. Prefixes aren't supported yet, so don't enable it if your game needs them. Default chat with no customisation needs no action; signs, boards and comment systems are unaffected. The property can only change in Studio. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **After restores, publish and verify; click Stop; use Limited playtesters and finish transfers** (multiple-creators): Restoring a place does not publish it: open, publish, then restart servers. Verify the correct build, since a bugged release caused a sharp player drop. Click Stop after every playtest or edits are lost. Use Limited > Playtesters, not Private, for testers. Finish or cancel transfers before editing; re-add payment details. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/version-history), [Seibo 2026-10 3:30](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=210), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/playtest-the-map), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't model revenue on myths: 1 Robux per visit, paid UGC ads, bloated shops or AFK farming** (multiple-creators): The 1-Robux-per-visit claim is false; one game earned far more per visit from purchases. 200 Robux per item across 17 paid UGC items produced no measurable lift next day. Cut shop items players won't buy. Avoid AFK-farm or pure gambling designs chasing Premium payouts, which are risky for player quality. Sources: [Perstile 2025-05 0:40](https://youtu.be/ET3fRqaFmFc?t=40), [Perstile 2025-05 0:40](https://youtu.be/ET3fRqaFmFc?t=40), [Erthlord 2026-07 9:32](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=572), [Erthlord 2026-07 9:32](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=572), [1Coal 2024-03 3:36](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=216). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set NPC follow exits, unanchor parts and clear player-specific hooks** (multiple-creators): Unanchor every BasePart in an NPC model or it cannot walk. Delete the setup emote chat hook in a copied Animate script, since it is player-specific and errors on NPCs. Give a follow state a timeout or distance exit, and clear the follow flag on attack. The library assumes R15 and is not fully supported for NPCs or R6. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779), [SimTek Game Development 2023-01 3:12](https://youtu.be/tjwI4b2ZRZk?t=192), [SimTek Game Development 2025-06 22:03](https://youtu.be/4tGM1Ac5fcI?t=1323), [SimTek Game Development 2025-07 55:45](https://youtu.be/DcLba0yVLHs?t=3345), [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904). Newest source 2026-02-18.
- **Parent scripts, actors and client code to valid containers** (multiple-creators): Actors must be parented to an object; workers under StarterCharacterScripts or the character never run. Don't put scripts directly under StarterGui; wrap LocalScripts in a ScreenGui. Scripts with RunContext Client in Starter containers duplicate. Default RunContext Legacy runs server-side only in server containers. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566), [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/locations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/locations). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Make trigger parts non-colliding and buffer fast touches** (multiple-creators): Triggers should have CanCollide off so they don't block players. HumanoidRootPart Touched can miss by a small gap, so add an invisible, non-collidable buffer part slightly larger than the wall. A bridge solid at start may have parts touching terrain that fire Touched early; anchor and separate them. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-06 18:33](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=1113), [SimTek Game Development 2024-11 13:22](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=802), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Iterate backward when removing and respect table semantics** (multiple-creators): Never table.remove inside an ipairs loop; iterate backward. Table keys match by reference, so store the table in a variable and reuse it. The # operator counts only the array part, so use a pairs counter for dictionaries. Avoid table.insert/remove at arbitrary positions in large tables, since shifting is costly. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/make-changes-to-arrays), [Roblox DevForum 2019-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930), [Roblox DevForum 2019-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930), [Roblox DevForum 2019-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check coordinate origins for each drawing API** (multiple-creators): CanvasDraw pixels run from (1,1) to (Width,Height), and out-of-range access causes buffer out-of-bounds errors. EditableImage starts at (0,0), so the bottom-right pixel is (width-1,height-1). Pass whole-number pixel coordinates; RGBA values range 0 to 1. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633), [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633), [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566). Newest source 2025-08-03.
- **Catch every Promise chain and yield in every infinite loop** (multiple-creators): Attach a catch to every Promise chain; the library warns in the console when a chain errors without one. A while true loop with no yield exhausts execution time and hangs or crashes Studio, so include task.wait() in each. Games cannot run true infinity; simulate its perception, e.g. an endless hallway. Loop only in the start stage, not init stages. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825), [ByteBlox 2024-06 1:22](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=82), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/control-structures), [Suphi Kaner 2024-11 39:31](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=2371), [ByteBlox 2024-06 1:22](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=82). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Clean up effects, connections and clones on respawn** (multiple-creators): Effects added on each respawn without cleanup cause steady lag and eventual crashes; delete old effects on reset. Connect Activated on every clone, parent clones to the right container, and disconnect or destroy instances you no longer need. Check the Unparented Instances view for leaked instances. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-05 3:09](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=189), [ByteBlox 2024-06 13:20](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=800), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/memory-usage). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Name attachments uniquely and set PrimaryPart to a hitbox** (one-creator-repeated): Attachment names must match exactly between Handle and body part, and must not be duplicated. Roblox overrides your welds on a real Accessory, so wrap custom-controlled parts in a Model instead. Set PrimaryPart to a transparent hitbox so collision and placement checks work. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207), [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207), [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753). Newest source 2020-08-27.
- **Use session-locked, UpdateAsync-based persistence in production** (multiple-creators): A simple data script lacks session locking and retries; use a library like ProfileStore for production to avoid data loss. Use UpdateAsync when you read and modify the previous value, though it is not inherently more secure than SetAsync. Avoid careless SetAsync on shared keys across many servers. Per-partition limits are estimates, so don't design around fixed numbers. Sources: [PineappleDev 2024-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/BOMfj0bSjf0?t=0), [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983), [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 5:05](https://youtu.be/ARPfIt16sXI?t=305), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check library and tutorial age before copying into production** (multiple-creators): roblox-js was superseded after logins required cookies; noblox.js was suggested. Yucon is memory-hungry and buggier. Old tutorial scripts are outdated and unpolished. Replica renamed members (Maid:Add, SetParent after construction). Demo modules such as a MessagingService demo are untested with multiple servers. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2017-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201), [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yucon-framework-optimization-organization-and-high-level-security/630895), [Roblox DevForum 2023-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/movement-system/2135683), [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142), [Roblox DevForum 2024-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replica-server-to-client-state-replication-module/3216980). Newest source 2024-10-16.
- **Verify every audio licence and expect sounds not to carry across places** (multiple-creators): 'Royalty free' means no per-use fee, not free, so check the actual licence. CC-NC tracks are blocked for any game with paid content. Stolen user-uploaded MP3s risk DMCA takedowns. Third-party audio stops working after privacy changes unless under 6 seconds or from Roblox/partners. Sounds and animations don't carry over from uncopylocked places. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490), [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490), [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697). Newest source 2024-03-18.
- **Keep hats rigid, install MaterialVariants and avoid capitalised hand names** (multiple-creators): Set AutoSkin to Disabled for hats, hair and glasses; skinning introduces unwanted deformation. Install MaterialVariants, or meshes referencing them render gray. Avoid capitalised hand-part names like RightHand; renaming to rightHand/leftHand fixed texturing issues. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check HttpService limits and flush memory stores with care** (one-creator-repeated): HttpService allows only x-api-key and content-type headers, HTTPS only, and blocks ports below 1024 except 80 and 443, plus 1194. Paths containing '..' are inaccessible. Flushing a memory store clears queues, sorted maps and hash maps, including live matchmaking data, so flush only if the game can run without it. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use Scribe correctly: no assignment, no ServerOnly+Shared, deploy together** (one-creator-repeated): Writing Scribe values with assignment (data.Coins = 5) is a type error that only shadows the accessor. Combining Scribe.ServerOnly and Scribe.Shared on one field is a startup error. Mixed-version deploys break protocol and schema changes, so deploy server and client together. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905), [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905), [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905). Newest source 2026-07-15.
- **Keep camera and first-person controls safe for non-humanoid and out-of-range cases** (multiple-creators): Setting CameraSubject to a non-humanoid part breaks shift-lock rotation; rotate the root toward the camera's XZ LookVector when MouseBehavior is LockCenter. In first person, block sprinting backwards or sideways via a MoveDirection vs LookVector dot product, and reset FOV and speed when out of range. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996), [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943). Newest source 2021-07-06.
- **Fix quality and economy bugs before paid promotion or sponsorship** (multiple-creators): Sponsoring or advertising an unpolished game wastes spend. Poke saw player count drop to zero once the ad ended. ThePurpleGuy's beta bugs cost six dislikes and forced a pause and relaunch. LovJak found a selling price equal to purchase price, which leaves no profit. Check retention and economy first. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 0:24](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=24), [Poke 2025-12 2:44](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=164), [ThePurpleGuy 2026-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/VEkuM5ZG6G4?t=0), [LovJak 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=0), [PolarBear 2024-03 4:26](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=266). Newest source 2026-07-09.
- **Enforce community rules; your community's actions reflect on you** (one-creator-repeated): Spam or misconduct done in your community's name is blamed on the owner even when it breaks your rules and you do not condone it. Moderate actively. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2019-09 6:17](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=377), [Last Level Studios 2019-09 6:17](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=377). Newest source 2019-09-06.
- **Don't fragment or paywall event progression** (one-creator-repeated): Last Level's 2020 critique of ThatGuyEvan's hunt: splitting it across 48 games with no hub and paid passes for easier eggs drew heavy criticism of Roblox and developers. Keep event progression unified and free. Sources: [ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 11:52](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=712), [ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 11:52](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=712). Newest source 2020-04-21.
- **Don't gate the best ending behind repeated content** (single-mention): ThatGuyEvan's 2019 hunt forced a second boss fight to reach the good ending, and players disliked it. Reward the best outcome without making players repeat content. Sources: [ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 10:45](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=645). Newest source 2020-04-21.
- **Give events enough original content** (single-mention): The 2015 hunt used only three developer-made minigames for 22 eggs and is widely seen as the worst. Thin content hurts event reception; budget minigames per egg or objective. Sources: [ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 5:16](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=316). Newest source 2020-04-21.
- **Design the game before building tech** (one-creator-repeated): Windlight Bay had lots of tech (NPCs, building, dialogue) but weak game design, so it did not find players. Dreams of systems don't show how they combine into a game. Write the core loop first. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2022-01 24:10](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1450), [Last Level Studios 2022-01 24:10](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1450). Newest source 2022-01-07.
- **Verify AI-built or copied systems actually connect** (one-creator-repeated): RoyalDev's AI-built flashlight and enemy AI worked separately but did not connect, so the game 'seemed done' but was not. Test integration end to end, and avoid hasty scripts and placeholder UI. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=205), [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=205), [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=205). Newest source 2026-08-29.
- **Understand a script before shipping and measure its frame cost** (one-creator-repeated): Last Level's untested camera bob script tanked frame rate. Read and understand any API or Toolbox script before use, and test performance impact rather than trusting it. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2019-10 28:45](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1725), [Last Level Studios 2019-10 30:48](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1848). Newest source 2019-10-11.
- **Don't over-simulate; ask what the object needs to do** (one-creator-repeated): Roox4 spent effort simulating a car system when the real need was enemies, which needed only one raycast in the middle. Model only the behaviour the player actually sees. Sources: [Roox4 2025-09 9:34](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=574), [Roox4 2025-09 9:34](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=574), [Roox4 2025-09 8:53](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=533). Newest source 2025-09-01.
- **Explain obvious mechanics to new players** (one-creator-repeated): Creators underestimate how much new players need explained. RoyalDev's whiteboard instruction, which they thought obvious, confused players. Test with a fresh player who has no briefing. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 5:28](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=328), [RoyalDev 2026-08 13:36](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=816). Newest source 2026-08-07.
- **Diagnose execution and friction before scrapping the concept** (one-creator-repeated): A dead game may be an execution problem (polish, friction) rather than a bad idea. A good idea can fail if poorly executed. Ads deliver the right players, but a weak game won't keep them. Audit friction first. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 6:09](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=369), [RoyalDev 2026-08 4:50](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=290), [RoyalDev 2026-08 4:50](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=290), [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:03](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=123). Newest source 2026-08-18.
- **Ignore popular 'secret' ad setting recipes** (single-mention): RoyalDev says specific budgets, start days and days of week for ads are unproven.
