Test your own ad settings with small spend and read results against retention. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=40). Newest source 2026-08-15.
- **Validate a niche with small tests before scaling** (single-mention): A niche that trends is not guaranteed to work. RoyalDev's Locus-in-backrooms and word-game experiments underperformed. Run small tests before committing production or spend. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-09 0:01](https://youtu.be/nLuxSFg5viw?t=1). Newest source 2026-09-18.
- **Don't rely on nostalgia for a re-release; modernise systems** (one-creator-repeated): Players judge a re-release against current games. Cumin says update matchmaking and progression, and do not hand-wave changes that should have been made with 'it's just like how you remember it'. Sources: [Cumin 2026-09 0:40](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=40), [Cumin 2026-09 9:04](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=544). Newest source 2026-09-13.
- **Ship a presentable release with no dead shop buttons** (one-creator-repeated): Non-functional monetisation makes an alpha look abandoned. First impressions decide whether players stay. A boring, unfinished game with no content is quickly abandoned. Hide or fix every dead button before release. Sources: [Cumin 2026-02 13:17](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=797), [Cumin 2026-02 13:17](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=797), [Cumin 2026-02 13:17](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=797). Newest source 2026-02-07.
- **Avoid repeated mission templates and identical maps** (one-creator-repeated): Cumin's game had four maps that all looked the same with similar objectives, and a reviewer finished all four in one session. Vary objectives and visuals per map and per mission template. Sources: [Cumin 2026-02 5:21](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=321), [Cumin 2026-02 5:21](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=321). Newest source 2026-02-07.
- **Gate admin and cheat-like tools carefully** (one-creator-repeated): CORSO found a large share of players quit right after gaining admin commands, which can destroy the core experience. Gate them, and explain why to players. Sources: [CORSO 2024-09 10:33](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=633), [CORSO 2024-09 10:33](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=633). Newest source 2024-09-05.
- **Balance rarity tiers to similar strength** (single-mention): Making top-tier units make lower tiers useless harms strategy depth. CORSO cites BTD6's 23 towers balanced to similar strength as the model. Sources: [CORSO 2023-10 1:40](https://youtu.be/H9MoA94zKdc?t=100). Newest source 2023-10-07.
- **Make challenge fair and readable** (single-mention): Difficulty is not the same as cheap, unfair design. Hard games need fair, readable mechanics and clear failure causes so players know what went wrong. Sources: [Cumin 2025-06 15:33](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=933). Newest source 2025-06-08.
- **Communicate clearly instead of expecting fans to explain the game** (multiple-creators): Cumin says fans will not explain your game through wikis; the problem is poor communication, not complexity. CORSO adds that transparency aligns player expectations with yours. Sources: [Cumin 2025-11 8:58](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=538), [Cumin 2025-11 8:58](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=538), [CORSO 2024-09 1:49](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=109). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Tie rewards to player actions, not passive timers** (single-mention): Lnor found passive currency every 10 seconds hard to understand and easy to exploit with auto clickers. Reward actions instead. Sources: [Lnor Roblox 2026-09 2:56](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=176). Newest source 2026-09-23.
- **Use event-driven detection, not per-second world scans** (single-mention): Lnor's scan of all world objects every second for player detection burned about 30% of server resources. Use event-driven detection or cached lists. Sources: [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Plan infrastructure for scale early** (single-mention): Rover reached about 100k servers and needed 32+ GB RAM, forcing a rewrite and a separate hosted service. Plan hosting and server scale before growth arrives. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-03 15:46](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=946). Newest source 2020-03-01.
- **Sandbox user code so errors cannot crash Studio** (single-mention): Last Level sandboxed user code so errors and infinite loops cannot crash Studio, which let players experiment freely. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-02 17:22](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=1042). Newest source 2020-02-07.
- **Lock scope with sponsors and asset approvals** (single-mention): Mid-project asset rejections forced deleted work and a 15-hour crunch day at Last Level. Agree scope and asset approval rules with sponsors before work starts. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-03 9:21](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=561). Newest source 2020-03-01.
- **Don't pay upfront for a game transfer** (single-mention): LovJak paid half upfront for a game transfer and the seller went silent. Pay only after ownership transfer is confirmed, or use a trusted escrow. Sources: [LovJak 2026-09 0:15](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=15). Newest source 2026-09-12.
- **Don't treat a pending review as a ban** (single-mention): Moderation queues can exceed 5 hours. Xaiyne's review was pending, not banned. Submit early and wait before assuming a problem. Sources: [Xaiyne 2025-08 14:39](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=879), [Xaiyne 2025-08 14:39](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=879). Newest source 2025-08-08.
- **Avoid aggressive FOMO and gotcha mechanics for children** (single-mention): Heavy monetisation aimed at children draws backlash. If targeting young players, avoid aggressive FOMO and gotcha mechanics. Sources: [Cumin 2025-11 8:10](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=490). Newest source 2025-11-05.
- **Don't upload on a brand-new social account's first day** (single-mention): #7Phantom says uploading on day one risks shadowbanning as a bot. Spend the first day engaging (follow, like, favorite) before posting promotional content. Sources: [#7Phantom 2025-07 11:16](https://youtu.be/xjZXsHxKerE?t=676). Newest source 2025-07-18.
- **Keep all parts inside an accessory unanchored** (single-mention): Emancyphur found an anchored part inside an accessory left the character stuck in place. Check every accessory part's Anchored property. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-04 1:43](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=103). Newest source 2021-04-23.
- **Expect copycat accusations even when you credit inspirations** (one-creator-repeated): Clones of top games draw accusations of copying even when the description openly credits the source. The Fish It! example credited Fisch and Fishing Simulator and still faced the criticism. Credit does not replace original design. Sources: [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026). Newest source 2026-00-00.
- **Differentiate kit-built or clone games with original art and animation** (one-creator-repeated): Pre-made clone kits from BuiltByBit and the Creator Store produce asset flips that draw community backlash. Critics cite missing character animations. Replace kit assets with original art and animation before release. Sources: [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026). Newest source 2026-02-18.
- **Track monetization per hour by age cohort, not just DAUs and hours** (one-creator-repeated): Traffic and hours growth can mask falling per-hour monetization. Roblox reported a bookings shortfall tied to declining per-hour monetization, most notably among younger U.S. and Canadian cohorts. Measure monetization per hour and per cohort alongside DAUs. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm), [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm). Newest source 2026-07-30.
- **Don't embed external video or social feeds in-game** (single-mention): Roblox only allows links from an approved list. Embedding external video or social feeds, or sharing off-platform, can get a game pulled. Check the current approved list before adding any external feed. Sources: [eGamers 2026-08](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/). Newest source 2026-08-25.
- **Don't compare bookings-based margins with revenue-based margins** (single-mention): Cost of revenue is deferred over the average paying-user lifetime of 27 months. Bookings-based margins are therefore not comparable to revenue-based margins. Don't read them as equivalent when modelling or benchmarking. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Design out unsafe content before adding detection** (single-mention): In open-ended creation, avatar, and roleplay games, prevent unsafe content through design choices (limits, defaults, restricted inputs) first. Prevention is cheaper than detection and enforcement later. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 4:03](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=243). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Remove flagged content for everyone, not per player** (one-creator-repeated): When content is flagged, remove the instance for all players. Hiding it only for one user leaves other observers seeing the bad content, which re-triggers detection and enforcement. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 6:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=410), [Roblox 2026-10 25:30](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1530). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Treat safety as continuous iteration, not a one-time fix** (single-mention): Expect bad actors on any platform at scale. Bypasses such as coded speech will appear, so keep reviewing and updating filters and rules after launch. Sources: [Roblox 2025-07 29:41](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=1781). Newest source 2025-07-17.
- **Don't assume DevEx Robux cash-out is permanent** (one-creator-repeated): DevEx ends in the US in mid-2027, with other regions ending later. Do not build long-term income plans on DevEx without checking the latest announced end dates for your region. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505), [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **Keep the account in good standing to protect pending earnings** (single-mention): Pending earnings may be held longer or forfeited for Terms of Use violations. Moderation problems can cost money, not just access, so treat compliance as part of revenue protection. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **Don't show inflated or artificial engagement metrics** (single-mention): Do not use in-game like or engagement counters that do not reflect real player engagement. A cited case showed like counts that were not real, which fed takedown speculation and damaged trust. Sources: [AllThingsHow (guide) 2026-08](https://allthings.how/steal-an-egg-roblox-ban-why-it-happened-and-how-it-returned/). Newest source 2026-08-25.
- **Signpost one-shot mission actions clearly** (single-mention): If a mission action is irreversible (for example, wasting a single photo), communicate the one-shot nature before the player acts. Avoid punishments that force a full game restart, since players quit over them. Sources: [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items). Newest source 2026-00-00.
- **Use the cheapest intelligence layer that fits each event** (single-mention): Layers of AI behavior are independent. Let default behavior handle expected events cheaply, and use dynamic or human-like behavior only where the moment needs it. Don't run the highest layer everywhere. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 1:22](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=82). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Don't assume ReserveServerAsync reserves a server** (single-mention): Official docs (2026-10) state ReserveServerAsync() does not actually reserve a server; it only generates a random GUID. Migrate reserved-server flows to HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId, and don't rely on the old reservation behaviour. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers). Newest source 2026-10-30.
- **Don't rush releases at the cost of core fun or stability** (multiple-creators): Rushing features or monthly release pace leaves many bugs and can sabotage the game's fun. Ship a smaller working core instead of many half-working systems. Fewer, polished updates beat frequent rushed ones. Creators cite this as a common cause of failed or weakened games. Sources: [JustDucksey 2026-08 8:12](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=492), [MonarchDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=206), [Duckable 2026-10 24:23](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=1463). Newest source 2026-10-18.
- **Re-test after every fix before pushing** (single-mention): Fixing shipped bugs can introduce new ones. Run a regression pass on the systems touched by each fix, not only the bug itself, before publishing the next version. Sources: [MonarchDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=206). Newest source 2026-08-23.
- **Don't carry over past-studio scope into a funded team** (single-mention): Founders often bring large scope from a previous studio into a newly funded one. Plan demand and market evidence first, and size scope to a realistic case rather than a best-case scenario. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 46:44](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2804). Newest source 2026-10-22.
- **Explain why code works and the design decisions, not just where to paste it** (single-mention): Script descriptions or comments that only say where code goes don't help readers. State why the code works and which design choices it encodes, so others can adapt it instead of copying it blindly. Sources: [Crusherfire 2024-05 13:38](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=818). Newest source 2024-05-22.
- **Check the correct setup function after copy-pasting** (single-mention): When copying setup functions from one script to another, confirm you call the right one. A wrong call can silently hide a prompt with no error, so verify the prompt appears in a test run. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-05 12:02](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=722). Newest source 2025-05-25.
- **Expect hidden scaffolding and pick the simplest working method** (single-mention): Players rarely notice clipping or out-of-bounds scaffolding, and unpolished hidden helper geometry is normal. Don't hunt for a perfect clean solution; choose the simplest method that works and polish only what players can see. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 2:04](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=124). Newest source 2025-01-22.
- **Competition entries must be public, runnable and original** (single-mention): Broken, unplayable or private submissions are eliminated immediately. Pre-made or old projects also get cut. Before submitting, confirm the place is public, loads and plays without errors, and was built for the contest. Sources: [Duckable 2025-10 0:29](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=29), [Duckable 2025-10 0:29](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=29). Newest source 2025-10-17.
- **Don't sell a way around a scarce reward you created** (single-mention): Limiting a desirable reward to one player at a time and then selling a paid bypass feels predatory to players and reads as a flawed design. Keep scarcity fair and avoid monetizing the workaround. Sources: [Cumin 2026-05 9:46](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=586). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Rank features by ROI and low-hanging fruit before big features** (single-mention): Estimate return on investment for each candidate feature and ship cheap, high-return fixes first. Skipping ROI estimates in favour of large features gives competitors an edge. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-08 27:12](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1632). Newest source 2022-08-30.
- **Study top-grossing games critically before prototyping** (one-creator-repeated): Play top-grossing games in your genre with a critical eye before building. Most design problems already have tested solutions. Learn the solution patterns, but don't clone or reskin the games (see existing clone rule). Sources: [GameMakers 2022-08 27:54](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1674). Newest source 2022-08-30.
- **Don't expect cosmetics to create demand on their own** (one-creator-repeated): Cosmetics need a social layer where players care about status and each other. Rolling out cosmetics alone is not enough. In single-player games, cosmetics rarely work in any meaningful way, so don't count on them for revenue there. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-08 55:36](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3336), [GameMakers 2024-08 55:36](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3336). Newest source 2024-08-12.
- **Verify max server player size before launch** (single-mention): Check the server max player setting before launch. One creator's rollout broke because the value was not set to 6. Treat it as a launch checklist item. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Despawn or respawn harvestable resources per server** (single-mention): Resource objects such as trees must despawn or respawn. Otherwise some servers run out of resources and the game feels broken for those players. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:58](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=898). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Check testing server visibility before playtests** (single-mention): Confirm a testing server is not set to public before playtesting. One creator's testing server was accidentally public and exposed unreleased update content. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:10](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=370). Newest source 2026-10-25.
- **Take a fixed fee, not a revenue percentage, for work on someone else's game** (multiple-creators): When building for another's game, take a fixed paid amount rather than a revenue share. Playtest the game thoroughly before agreeing to any deal. Caution: this differs from a royalty split, which is a deliberate partnership choice that should be written down up front. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-03 5:44](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=344), [TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617). Newest source 2026-03-18.
- **Keep titles and intros honest; avoid clickbait and long intros** (single-mention): Titles must deliver what they promise. Avoid random games with no story or goal, long boring intros, and clickbait. Pair this with a thumbnail strategy and respond to viewers. Sources: [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:53](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=293). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Limit each channel to 1–3 games** (single-mention): Focus each channel on one to three games. One creator advises against spreading across 10–20 different games. Sources: [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:53](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=293). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Don't assume a small audience clears kids' game thresholds** (single-mention): For games aimed at young children, a small viewer base will not meet the 16+ engagement requirement needed to clear the trial threshold. Plan engagement for the actual audience age. This claim is low-advice and should be verified against current platform criteria. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 8:04](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=484). Newest source 2026-06-18.
- **Keep many moving platforms off physics constraints** (one-creator-repeated): Physics-constraint-driven movement (e.g. PrismaticConstraint) for many platforms lagged the server. Running physics on the server also made gameplay look like slow motion. Before polishing content, check simulation ownership, measure server cost, and evaluate simpler scripted movement for those parts. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=0), [janisjanis01 2025-06 13:16](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=796). Newest source 2025-06-15.
- **Check the output console early and fix error floods** (single-mention): One single line of code produced 51,000 errors in one game. Watch the console during early testing, not just at launch, and fix the root line before tuning content or running playtests. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=0). Newest source 2024-04-14.
- **Fix player-found exploits and bugs fast** (one-creator-repeated): A luck potion once granted infinite best pets through a duplication bug. Test for duplication during development, and when players find an exploit, patch it quickly and re-test the fix before pushing. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-06 1:02](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=62), [janisjanis01 2024-06 1:02](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=62). Newest source 2024-06-30.
- **Set expected KPIs by market and device before soft launch** (single-mention): Before soft launch, write down expected LTV, retention and crash rate per market and device. Then bad numbers are not a surprise, and the team does not blame user acquisition for a product problem. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 42:24](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2544). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Avoid tools or plugins that players dislike** (single-mention): A plugin that generated the game drew player backlash, and the game died off. Check whether players will accept a tool or generation method before relying on it for the core product. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-10 3:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=189). Newest source 2024-10-27.
- **Don't stack manipulative mechanics on a weak game** (single-mention): Manipulative mechanics stacked on a weak game may keep players in the short term, but the speaker's claim is that players stay and leave hate instead of praise and growth. Fix the core loop first. Sources: [rasdev 2026-04 3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203). Newest source 2026-04-30.
- **Don't hide paid progression or surprise players with monetisation** (one-creator-repeated): Hidden paid progression in a premium game caused a backlash that EA reversed within two days. Do not surprise players or influencers with monetisation changes; announce them clearly before they go live. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 37:59](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2279), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 36:36](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2196). Newest source 2026-07-16.
- **Expect fast, severe community backlash to monetisation or disliked tools** (multiple-creators): Community anger over monetisation surprises or disliked tools spreads quickly and can ruin a game's standing. Treat players as able to organise against you; plan changes and communication to avoid provoking them. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-10 3:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=189), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403). Newest source 2026-07-16.
- **Test data saving before any holiday or major update** (single-mention): A buggy update with broken data saving hurt growth. Test save and load handling thoroughly before releasing a holiday or large update, not after. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-10 6:41](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=401). Newest source 2024-10-27.
- **Keep core gameplay inside the defined play area** (single-mention): Judges flagged gameplay happening outside the intended play area as controversial (GnomeCode, 2020). Define the play bounds explicitly, keep objectives, spawns and core loops inside them, and block or warn players who leave. Treat this as a judging and community expectation from older guidance, not a confirmed current platform rule. Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-06 41:43](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2503). Newest source 2020-06-26.

## Checklist
- [ ] Prototype a small, scoped first game
- [ ] Validate demand and ship early, then iterate
- [ ] Fix the core loop before spending on ads
- [ ] Test on a phone; keep buttons few and off the left movement side
- [ ] Test console and gamepad support at launch
- [ ] Anchor parts and use original assets, not free models
- [ ] Cap pets, eggs and purchase counts server-side
- [ ] Verify purchases via receipts, never prompt-finished events
- [ ] Check subscription status on the server
- [ ] Use MarketplaceService for prices
- [ ] Make rewarded ad rewards deterministic and account-bound
- [ ] Avoid P2W; lower overpriced items after launch
- [ ] Limit monetisation methods
- [ ] Add referral limits and cooldowns
- [ ] Answer the maturity questionnaire on the most extreme content
- [ ] Avoid third-party IP; get licences
- [ ] Set uploads to Restricted before selling
- [ ] Use a custom thumbnail
- [ ] Run experiments for the full planned duration
- [ ] Use GetConfigForPlayerAsync and GetValue correctly
- [ ] Plan sinks and event economy impact
- [ ] Schedule updates at players' peak hours and test them
- [ ] Get contracts for collaborators; avoid company partnerships
- [ ] Link a verified email and secure the account
- [ ] Keep UI consistent with a limited palette, and places under 100 MB
- [ ] Test every Luau if/and/or branch with 0 and empty string inputs; only nil and false are falsy.
- [ ] Use ipairs for ordered arrays and pairs for sparse tables; never rely on pairs order for leaderboards.
- [ ] Clear all references and call Instance:Destroy() on instances you are done with; disconnect connections.
- [ ] Replace spawn() with task.spawn, task.delay or coroutines.
- [ ] Verify every client-reported hit or result on the server before granting rewards or changes.
- [ ] Filter all player-visible text through GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync inside pcall.
- [ ] Freeze the character until data loads; never wait for data inside PlayerRemoving.
- [ ] Anchor each placed map part and re-check positions and orientations after building.
- [ ] Run a two-client test and a server restart test before promoting.
- [ ] Repeat farming and stacking actions many times to check for infinite-resource and stacking exploits before launch.
- [ ] Walk the full progression to the final wave or level to find impossible points.
- [ ] Add invisible boundary walls at launch so players cannot fall through the map.
- [ ] Set team spawns explicitly so teammates do not spawn together by default.
- [ ] Preview Avatar Settings defaults before publishing a live game.
- [ ] Watch CCU over several days after each update; roll back quickly if an update is game-breaking.
- [ ] Fix game-breaking bugs and check ad click-through before scaling ad spend.
- [ ] Disable Team Create before reverting a place version.
- [ ] Do not edit a Build game in Studio; make changes in the Build chat.
- [ ] Check Open Use and game permissions before setting assets, since Open Use is one-way.
- [ ] Check upload fees, moderation guidelines and asset IDs before paying to upload.
- [ ] Check avatar cages against validation rules: distinct cages, clothing inside outer cage only.
- [ ] Set memory store expiry expectations (45 days max) and use data stores for long-term persistence.
- [ ] Parse both error shapes from Open Cloud and wrap calls in pcall.
- [ ] Set pathfinding material costs and keep requests under 3,000 studs straight-line.
- [ ] Verify uploads are set to Public and audio levels are normal after publishing.
- [ ] Before building, write down who will find the game and through which channels (T13738, Q1420).
- [ ] Put roles, royalty splits and exit terms in writing before any collaborator starts (T10743, T10744).
- [ ] Ship when the core loop is fun and content is sufficient, not on a deadline alone (T13647, T13605).
- [ ] Judge a launch on retention after the spike, not peak CCU (T13674).
- [ ] Remove vanity buttons and empty windows; reset players in-session instead of kicking on death (T11011, T11016).
- [ ] Gate score accrual by a per-player alive flag; persist time-reward progress if it must carry over (T14400, T14582).
- [ ] Keep movement and camera free of purchases; gate only optional content behind age checks (T12472, T13336).
- [ ] Audit reward wording in feeds for implied viewing rewards (T13734).
- [ ] Verify every audio and model licence; 'royalty free' is not a licence (T11574, T11575).
- [ ] After a restore, publish and restart servers; verify the live build (T15355, T13901).
- [ ] Click Stop at the end of every playtest; use Limited > Playtesters rather than Private (T15332, T13383).
- [ ] Migrate CameraAction to GetCameraState and FilterDescendantsInstances before deprecation (T13566, T13277).
- [ ] Plan the platform-integrated chat migration before May 2027 (T14033).
- [ ] Use GetTouchingParts and InputAction:GetState under rollback; don't publish Server Authority yet (T13088, T13087).
- [ ] Unanchor every NPC BasePart and remove player-specific Animate hooks (T11414, T11548).
- [ ] Parent actors and workers to valid containers; wrap LocalScripts in a ScreenGui (T12923, T11923).
- [ ] Iterate backward when removing; use a pairs counter for dictionary sizes (T14569, T10590).
- [ ] Check coordinate origins: CanvasDraw starts at (1,1), EditableImage at (0,0) (T11276, T12924).
- [ ] Attach a catch to every Promise chain and put task.wait() in every infinite loop (T10558, T14668).
- [ ] Delete old effects and clone connections on respawn (T12111, T12200).
- [ ] Set PrimaryPart to a transparent hitbox and name attachments uniquely (T10748, T10778).
- [ ] Use a session-locked data library such as ProfileStore in production (T12157).
- [ ] Use a ~300s cooldown in production and ~5s only for testing (T12396, N4698).
- [ ] Don't assign Scribe values with '='; deploy server and client together (T13589, T13590).
- [ ] Enable Game Settings > Security > Studio Access to API Services, or Studio cannot access DataStores and saving fails (Amora).
- [ ] Name the leaderstats folder exactly 'leaderstats' in lowercase, or the leaderboard will not display it (Amora).
- [ ] Check that the respawn or reset popup does not reappear after each death, since a bug here can make the game unplayable (Mulch).
- [ ] Check for Z-fighting on floors and NPC sitting animation glitches before release (LovJak).
- [ ] Fusion: check for stray or extra commas and make sure the component function returns the instance (Possible Panda).
- [ ] Check the economy: selling price must be higher than purchase price, or there is no profit (LovJak).
- [ ] Replace or hide every shop button that does nothing before release (Cumin).
- [ ] Keep every accessory part unanchored, or the character gets stuck (Emancyphur).
- [ ] Submit moderation reviews early; queues can exceed 5 hours and pending is not a ban (Xaiyne).
- [ ] Pay for a game transfer only after ownership is confirmed, or use escrow (LovJak).
- [ ] Replace per-second world scans with event-driven detection (Lnor).
- [ ] Playtest with a fresh player who has no briefing and add explanations for obvious mechanics (RoyalDev).
- [ ] Ask what each object needs to do before modelling systems; prefer one raycast where it suffices (Roox4).
- [ ] Confirm the game is polished and retains players before sponsorship or paid ads (PolarBear, Poke).
- [ ] Check that each map and mission template has distinct objectives and visuals (Cumin).
- [ ] Sandbox user code so errors and infinite loops cannot crash Studio (Last Level Studios).
- [ ] Plan hosting and server scale before growth; Rover needed 32+ GB RAM at ~100k servers (Last Level Studios).
- [ ] Agree scope and asset approval rules with sponsors before work starts (Last Level Studios).
- [ ] Keep event progression in one hub, free, and avoid splitting it across many games (ThatGuyEvan).
- [ ] Give every event enough original content; do not reuse a few minigames across many eggs (ThatGuyEvan).
- [ ] Gate admin commands and tell players why; CORSO saw many quits after admin tools arrived (CORSO).
- [ ] Tie currency and rewards to player actions, not passive timers that auto clickers can exploit (Lnor).
- [ ] Explain the game with clear in-game communication rather than relying on fan wikis (Cumin, CORSO).
- [ ] Test any script or Toolbox asset for frame-rate impact before adding it (Last Level Studios).
- [ ] Do not upload promotional content on the first day of a new social account (#7Phantom).
- [ ] Before publishing a concept close to a top game, change the core mechanic and replace borrowed art, not just the name.
- [ ] Credit inspirations in the description, but don't rely on credit to prevent copycat accusations.
- [ ] If using a clone or asset kit, replace its assets with original art and character animations before release.
- [ ] Audit any external video, social or link embeds against Roblox's current approved list before adding them.
- [ ] Report monetization per hour, split by age cohort, next to DAUs and hours.
- [ ] Label any bookings-based figure as bookings, and don't compare it directly to revenue-based margins.
- [ ] Before planning income from DevEx, check the latest announced end date for your region (US: mid-2027).
- [ ] Keep your account in good standing; Terms of Use violations can hold or forfeit pending earnings.
- [ ] Before shipping open-ended creation, avatar, or roleplay features, list the unsafe-content paths and design them out first.
- [ ] When moderation flags an instance, remove it for all players, not just the one who reported or triggered it.
- [ ] Schedule a recurring review of safety filters against new bypasses such as coded speech.
- [ ] Do not display like or engagement counters that do not reflect real player engagement.
- [ ] For any one-shot or irreversible mission action, show the consequence before the player commits.
- [ ] Avoid forced full-restart punishments for a single mistake in missions or quests.
- [ ] Default NPC and AI behavior to cheap rules; escalate to dynamic behavior only for events that need it.
- [ ] Search the codebase for ReserveServerAsync and replace it with HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId.
- [ ] Test reserved-server teleport and join flows after migrating, including players arriving at the reserved server.
- [ ] Before each release, confirm the core loop is fun and stable, not just that the planned features exist.
- [ ] If the update cadence is under pressure, cut features rather than shipping half-working systems.
- [ ] After each bug fix, run a regression check on the systems the fix touched before pushing.
- [ ] Size a funded team's scope to market and demand evidence, not to a past studio's scope.
- [ ] Treat claims about platform recommendation rules from creators as unverified until checked against official docs.
- [ ] Add comments or descriptions that explain why code works and key design decisions, not just placement.
- [ ] After copy-pasting setup functions, check which function is called and confirm the prompt appears in a test run.
- [ ] Before submitting to a contest or jam, confirm the place is Public, loads without errors, and was built new for that event.
- [ ] Do not sell a bypass for a scarce reward you limited to one player at a time.
- [ ] Accept simple, working hidden scaffolding; polish only what players see.
- [ ] Before launch, confirm the server max player setting is correct (one rollout broke at a wrong value).
- [ ] Before any playtest, confirm the testing server is not public.
- [ ] List candidate features with a rough ROI estimate and do the cheap, high-return fixes first.
- [ ] Before prototyping a genre, play top-grossing games critically and note the solved design problems.
- [ ] Before adding cosmetics, confirm there is a social layer where status and player interaction matter.
- [ ] Do not count on cosmetics for revenue in single-player games.
- [ ] Give harvestable resources (e.g. trees) a despawn or respawn path so every server keeps supply.
- [ ] For contract work on another's game, quote a fixed fee and playtest thoroughly before signing.
- [ ] Check that each title delivers on its promise; cut long, boring intros.
- [ ] Keep each channel focused on 1–3 games.
- [ ] For kids' games, plan engagement against the actual age audience, not just viewer count.
- [ ] Before testing eggs or planting, confirm every imported model (pets, crop models) is inserted into the game and matches its config (T18816, T18873).
- [ ] Each seed or item in config needs a matching model; check this before testing to avoid breaking gameplay (T18873).
- [ ] After configuring drops or eggs, re-test them and check that placed objects are anchored (T18843).
- [ ] Test every developer product purchase in Studio to confirm each product ID grants the intended reward (T18854).
- [ ] After any change, test each sell and economy path end-to-end, including gold credit (T18879).
- [ ] Test that progression areas must be completed in order and cannot be skipped (T18824).
- [ ] Test point-to-point traversal and movement (e.g. hoverboards, levitation) before release (T18817).
- [ ] Test data saving and loading before any holiday or major update (T18806).
- [ ] Watch the output console from early builds onward and fix error floods at their root line (T18764).
- [ ] Test for duplication exploits (e.g. stacking rewards from one item) and patch quickly if players find one (T18766, Q1944).
- [ ] Before soft launch, write expected LTV, retention and crash rate per market and device (T18794).
- [ ] Check whether players will accept any plugin or generation tool before shipping with it (T18805).
- [ ] Announce any monetisation change before it goes live; do not hide paid progression (T18956).
- [ ] Re-test after each fix before pushing it live (T18766, T18843).
- [ ] Define the intended play area and keep core gameplay, objectives and spawns inside it.
- [ ] Block or clearly warn players who leave the intended play area, rather than letting core play happen outside it.
- [ ] Check competition or judged entries for gameplay outside the defined bounds before submitting.

## Where creators agree
- Pay-to-win and over-monetisation drive players away (Roblox Creators, GameMakers, Tizzy, Roblox DevForum).
- Don't start with a complex dream game (SmartyRBX repeatedly, Roblox Creators, DitchyDevelopments on feature creep).
- Ads can't fix a weak game (AlvinBlox, Tizzy, TheDankDeveloper, ItsZyonic, Roblox DevForum).
- Don't clone or template-reskin (Tizzy, Roblox Creator Docs, Daiorg, TheDankDeveloper).
- Chasing trends fails (Tizzy, kra_DeZ, Roblox official talks).
- Mobile-first, uncluttered, consistent UI (Roblox Creators, Tizzy, Creator Docs).
- Wait for experiment results; novelty effects mislead (Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox).
- Don't use IP without permission (Roblox, Creator Docs).
- Don't release and walk away (MoseyTube, Tizzy, kra_DeZ, Creator Docs).
- Stabilise bugs before spending on ads or scaling: janisjanis01 (T6344, T6430, T6503) and Duckable (T6550) agree.
- Do not clone or copy popular games: rasdev (T6587, T6903, T6775) and Yann (T6656) agree; Roblox Creator Docs (T9524) says copies are a hard sell for Standout Games.
- Do not learn by blindly copying tutorials or free models: Duckable (T6547, T6549) agrees with DevForum guidance to review free models (T5148).
- Anchor placed map parts: janisjanis01 (T6118, T6418, T6570) and Roblox docs (T9470) agree.
- Verify results on the server: official docs (T7910) and the DevForum anticheat note (T5316) agree client-side protection is weak.
- Use exact, case-sensitive names: official docs (T9247, T7038, T8929) and janisjanis01/Duckable typo debugging (T6690) agree.
- Client-side checks and FilteringEnabled alone are not security; validate on the server and design abuse to yield nothing (T10802, T10803, T14935).
- Ads and paid promotion alone do not rescue a game with no players or weak retention (T13412, T13155, T13622, Q1398, T13761).
- Percentage-only deals with unproven teams are risky and often never pay out (T11968, T12236, Q1234).
- Never share passwords and treat free-Robux or cash-out promises as scams (T14066, T14067, T13499, T10805).
- Verify the correct build and restart servers after a restore before players see changes (T15355, T13901).
- Stop chasing peaks; retention after the hype decides long-term success (T13674, T10598).
- Quality and economy must be fixed before paid ads or sponsorship (PolarBear, Poke, ThePurpleGuy, LovJak).
- Ads do not rescue a weak game; retention and execution come first (Poke, RoyalDev, Mulch).
- Communicate clearly rather than rely on players or wikis to explain the game (Cumin, CORSO).
- Execution and polish matter as much as the idea (RoyalDev, Cumin).
- Don't publish unchanged templates or copy viral hits (LovJak, Cumin).
- RoWatcher's T17426 and T17443 agree that cloned or kit-built games draw community backlash.
- Roblox Investor Relations (T17513, Q1753) agrees that traffic and hours can hide weakening per-hour monetization.
- Roblox OFFICIAL sources (T17575, T17730) agree that safety requires prevention by design plus continuous iteration after launch.
- JustDucksey (T17804), MonarchDev (T17851) and Duckable (T17940) agree that rushed releases add bugs and erode game quality or fun.
- Monetisation surprises and disliked tools cause fast, severe player backlash (janisjanis01 T18805; Deconstructor of Fun T18956, Q1974).
- Test core systems (purchases, progression, economy, saving) before release rather than relying on player reports (janisjanis01 T18854, T18879, T18824).

## Where creators disagree
- **Is monetisation restraint or heavy monetisation the mistake?**
  - Tizzy, Roblox Creators, GameMakers: over-monetising and P2W kills games.
  - 1_Bruce: over-restraint hurt revenue (about a third of a comparable game, self-reported).
  - Alex Builds Games: too many methods dilute the best ones.
- **Does game quality matter?**
  - AlvinBlox, 1_Bruce, Tizzy: weak games fail; fix the game.
  - CaberJaber (low-advice): quality doesn't seem to matter.
  - TheDankDeveloper: even the biggest games are low-effort cash grabs.
  - Daiorg: numbers don't directly translate to quality.
- **Cloning and early growth**
  - Daiorg: cloning can drive early growth but hurts platform reputation.
  - Tizzy and Roblox Creator Docs: clones and templates rank lower and fail.
- **Ads and recommendations**
  - Tizzy: not updating is why ad spend doesn't get you on the algorithm.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026): ad-acquired users are excluded from RFY signals, so an RFY drop after ads is not a penalty.
- **Monetisation of copied or familiar loops: Yann (T6513) says add meaningful monetisation instead of filler; rasdev (T6700) says do not paywall basics like sprinting or teleporting and avoid purchase-prompt clutter. Not a true conflict: monetise extras and depth, not basics.**
  - Yann (T6513): add meaningful monetisation instead of filler when copying a core loop.
  - rasdev (T6700): do not paywall basics; avoid clutter with purchase prompts.
- **When to check ad performance versus when to spend: janisjanis01 (T6503) says check ad performance early and replace creative at about 1.9% CTR; janisjanis01 (T6344, T6430) and Duckable (T6550) say stabilise before buying ads. Reconcile: run a small creative check early, but do not scale spend before the build is stable.**
  - janisjanis01 (T6503): check ad performance early.
  - janisjanis01 (T6344, T6430) and Duckable (T6550): stabilise the game before buying ads.
- **Reusing proven structures vs. avoiding clones and reskins**
  - MonarchDev (T13713): don't over-invest in originality; proven structures can be reused and reskinned, as with the Grow a Garden and Sell Lemons UI.
  - MonarchDev (T13786, T13799): don't just clone a game; change the core concept while keeping the proven loop.
  - VuukStudios (T13031, Q1343): avoid low-effort template clones; some studios reskin one template for ~2,000 CCU.
  - PineappleDev (T12709): reskinning a successful game into a sequel did not carry over its success; viral hits are not guaranteed to repeat.
  - MonarchDev (N5424): snow-reskinned clones of leaves underperformed the original.
- **When to optimise and handle streaming**
  - Roblox DevForum (T11001): as a beginner, don't optimise for performance or good practice too early.
  - Roblox DevForum (T10607): don't use StreamingEnabled as a first resort for per-player content.
  - Existing rule 'Design network efficiency in from the start' conflicts in timing; reconcile by profiling first and designing network shape early without premature micro-optimisation.
- **Whether UpdateAsync is safer than SetAsync**
  - Suphi Kaner (T11764): UpdateAsync is not inherently more secure than SetAsync; it only helps when you read and modify the previous value.
  - Roblox DevForum (T11937): suggests UpdateAsync for safer host-data writes and warns against SetAsync on shared keys from many servers.
- **Ship early vs. ship only when proud of the game**
  - Existing rule: do not spend months polishing; validate demand and ship.
  - Raul (T13647, T13605): don't ship a game you are not proud of; very fast finishes with little content can signal thin gameplay.
  - Reconcile: ship early to validate, but hold a minimum quality bar for the core loop.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- GameAnalytics setup advice (T3325-T3328, Q669) dates from 2017 and may be obsolete.
- 2021 advice that PC is an afterthought should be weighed against 2025 guidance to support all devices including console.
- Rewarded ad rules (no RNG) come from 2025-2026 standards and supersede looser practices.
- Creator Rewards (2025) replaces EBP, removing the long-session advantage.
- Cross-game pass sales were disabled May 30, 2026; payout withholding applies from Nov 1, 2026.
- 2025 official clarification that games are never permanently quarantined supersedes older folklore; 2026 Tizzy says bounce rate now kills clickbait titles.
- Subscription API notes (T3529, Q705) are 2023 launch-era.
- Age rules differ between listings (13+ vs 16+/18+ for social hangouts); prefer the latest docs.
- Indie figures are self-reported and unverified.
- PointsService: removal was announced for June 30 (2023) in a 2019 DevForum post (T5070, Q885); treat as obsolete and use leaderstats or own data.
- Many 2019-2021 DevForum tips (GC, spawn(), custom Humanoid, FindFirstChild) predate the Oct 2026 official docs (T8092, T8093, T8139, T10110); prefer the official docs where they conflict.
- spawn() advice dates from 2020 (T5267); the current docs use task-based scheduling (T8139).
- Oct 2026 official rules on Build games, Open Use permissions, avatar caging, Open Cloud error tables, and memory store 45-day lifetime may change; verify before relying on them.
- Creator metrics (ad CTR, Robux spend, CCU drops) are 2025-2026 anecdotes and vary widely by genre and audience.
- As of 2026-10-29: the Phase 2 opt-out window (H2 2026) is current; check the status of the legacy PD and opt-out flags before relying on them (T13159, T13453).
- Platform-integrated chat becomes mandatory May 2027; about 18 months away, so plan migration now (N5577, T14033).
- The Transfers API launched May 4, 2026 and cross-game sales were disabled May 29, 2026; both dates are past (N5166).
- Private for playtesters was limited to Edit-permission creators in May 2026; Limited > Playtesters is the replacement (T13383).
- Private audio's March 22, 2022 cutoff and the 6-second third-party audio exception are older rules; verify current audio handling (T11321, T11316).
- Older advice that is now superseded: roblox-js (2017, use noblox.js), Yucon (2020, avoid), StreamingEnabled as a first resort (2020), outdated 2023 tutorial scripts (T10464, T10704, T10607, T11544).
- Replica renamed members in 2024 (AddCleanupTask became Maid:Add); check names against the current library version (T12419).
- Server Authority-enabled games were not supported for player clients as of Dec 2025 (T13087); recheck before publishing.
- Graphs did not play in-experience as of Apr 2026 (Studio only); this may have changed (T13248).
- Official 2026-10 Creator Docs items are the latest platform ground truth; prefer them over older indie advice when they conflict.
- The Aug 2026 official notice says bot-inflated player counts are being corrected; do not treat pre-correction numbers as benchmarks (T13723).
- Older advice (Last Level Studios 2019-2021, ThatGuyEvan 2015-2020, Rover scale 2020) predates current server and hosting tooling; verify against current docs before acting on infrastructure numbers.
- The Studio Access to API Services setting (Amora, 2023-08) and leaderstats naming may have changed; verify in current Studio.
- Moderation queue times (2025) and TikTok shadowban advice (2025) are self-reports and change over time.
- The most recent creators (RoyalDev, LovJak, Cumin, 2026) emphasise execution, polish and retention over ad spend; prefer these where they conflict with older ad-first advice.
- Items tagged low-advice are weaker evidence and should not outweigh official docs or multiple-creator agreement.
- 2026-07 (Roblox Investor Relations): reported a bookings shortfall from declining per-hour monetization, especially among younger U.S. and Canadian cohorts. Treat per-hour monetization by cohort as the current metric to watch.
- 2026-08 (eGamers): external embed and off-platform sharing guidance. Policy lists change, so verify the current approved list before relying on it.
- 2026-09 (Roblox Investor Relations): 27-month average paying-user lifetime used for cost-of-revenue deferral. Figures apply to Roblox's own reporting, not to individual developers' games.
- DevEx: as of 2026-09, US DevEx transitions away in mid-2027 and other regions later (T17654, T17666). Check current announcements, since this may change.
- Moderation stance: in 2025-08 Roblox said it wants to be fair and not moderate developers for user behavior (Q1772). In 2026-09 it said pending earnings may be held or forfeited for Terms of Use violations (T17655). Treat the 2025 statement as stated intent, not a guarantee.
- Flagged-content handling: the 2026-10 guidance to remove flagged instances for everyone (T17720, Q1793) is the current approach; per-user hiding is not recommended.
- Expected ~40% ban reduction (N7517, 2026-09) is a forecast; verify against actual outcomes.
- 2026-10: the official Roblox Creator Docs say ReserveServerAsync() does not reserve a server (it only returns a random GUID). Any older guidance treating it as a real reservation is outdated; use GenerateGUID with ReservedServerId instead.
- T17984 (Deconstructor of Fun, 2026-10) on AI disclosure labels reducing sales is Steam-specific and the creator notes the data is confounded by top-game skew; it is not Roblox platform ground truth.
- T17899 (Alex Builds Games, 2026-10) claims Roblox docs say direct copies won't earn home recommendations. This is self-reported and not verified in these notes; check current official Roblox docs before relying on it.
- T17941 (MonarchDev, 2026-10) reflects current AI tool limits: a single prompt yields a prototype, not a finished game.
- Newest items are from 2026-05 (Cumin) and 2025-10 (Duckable); no platform changes are mentioned in these items.
- Competition rule on public, runnable entries (2025-10) reinforces existing public-upload guidance; no conflicts with earlier advice.
- Q1871 (2020-05) is the oldest item; its tycoon remark is dated and should be weighted below newer items.
- No new item conflicts with an existing rule. The 2024–2026 items (T18542, Q1893, T18588, T18590, T18610, T18640, T18686, T18725) take precedence where they overlap older advice.
- T18686 (2026-06) on kids' game trial thresholds is low-advice and may change with platform policy; verify against current official criteria.
- T18725 (2026-10) on testing server visibility reflects current Studio/testing practice; re-check if settings change.
- Items span 2024-03 to 2026-07; no conflict with existing rules. The EA/Battlefront 2 numbers are an older, historical case cited in a 2026 video; treat them as illustration, not current market data.
- Physics-driven and plugin-related advice (2024–2025) is creator experience, not platform guidance; check current Roblox docs before relying on specific physics or tool behaviour.
- T19002 (GnomeCode) is from 2020-06, so it is older guidance on judging play-area bounds; verify against current contest rules and official guidelines before relying on it.
- Q1976 (AlvinBlox, 2025-04) repeats the existing rule about not relying on ads to rescue a weak game, so it was not added.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If a user proposes a large first game, then recommend shrinking scope to one core loop and shipping early.
- If a user wants to buy ads for a game with poor retention, then advise fixing the loop first.
- If implementing purchases, then process via ProcessReceipt and server checks, never prompt-finished events or client subscription checks.
- If showing prices, then fetch them from MarketplaceService instead of hard-coding.
- If designing rewarded ads, then make rewards deterministic, account-bound, developer products, with no RNG.
- If designing UI, then check mobile layout, contrast, palette consistency and controller support first.
- If reading A/B results, then wait the full duration (about two weeks), segment players, and don't change content mid-test.
- If third-party IP or AI-modified work appears, then advise licensing or removal; credit and fair use belief don't protect.
- If a feature has unbounded counts or referral rewards, then add server caps, one-time flags and cooldowns.
- If quoting indie CCU or revenue claims, then label them self-reported; prefer official docs for mechanics.
- If code connects Touched on a Model, loop over child BaseParts and connect each one.
- If the user wants performance gains, profile first; do not build a custom Humanoid or blame the default one without evidence.
- If the user asks to free memory, clear all references and call Instance:Destroy(); never recommend collectgarbage().
- If the user passes tables through a BindableFunction, use a pure array or pure dictionary with no nil values.
- If the user plans ad spend, first confirm bugs are fixed and check CTR on a small test before scaling.
- If the user's concept copies a top-earning game, flag clone risk and ask for one differentiating mechanic.
- If the user is about to set an asset to Open Use or grant a game permission, warn that it is irreversible.
- If player-visible text is broadcast, require the text filter call inside pcall before sending.
- If the user cites PointsService, redirect them to leaderstats or their own data store.
- If the user asks for a whole game from one AI prompt or a free model they don't understand, advise building in stages and reviewing the code first.
- If the user's game uses a deprecated or migrating API (CameraAction, FilterDescendantsInstances, PlatformIntegratedChat, legacy PD), check the migration date first and recommend the replacement.
- If the user's game uses Touched or cached AnimationTrack objects under rollback or Server Authority, switch to GetTouchingParts(), InputAction:GetState() and GetTrackByAnimationId.
- If a reward is tied to fast completion or teleporting, gate it with server-side state checks rather than trusting the client.
- If the user asks about passwords, free Robux or Robux-to-cash sites, warn that these are scams and refuse to help with them.
- If the user asks about launch timing, separate 'polish forever' from 'ship a thin game': recommend a minimum quality bar for the core loop.
- If the user proposes a clone or reskin of a hit, flag it and recommend changing the core concept while keeping the proven loop.
- If the user asks how to monetise, recommend designing anchor features first and avoiding gated core movement or camera.
- If the user copies audio, models or code, verify the licence and the source age before use; 'royalty free' is not a licence.
- For production persistence, recommend a session-locked library (ProfileStore-style) and UpdateAsync for read-modify-write.
- When a tutorial or library is more than a few years old, check current API names and deprecations before copying it.
- If a user wants to spend on ads or sponsorship, first check retention, bugs and the economy; advise against spend until they pass.
- If a user says a game is dead, audit execution and friction (polish, explanation, dead buttons) before advising to scrap the concept.
- If a user adds a script or Toolbox asset, advise reading it and measuring frame-rate impact before shipping.
- If a user is building systems with AI or copied code, check that the systems connect end to end.
- If a user plans a re-release, advise modernising matchmaking and progression rather than relying on nostalgia.
- If a user asks about events, advise unified, free progression and enough original content per objective.
- If a user is targeting children, avoid aggressive FOMO and gotcha mechanics.
- Treat indie figures as self-reported; do not present them as benchmarks without context.
- If a user's concept is a clone of a top game, warn about copycat accusation risk even if they plan to credit the source, and recommend original mechanics and art.
- If a user uses a clone kit or asset pack, flag the asset-flip backlash risk and recommend original character animation.
- When reviewing a game's metrics, ask for monetization per hour by age cohort, not only DAUs and hours.
- When a user wants external video or social feeds in-game, check the approved-link policy first and advise against it if unsure.
- When comparing Roblox financial figures, don't equate bookings-based and revenue-based margins.
- Treat the Roblox Investor Relations items as platform ground truth and the RoWatcher and eGamers items as self-reported, low-advice sources.
- If a user plans income from DevEx, check the latest announced end date for their region before advising.
- If moderation flags content, advise removing it for everyone, not per-user hiding.
- If the user builds open-ended creation, avatar, or roleplay features, advise design-level prevention before detection tooling.
- If the user adds engagement counters, show only real metrics and warn against artificial counts.
- If the user designs a one-shot mission action, require clear signposting and no forced-restart punishment.
- If the user adds AI or NPC behavior, default to cheap rules and escalate only where needed.
- Treat indie figures (GosuGamers, AllThingsHow, AlvinBlox) as self-reported; treat Roblox OFFICIAL items as platform ground truth.
- Don't cite N7517's 40% as a measured result.
- If code calls ReserveServerAsync expecting a real server reservation, flag it and migrate to HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId before shipping.
- Treat [OFFICIAL] docs as ground truth over older creator advice when they conflict.
- If a release is under cadence pressure and core fun is unproven, cut scope rather than ship half-working systems.
- If a bug fix is ready, run regression tests on the touched systems before pushing.
- If a user asks about home recommendations for near-copy games, state the claim is self-reported and direct them to official docs.
- If a user asks about AI disclosure effects on sales, note the Steam data is confounded and not Roblox-specific.
- If a funded team is planning scope, anchor it to demand and market evidence first.
- If a submission to a contest is requested, first verify it is Public, runs without errors, and is original work for that event.
- If copying a setup function between scripts, verify the correct function is called and the prompt or feature works in a test run.
- If writing scripts for others, add comments that explain the reasoning and design decisions, not only where to paste them.
- If a reward is scarce, do not offer a paid bypass; keep the scarcity fair.
- If hidden geometry or scaffolding is out of view, prefer the simplest working method over perfect cleanliness.
- If a user proposes cosmetics as a revenue source in a single-player game, flag it as weak and suggest a social layer first.
- If a user asks what to build next, rank candidates by ROI and cheap wins before big features.
- If a user plans to build for another person's game on a revenue share, recommend a fixed fee and a thorough playtest before signing.
- If a user makes a kids' game and relies on viewer counts, flag the engagement threshold risk and check current platform criteria.
- If a user is about to playtest, remind them to confirm the testing server is not public.
- If a user is about to launch, prompt them to check the server max player setting.
- If a user builds harvestable resources, make sure they have despawn or respawn logic per server.
- If a user plans many games on one channel, suggest focusing on 1–3 games.
- If server lag appears with many physics-constrained moving parts, evaluate simpler scripted movement for those parts and profile before polishing content.
- If the output console shows repeated errors, fix the root cause line first, then re-test.
- If a player reports an exploit or duplication, patch it quickly and re-test the fix before pushing.
- If the game's core loop is weak, do not add manipulative mechanics or monetisation to compensate; fix the loop first.
- If planning a monetisation change, announce it clearly before it goes live; never hide paid progression.
- If using a plugin or generation tool, check community acceptance before relying on it for the shipped product.
- Before soft launch, set expected LTV, retention and crash rate by market and device so results are not a surprise.
- Before any holiday or large update, test save/load paths and each economy path end-to-end.
- When configuring product IDs, drops or eggs, verify each in Studio and confirm placed models are anchored and present.
- Treat backlash as a fast-moving risk; respond to community concerns early and transparently.
- If core gameplay occurs outside the defined play area, flag it as a risk before submitting to contests or judging.
- If a new item restates an existing rule about ads or marketing rescuing weak games, skip it rather than duplicating the rule.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No concrete benchmarks for retention, conversion or CCU thresholds beyond anecdotes.
- Little on Luau performance and memory mistakes beyond a few examples.
- Little on team management, payroll and tax pitfalls beyond partnership advice.
- Current Creator Rewards payout formulas not covered.
- Anti-exploit server validation patterns are thin.
- Legal specifics (COPPA, regional compliance) need external research.
- Conflicting age thresholds for social hangouts need checking against current docs.
- No benchmarks for typical CCU, retention or conversion rates across genres.
- No DevEx payout rates or revenue projections beyond the anecdotes above.
- No guidance on Luau typing or strict mode, automated testing frameworks, or version control (git) workflows.
- Little on performance budgets (frame time, physics or instance counts) beyond the anchoring and network notes.
- No timelines for Roblox moderation or review turnaround for uploads and Build games.
- No detailed guidance on appeals or what to do after a Roblox takedown beyond the 'remove and comply' notes.
- Limited coverage of marketing channel strategy beyond the anecdotal thumbnail and banner examples.
- No quantitative retention, conversion or CCU benchmarks beyond a few anecdotes; the knowledge base lacks typical day-1/day-7 retention targets.
- No pricing guidance for Robux price points, DevEx conversion or subscription economics beyond the shop example.
- No detailed guidance on effective marketing or promotion channels; the sources mostly report failures.
- Limited coverage of Roblox policy specifics (full Community Standards, age-check mechanics, moderation timelines) beyond the listed items.
- No testing methodology for multiplayer, rollback or Server Authority beyond the warnings; check official networking docs.
- Little on mobile performance budgets or device-specific UI testing numbers.
- No legal or contract templates; the sources only warn about royalty and percentage deals.
- Several platform changes (Phase 2 opt-out, chat mandate, Server Authority) have dates or statuses that must be checked against current official announcements.
- Unclear status of graphs, the new avatar controller and banned capability lists after 2026-04; verify before advising.
- No guidance on how long to reach traction or how many games to release before success, beyond single-creator anecdotes.
- Not covered in these notes: level-design flow, balance without dominance (T11221), cooldown and filtering details beyond the listed items, and asset-specific UGC policy checks; check the source notes or official docs.
- No official Roblox docs were provided for moderation queue times, Studio settings or DevForum guidance on the listed traps; verify against the Developer Hub.
- No data on how often admin commands cause quits beyond CORSO's unquantified internal research.
- No counter-evidence or failure rates for ads across a broad sample; figures are single-creator anecdotes.
- No guidance on sponsorship contract terms, pricing or payment structures beyond escrow and the transfer example.
- No specific frame-rate or server-cost benchmarks for common scripts beyond the 30% scan example.
- No information on Roblox policy changes affecting child-targeted monetisation or ad rules after 2026.
- No quantitative data on how often clone or kit-built games are reported or removed.
- No detail on the contents of Roblox's approved external link list or the exact off-platform sharing rules.
- No actual per-hour monetization figures or cohort breakdowns are given, only the direction of the trend.
- How the Fish It! developers handled the backlash, and whether crediting helped, is not documented.
- The eGamers quote (Q1754) is satirical and gives no actionable rule, so it was not added.
- Q1731 (AlvinBlox) repeats an existing rule about not relying on marketing to fix a weak game, so it was not added.
- No explanation of what the 'coded speech' bypasses are or how to filter them (T17575).
- No details on how long earnings are held or what conditions cause forfeiture (T17655).
- No replacement program details or regional end dates beyond the US mid-2027 date (T17654, T17666).
- Q1796 ('Shiao', three levels) has no context in the sources; its relevance to common mistakes is unclear.
- The GosuGamers (Liz's quest) and AllThingsHow (Steal An Egg) cases are not verified against official sources.
- No definition or examples of the 'intelligence layers' referred to in T17741.
- N7487 (4,401 bugs closed) has no baseline, so its meaning for creators is limited.
- Sources do not give the exact ReservedServerId usage or teleport signature; check the official API reference before implementing.
- Q1805 (AlvinBlox, 2025-04: fix the game loop before marketing) was skipped because it repeats an existing rule about not relying on ads to rescue a weak game.
- No official Roblox documentation on home recommendation rules for near-copy games is included in these sources.
- No quantitative data linking release cadence to bug rates or player retention.
- No Roblox-specific evidence on the effect of AI disclosure labels.
- No concrete guidance on regression test tooling or test coverage for Roblox projects.
- Scope-setting for funded studios is covered only by a single self-reported anecdote.
- No official (OFFICIAL) sources in this batch; contest rules and eliminations are indie, self-reported.
- No total entry count for the Duckable contest, so elimination rate cannot be computed.
- No concrete code examples for the setup-function mix-up or PrimaryPart issues beyond the tip lines.
- Q1849 (effects that look easy to replicate) and Q1865 (chasing visits) were not added: they repeat existing rules on cloning and inflated metrics.
- Duplicates skipped: testing after each change (covered by re-test rule), reskin-as-lazy (covered by clone/reskin rule), PrimaryPart before scripting (covered by PrimaryPart rule), marketing cannot fix weak game (covered by ads rule).
- No ROI calculation method or worked example; research how to estimate ROI for Roblox features.
- Official documentation on the max players setting and its default; verify in Roblox docs.
- Official rules for testing server visibility and how to make a testing server private.
- Official age-rating and engagement criteria for kids' games and the trial threshold; the T18686 claim is unverified.
- Typical fixed-fee rates for contract development work on Roblox games.
- Efficient resource respawn patterns (timers, pooling, per-server state) with code examples.
- Analysis method for top-grossing games and what metrics to compare.
- Evidence on clickbait versus retention and channel-growth benchmarks.
- No official Roblox sources in these items; physics-constraint performance claims are single-creator experience and need verification against current docs and profiler data.
- No benchmark values for expected LTV, retention or crash rates by market or device; the sources only say to set them.
- No guidance on how to recover from backlash or rebuild trust after a monetisation controversy.
- No specifics on which physics alternatives work best for many moving platforms.
- No data on how the console error count affects performance or player experience beyond the single example.
- Only one new item (T19002, a single 2020 comment) was supplied; no evidence on how current judging or Roblox guidelines treat out-of-bounds play.
- No new numbers or benchmarks were provided for common mistakes in this batch.
- No corroborating creators for the play-area rule; it is single-source and self-reported.

## Quotes (near-verbatim)
- "Call THIS ONCE IN THE ENTIRE GAME!!!!!!!" ([Roblox DevForum 2017-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083))
- "If you've bait[ed] players with an advert, how are they going to trust you that their products are going to be what they expect." ([Roblox 2018-07 22:51](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1371))
- "If you fork the player scripts to do that, there is now an Xbox-shaped hole in your total addressable market." ([Roblox 2018-07 3:59](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=239))
- "A computer can't pull off stunts like this for you; without open communication with translators, they won't rewrite content." ([Roblox 2018-07 15:18](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=918))
- "Roblox has recently announced their plan to remove support for PointsService on June 30th." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754))
- "Anything your community does often reflects on you, even if it's against your rules and you don't condone it." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 6:17](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=377))
- "Unlike in vanilla lua, it is impossible to force a garbage collection." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954))
- "Before you blame humanoids for your game's lackluster performance, analyze your code." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801))
- "the feature games would get lucky if they've reached a hundred players" ([anomalousDucky 2020-01 2:44](https://youtu.be/R5LnkhhJtno?t=164))
- "asking for help on the forum sucks because the first guy replies says something really dumb and it's right there at the top forever" ([Last Level Studios 2020-03 34:07](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=2047))

- "copycats die anyways" ([rasdev 2026-10 6:48](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=408))

- "Verify that your calls to the AnalyticsService methods occur after a successful operation, not when the user attempts a purchase." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types))
- "if you engage in malicious practices to inflate impressions or teleports, Roblox might deduct Robux from your expected payout or reclaim the Robux you earned" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads))
- "It's more challenging to develop a monetization strategy after launching your game." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization))
- "Base your answers on the most mature or extreme content players can encounter within your experience." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines))
- "If you intentionally misrepresent your experience, you may be subject to moderation consequences." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines))
- "Only false and nil are considered false." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/expressions))
- "Users are likely to downvote your game if they do not approve of your strategies." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization))
- "Otherwise, players may not know that the action they took did anything, and wonder if they did something wrong, or wonder if the game is buggy." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques))
- "Submitting a knowingly false copyright notice or counter notice is illegal and may subject you to liability under section 512(f) of the Digital Millennium Copyright Act." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines))
- "You can still infringe a copyright even if you didn't monetize your content, credited the original creator, or downloaded it from the internet." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines))
- "You remain responsible for permanently deleting all personal data covered by an RTBF request." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators))
- "if you have hard-coded the price into your game's UI, that number does not update as it's not dynamic or accessible by Roblox." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing))
- "If you do not complete your registration within one week of receiving the registration link, Roblox automatically declines your DevEx request and refunds your Earned Robux." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange))
- "If players can use the wheel to "say anything, just slower," the design is not permitted." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines))
- "Per Roblox 2026 standards, no RNG. You cannot reward a 'Random Legendary Item.'" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg))
- "If your game contains inaccurate content maturity information, your game and account are subject to moderation." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity))
- "Base your answers on the most mature or extreme content players can encounter within your game." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity))
- "We originally had lore on everything you could click on! This wasn't helpful because players weren't sure what information was important." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative))

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2015-01-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743): Do not treat the Right/Top/Back vectors as positions; they only encode orientation, and the position is CFrame.p alone.
- 2016-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756): Do not sacrifice readability for micro-optimizations; apply basic optimizations that keep code easy to read.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Never expose the GameKey and SecretKey publicly; keep them out of shared or distributed code.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Call ServerInit only once in the entire game, and ClientInit once per client; repeat calls break event tracking.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Ensure HTTP service is enabled in the game settings, otherwise GameAnalytics events will not send.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Do not put spaces in plain-text string arguments to analytics functions; spaces break the event formatting.
- 2017-10-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201): Don't use roblox-js anymore; Roblox changed logins to require cookies. Use noblox.js, which the author says has better support for the new login.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Don't focus on monetisation early; spend that time designing the anchor features of the game instead.
- 2018-02-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082): Don't assume Lua's GC will free cycles through Instance events; connections are held in C++ and invisible to Lua, so a part referenced indirectly via a table still leaks.
- 2018-05-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601): Don't take all the credit; bragging makes teammates resent you rather than admire you.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [10:03](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=603): Don't put persistent panels (e.g. a task list) where they overlap restricted areas; players then can't move or act while it's open.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [10:43](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=643): Keep button positions consistent across screens. Swapping Delete and Save between screens causes muscle-memory mis-clicks and bad surprises.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:51](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=711): Premium currency hoards build up and top players spend stored balance instead of buying; plan sinks before big content drops.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [24:44](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1484): Don't rush a trend game with heavy monetisation at the expense of fun; one speaker's rushed monetised game and fun unmonetised game both underperformed.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [27:33](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1653): Check where players spend time before adding shop items; items placed in areas players ignore won't sell.
