- 2018-07-24 Roblox [22:51](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1371): Avoid clickbait ads; they may boost early numbers but damage trust in your studio brand.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [39:23](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=2363): Don't spam advertisements in groups or comments; it risks moderator bans. Read the Terms of Service.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [3:59](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=239): Avoid forking control scripts per platform unless intentional; a PC-only fork cuts off console/tablet/mobile players from day one.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [9:15](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=555): Don't build kill feeds or messages from concatenated sentences with parameters; grammar in Spanish etc. changes verb forms and gender, forcing sentence rewrites.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [15:18](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=918): Machine translation loses idioms, puns and memes; add translator notes explaining intent and maintain two-way communication with translators.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [31:25](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1885): Don't ignore environment quality: visual polish affects how outsiders judge a game's popularity, so unpolished art lowers results.
- 2018-08-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780): Check your rate-limit window math: the example sets windowMs to 15*10000 (150 seconds) while the comment says 15 minutes.
- 2018-11-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509): Avoid defining methods with a custom self parameter name; it hurts readability for others, so keep the conventional self.
- 2019-01-26 AlvinBlox [24:29](https://youtu.be/0UhMpxxRFyc?t=1469): When checking a BoolValue, compare its .Value (e.g. 'if not debounce.Value'), not the instance itself, or the check silently misbehaves.
- 2019-03-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902): Avoid pay-to-win: if players think you over-monetised, they call the game P2W and leave.
- 2019-03-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902): Do not rely on paid entry as a growth lever; thumbs-up gains do not reliably correlate with success.
- 2019-03-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142): Do not use this module in production; author states it is untested with multiple servers and is a demo for the new MessagingService.
- 2019-03-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754): Do not rely on PointsService; Roblox announced its removal on June 30 (2023), so reroute the value to leaderstats or your own data.
- 2019-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629): Rewards tied to fast completion (e.g. The Labyrinth's coin reward for escaping) get exploited by teleporting; remove or gate them with state checks.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Don't use the CFrameValue-plus-SetPrimaryPartCFrame legacy pattern; the author says it's fragile and should not be accepted as a solution.
- 2019-09-06 Last Level Studios [6:17](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=377): Assume community actions reflect on you; spam done in your community's name will be blamed on the owner, so enforce rules.
- 2019-09-13 Last Level Studios [49:27](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2967): Don't limit job hunting to big-name studios; most openings are at smaller companies, and mobile/free-to-play is a realistic entry point.
- 2019-09-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825): Always attach a catch to Promise chains; the library warns in the console when a chain errors without a catch handler.
- 2019-10-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838): Remember nil and false are falsy in if statements; all other values, including 0 and empty tables, are truthy.
- 2019-10-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838): Beware that ipairs stops at the first nil gap in an array; use pairs for tables with gaps.
- 2019-10-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838): Don't use free models blindly; many contain malicious scripts. Review code before putting it in your game.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [28:45](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1725): Understand an API or script before implementing it; untested features like a camera bob script that tanks frame rate hurt the game.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [30:48](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1848): Learn from the Roblox Developer Hub and DevForum, and check the Toolbox for scripts, but verify and understand them before use.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Do not call collectgarbage("collect") in Roblox; it errors, and GC timing is unpredictable and undocumented, so memory cannot be freed on demand.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Do not assume a root script environment will be collected; the author could not force it, though it collects when all threads referencing it are yielding or dead with no references to yielding threads.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Remember numbers are never garbage collected and strings in your script keep their copies alive; these are not reliable leak sources to chase.
- 2019-11-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801): Don't build a custom Humanoid just for performance; the default is refined and useful, so only replace it if you won't need most of its features.
- 2019-11-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618): Define variables and functions before using them; Lua reads top to bottom, so using them earlier returns nil.
- 2019-11-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618): Keep print, function names and keywords lowercase and without spaces before parentheses; incorrect casing or spacing makes Lua fail to run the command.
- 2019-11-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618): Do not put quotes around variable names when you want their value; quotes turn the name into a literal string.
- 2019-11-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618): Remember order of operations: exponents, then multiply/divide, then add/subtract. print(1 + 5 * 2) gives 11, not 12.
- 2019-12-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930): Don't call table.remove while looping with ipairs; the iterator doesn't account for length changes and skips elements.
- 2019-12-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930): Table keys match by reference, not contents: x[{"a"}] will not find a value set with a different table literal. Store the table in a variable and reuse it.
- 2019-12-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930): The # operator only counts the array part; use a pairs-based counter to get the full size of a dictionary-containing table.
- 2019-12-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930): Avoid table.insert/table.remove at arbitrary positions in large tables; shifting elements is inefficient.
- 2020-01-03 anomalousDucky [2:44](https://youtu.be/R5LnkhhJtno?t=164): Live Ops was poorly executed: the speaker says many featured games got under 100 players, so visibility alone did not help small games.
- 2020-01-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374): Don't use StreamingEnabled as a first resort for per-player content; advanced developers who know their game's mechanics can optimise better than its general-purpose defaults.
- 2020-02-07 Last Level Studios [17:22](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=1042): Sandbox user code so errors and infinite loops cannot crash Studio; this lets players experiment freely.
- 2020-03-01 Last Level Studios [15:46](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=946): Plan infrastructure for scale early: Rover hit ~100k servers and needed 32+ GB RAM, forcing a rewrite and a separate hosted service.
- 2020-03-01 Last Level Studios [9:21](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=561): Lock down scope with sponsors; mid-project asset rejections forced deleted work and a 15-hour-day crunch at the end.
- 2020-03-23 Last Level Studios [6:50](https://youtu.be/n0ODJH9EgWc?t=410): Rushed, poorly edited video tutorials are worse than well-written articles; pick a content format you can produce to a decent standard.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [12:18](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=738): Pay-to-win designs self-correct on Roblox: players leave for other content, so over-monetised games lose players rather than whales staying.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [29:03](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=1743): Don't assume mobile designs transfer: Roblox games are social and synchronous, which differs from mostly asynchronous mobile games.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [35:31](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=2131): Roblox's advantage is its built-in 115M MAU user base and user-generated expression, which new competitors lack.
- 2020-04-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830): Don't just disable the Security toggles and stop; you must still remove the malicious content from the game.
- 2020-04-21 ThatGuyEvan [11:52](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=712): Splitting the 2020 hunt across 48 games with no hub and paid passes for easier eggs drew heavy backlash; avoid fragmenting progression and paywalling it.
- 2020-04-21 ThatGuyEvan [10:45](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=645): In 2019 players disliked forcing a second boss fight to reach the good ending; avoid making the best ending require repeating content.
- 2020-04-21 ThatGuyEvan [5:16](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=316): The 2015 hunt used only three developer-made minigames for 22 eggs and is widely seen as the worst; thin content hurts event reception.
- 2020-04-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683): Avoid spawn() due to reported delays (up to 15 seconds); coroutines or task-based alternatives are preferred.
- 2020-04-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683): Be aware a FastSpawn BindableEvent trick runs in the main thread's error scope; an error in its body will affect the main thread.
- 2020-06-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yucon-framework-optimization-organization-and-high-level-security/630895): Do not adopt Yucon. The author says it is more memory-hungry and buggier than other frameworks.
- 2020-06-26 GnomeCode [41:43](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2503): Judges flagged gameplay happening outside the defined play area as controversial; keep core gameplay inside the intended bounds.
- 2020-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943): Don't allow sprinting backwards or sideways in first person; check MoveDirection against LookVector dot product and reset FOV/speed when out of range.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475): Do not hire a friend to avoid conflict or keep someone on who is falling behind; it damages the whole team.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475): Avoid cutting royalty percentages later, which causes disputes; decide compensation structure before the project starts.
- 2020-07-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958): Avoid spending time building client-sided anticheat; the author recommends not wasting effort on it.
- 2020-07-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753): Set PrimaryPart on models to a transparent hitbox part; without it collision and placement checks won't work correctly.
- 2020-08-24 Last Level Studios [31:13](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1873): Lag-skipped sensors can leave a train stuck in a block and hold up the whole network; plan for missed enter/exit events.
- 2020-08-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207): Attachment names must match exactly between Handle and body part. Mismatched names fail to weld, or attach to the wrong attachment.
- 2020-08-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207): Do not give multiple attachments the same name; results may be unsatisfactory. Use concise, meaningful names.
- 2020-08-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207): Roblox overrides any welds or motors you create on a real Accessory with its own. For custom-controlled attachments, wrap the parts in a Model instead.
- 2020-09-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594): Do not rely on FilteringEnabled alone; it only stops unvalidated replication to the server and does not stop exploiters from reading client code.
- 2020-09-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594): Do not paste anti-exploit scripts only on the client; an exploiter can delete them and be free of the check.
- 2020-09-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594): Do not assume window-name detection works; most exploits randomise window names, and injection detection is weak.
- 2020-09-17 Emancyphur [1:29](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=89): Skill compounding matters: MiniToon spent ~3 years on free models and smaller projects before Piggy; his scripting and building skill made a one-week prototype viable.
- 2020-09-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/account-security-psa/792056): Never click links you do not recognize, even from friends; phishing DMs can come from compromised accounts that look friendly.
- 2021-01-02 AlvinBlox [4:03](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=243): Avoid free models in published games; players can tell from materials and meshes, and they make the game look amateur.
- 2021-01-02 AlvinBlox [8:54](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=534): Don't ship dead features such as a VIP badge or shop items that do nothing; players notice when purchases fail.
- 2021-01-02 AlvinBlox [4:03](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=243): Fix bugs before release; a game that will not start or has around 20 messy scripts loses players immediately.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [34:49](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2089): Good games can flop at launch and blow up later; discoverability is a real issue, so keep promoting and iterating.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [38:55](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2335): Treat account security seriously: change passwords often and protect the account, since breaches are common and can end income.
- 2021-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078): Network efficiency is hard to retrofit. Designing entity and remote systems with it in mind from the start avoids large rewrites later, similar to security.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Avoid unfair bugs and cheap server-side exploits (e.g. being crashed remotely) that frustrate players and drive them away.
- 2021-03-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-a-mesh-deformation-ocean/1122587): Deformable mesh water has no collisions and does not respond to raycasts; plan gameplay interaction around this.
- 2021-03-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437): Don't use many free Toolbox models; if you do, check them for malicious scripts first and prefer Roblox-approved or DevForum Resources models.
- 2021-03-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437): Don't optimise for performance or good practices too early as a beginner; premature optimisation is unnecessary at the start.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [1:43](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=103): Keep all parts inside an accessory unanchored, or the character gets stuck in place.
- 2021-05-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208): When animating with Moon Animator, guard the weld function with 'if v ~= Main then' or the plugin crashes.
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Don't blindly avoid FindFirstChild; avoid it only where it isn't needed. Don't avoid _G just because it's called bad practice; storing everything in it is the problem.
- 2021-05-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966): Avoid capitalised Part names like RightHand for VR hands; the tutorial reports texturing issues fixed by renaming to rightHand/leftHand.
- 2021-06-12 B Ricey [0:00](https://youtu.be/Vi2FckNJv9w?t=0): Avoid putting a separate copy of a script inside every part; editing behaviour then means editing each copy. Centralize logic instead.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [0:00](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=0): Don't include vanity buttons (info, credits) that do nothing or settings windows with 'coming soon'. Add buttons only when their content exists.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [9:11](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=551): Never kick players on death (e.g. 'please restart'). Kicking forces a full rejoin and hurts replayability; reset the player instead.
- 2021-06-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175): A custom assert wrapper that takes a message argument behaves the same as built-in assert and keeps the same eager-evaluation cost.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [26:11](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1571): Do not repeat identical free models everywhere; mix plant pots, colours and sizes so the game has its own identity.
- 2021-07-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996): Setting CameraSubject to a non-humanoid part breaks shift lock rotation; manually rotate the root part toward the camera's XZ LookVector when MouseBehavior is LockCenter.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [37:51](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2271): Pair unpopular changes with good news and explain the reason; hiding the why makes players angrier than hearing an answer they dislike.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [57:59](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3479): Prioritise bugs by whether they make players quit (purchase failures, data loss) rather than by severity alone; fix those before cosmetic issues.
- 2021-07-24 Emancyphur [2:28](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=148): Avoid simultaneous edits to the same objects in Team Create, since they can break undo/redo.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Don't use bright, flashing or excessive light sources as the only visibility solution; too-dark scenes with no light source are as harmful as too-bright ones.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Write and enforce anti-ableism rules in your game; ableist language is a bannable offence under Roblox ToS, but minor ableist jokes should be stopped too.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [35:53](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=2153): Avoid rebirth systems that trivialise progression; time-based level challenge keeps high-level players' status meaningful.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [59:15](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=3555): Banning critics on a 200-CCU game was a mistake; handle criticism constructively and hear suggestions out.
- 2021-08-27 Last Level Studios [20:35](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=1235): Tone check: he cut realism for goofiness after about 30 percent of development, saying the gritty version wasn't fun.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [11:52](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=712): Check colour contrast on frames and buttons; for audio-based games, show on-screen text for sounds, since accessibility is often skipped.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [35:34](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=2134): On mobile, consolidate buttons into one or a few to limit clickable items and avoid covering most of the screen.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [1:13:34](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4414): Don't prioritise PC for a non-combat game; mobile is over 70% of Roblox's user base, so PC should be an afterthought.
- 2021-09-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/data-storingpresenting-using-profileservice-and-replicaservice/1464984): Don't assign to DataReplicas[player] after nil-ing it; the destroy call in ListenToRelease in the tutorial code is wrong and should be replica:Destroy() before clearing the reference.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:10:16](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4216): Do not let players complete all content on day one; pace missions per day, a common season pass mistake.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:01:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3671): Do not assume players play the full season duration; compute days played per week from player profile data first.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:04:48](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=3888): Do not round intermediate values in balance formulas; round only the final result to avoid compounding error.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [5:45](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=345): Don't force irregular gameplay (e.g. specific decorations, placing or finishing in a way that hurts fun); give adequate rewards when you do ask extra effort.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [7:07](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=427): Avoid quests that create social conflict, e.g. 'join a group 5 times' encourages join-leave griefing; design so cooperation is genuine.
- 2021-10-28 Roblox Creators [20:42](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=1242): Provide quest management tools: hide all but the current quest or delete unwanted quests when you have many quests.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [11:38](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=698): Do not sacrifice clarity for theme; flavorful text is pointless if players cannot understand the instruction.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [37:28](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=2248): Avoid idioms that won't translate; check your player base's country mix before using region-specific language.
- 2021-11-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dynamic-weather-library-beta-10/1547614): Expect rain to take a while: values are modelled on real hometown data, so storms may not trigger quickly in the demo.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [17:37](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1057): Don't add community-requested features that don't fit the core loop; the pet update was poorly integrated and was judged bad.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [20:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1244): Don't over-complicate early games; successful Roblox games centre on a single fun core idea (Piggy, Bee Swarm Simulator).
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [24:10](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1450): Filter additions by whether they fit what your game is meant to be; a housing system was rejected as too far from the core game.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [4:34](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=274): Do not form a company partnership. If partnering, each person forms their own LLC/S corp and signs a memorandum of understanding defining ownership and responsibilities.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [16:48](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=1008): Answer the experience guidelines questionnaire accurately; it sets descriptors, compliance countries and age recommendation that restrict where your game is available.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [19:07](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=1147): Use policy APIs to swap restricted features per player (e.g., replace skeletons in China with robots, realistic guns with toy guns for kids) instead of maintaining separate builds.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [6:55](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=415): Keep event asset names and alt-account names different from your normal naming scheme so the event cannot be found by searching the catalog.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [17:50](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=1070): Do not sink or change the map with a countdown at an unclear location; players got lost and did not know where to look.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [4:15](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=255): Avoid overwhelming players with too much HUD text, too many buttons, or oversized close buttons; keep the layout navigable.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [5:37](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=337): Keep fonts, font weights, icons, and colors consistent with one palette and theme; inconsistency is the easiest mistake to make.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [6:18](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=378): Keep padding and alignment consistent; alignment improves readability and makes elements feel related.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [6:59](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=419): Set visual hierarchy so important buttons are sized and placed by importance; an oversized Done button or scattered elements distract.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [7:39](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=459): Don't over-decorate: ensure buttons and icons contrast with their frame so confirm buttons don't blend into the background.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [7:39](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=459): Use familiar icons that players understand without second-guessing their meaning.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [22:10](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=1330): Assume anything posted, even in DMs, can be shared out of context; be mindful of what you say to avoid future cancellation.
- 2021-12-09 Last Level Studios [8:52](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=532): VR is a small niche (~2-3% of Steam PC users, and Roblox has no PlayStation VR); size expectations accordingly before building.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Don't compare your game to other games; judge your own project on its own terms.
- 2021-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238): Don't assume balance means everything is even. Intentional differences between options are fine as long as no single option dominates.
- 2022-01-07 Last Level Studios [24:10](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1450): Don't build tech without designing the game. Windlight Bay had lots of tech (NPCs, building, dialogue) but weak game design, so it didn't find players.
- 2022-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633): CanvasDraw pixel coordinates start at (1,1) and end at (Width, Height), unlike most Roblox APIs; out-of-range access causes buffer out-of-bounds errors.
- 2022-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633): Pass whole-number or rounded pixel coordinates; RGBA values range 0 to 1.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [9:34](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=574): Avoid releasing variations of the same content every week; it caused community drop-off and team burnout. Vary content types between updates.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [34:46](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=2086): Re-engage lapsed players through cross-promotion events; players with a bad first impression will not return until you prove the game changed.
- 2022-01-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298): Don't use Humanoid.Running speed argument as WalkSpeed; it reports HumanoidRootPart velocity including vertical momentum, so falling triggers false positives.
- 2022-01-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298): A bad anti-exploit is as good as none; false positives are inevitable, so design for how often they occur and how mild the punishment is.
- 2022-02-01 GDC Festival of Gaming [42:40](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=2560): Do not judge a game by a single session. Many important issues only appear over days or weeks, so use longitudinal tests.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [5:24](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=324): Beware false positives: paper-fun may not carry over to screens or phones, so retest in the actual platform.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [13:31](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=811): Show social cues like typing dots and an 'in menu' book animation so players know if others are available to talk.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [57:16](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=3436): Avoid overextending with too many interconnected worlds; players got lost, wandered and left.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): CFrame.new() * CFrame.Angles(...) * part.CFrame rotates around global origin, causing unintended orbital motion; multiplication order matters.
- 2022-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697): Do not rely on audio uploaded by other users or groups; it will stop working after privacy changes unless the audio is under 6 seconds or from Roblox/partners.
- 2022-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697): Private audio becoming Private cannot be reverted to public after March 22, so plan replacements rather than relying on toggling privacy back.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [52:53](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3173): Commonly overlooked: social space in lobbies. Too many plots spread out felt empty; consolidating buildings into a town improved it.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [54:17](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=3257): Keep level art cohesive with core movement mechanics; a great movement system is undermined by an unrelated map layout.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [11:11](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=671): A gamepass you created is auto-owned by you, so testing shows an 'already own' prompt; test with a separate account or expect that message.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [0:01](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=1): Test for infinite-resource exploits before launch; a repeatable tap (e.g. respawning cows) can let players farm unlimited wealth early.
- 2022-04-25 Game Maker's Toolkit [7:30](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=450): Patching an exploit with a punishing spawn can create a new exploit; CD Projekt Red's fix (level 27 monster spawn) was itself farmed for heads, requiring a second patch.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356): Do not assume a report button leads to action; the author reported repeated lack of visible outcomes, so give players feedback on resolved reports.
- 2022-05-05 Roblox Creators [35:57](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=2157): Ask partners to link the game in descriptions or titles; reward influencers who give credit with in-game items or early access.
- 2022-05-05 Roblox Creators [1:02:57](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3777): Release a product you're proud of, then iterate on feedback; avoid feature creep and endless polish before launch.
- 2022-05-05 Roblox Creators [1:15:27](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4527): Avoid over-promoting 6-8 months out; viewers lose interest and expectations rise too high.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [34:10](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2050): Don't accept IP just because the licensor offers it; reps often push lesser-known titles. Pitch only IPs that genuinely interest you and fit the audience.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [40:25](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2425): Specify in the agreement which elements need approval (e.g., logos, specific models) versus which allow revisions (UI, effects); this avoids disputes.
- 2022-05-28 B Ricey [10:07](https://youtu.be/T0xqkYODIl0?t=607): Assert that the badge ID attribute exists on each tagged part so a forgotten attribute gives a clear error instead of a confusing one.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [15:01](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=901): Avoid 'tumors': systems that compete with the core loop for attention. Players split badly, one system dies, and some players quit.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [52:31](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3151): Don't spend months on maps, characters or tech without showing core gameplay. Teams that did this often threw away large amounts of work.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [28:45](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1725): Intrusive fact pop-ups that block movement felt like a nuisance and taught little. Remove them or make them non-blocking.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [32:20](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1940): Don't make it education-first. If players aren't engaged, nothing gets taught; build learning objectives into already-engaging mechanics.
- 2022-06-28 Roblox [35:12](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2112): Assume generic systems like data storage will be abused or spammed; design rate limits and abuse protection in from the start.
- 2022-06-28 Roblox [23:28](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=1408): Don't break existing experiences or avatars with surprise changes; treat APIs as permanent and plan changes years ahead.
- 2022-07-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779): Unanchor every BasePart in an NPC model, otherwise anchored parts stop it from walking.
- 2022-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578): Avoid building for a niche that excludes most of its own community; do not purposely limit scalability of the audience.
- 2022-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578): Do not raise gamepass prices expecting player-side inflation; the author says gamepass prices have hardly moved because only developers, not players, get richer.
- 2022-08-30 GameMakers [27:12](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1632): Don't skip ROI estimates and neglect low-hanging fruit in favour of big features; that gives competitors a significant edge.
- 2022-08-30 GameMakers [27:54](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1674): Play top-grossing games critically before prototyping; most design problems already have battle-tested solutions.
- 2022-09-23 B Ricey [16:33](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=993): Comment why you are doing something, not what the code does; skip comments when a solo codebase is self-explanatory.
- 2022-09-23 GnomeCode [1:22](https://youtu.be/aFKaY-PmR08?t=82): Don't edit other people's scripts until you understand the fundamentals (if statements, functions, properties, variables); otherwise debugging them becomes very hard.
- 2022-11-17 Roblox Creators [0:43](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=43): Pay-to-win items frustrate players and make them stop playing, especially older players who feel dunked on by a purchased weapon.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [21:54](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=1314): Older audiences notice quality issues (blurry textures, clunky movement, low FPS); prioritise polish and performance.
- 2022-12-01 Roblox Creators [8:39](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=519): Don't mistake it for an audience-only problem: Roblox's kid-focused branding and bright cartoon visuals signal 'kids' games' to older players.
- 2022-12-10 SimTek Game Development [4:52](https://youtu.be/mrmB0hQGCXE?t=292): Don't hard-code Y positions for terrain-placed objects; terrain height changes and hard-coded values break silently.
- 2023-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/movement-system/2135683): Author warns this tutorial's approach is outdated and the scripts are not final; polish them before using in a real game.
- 2023-01-19 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0): Don't let pay-to-win creep in; free players leaving causes paid players to churn too, leaving a dead game with zero concurrency.
- 2023-01-19 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0): If a monetization bug cheats paying players, detect affected purchases in data and notify players with an apology and fix.
- 2023-01-19 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0): Tune prices and balance after launch: lower overpriced items and add free content to recover trust after over-monetizing.
- 2023-01-26 Roblox Creators [5:46](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=346): Watch for loops of barely differentiated content; if players feel overwhelmed or the store is bloated, step back and check if the core loop is still good.
- 2023-01-28 SimTek Game Development [3:12](https://youtu.be/tjwI4b2ZRZk?t=192): Delete the setup emote chat hook block in the copied Animate script; it causes output errors on NPCs because it is player-specific.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [1:57](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=117): Do not break Roblox rules: no copying others' work, no gambling with Roblox items, no hacking, or the game is likely to be removed.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Do not assume 'royalty free' or 'copyright free' labels grant usage rights; always check the actual license, since these phrases are not legally binding alone.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Do not use Creative Commons NC (non-commercial) licensed tracks on Roblox, because any game with premium payouts or paid content counts as commercial.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Do not rely on unauthorized uploaded MP3s; the author notes most user-uploaded audio is stolen or made private, and stolen music risks DMCA takedowns.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [22:41](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1361): Avoid placing buttons on the left movement side of mobile screens, since pressing them stops character movement.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [32:04](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1924): Remember live chat covers the top of the mobile screen; don't rely on upper-screen space when designing HUD.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [24:13](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1453): Use color only to carry meaning; random rainbow buttons add noise and confuse players.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [36:58](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=2218): Allow for longer translated text (e.g., German) in UI boxes so localized strings don't become illegible.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [2:22](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=142): Don't build portrait mode for a new game; players expect landscape on mobile. Only consider it for very simple games or later, after a hit.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [3:02](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=182): Don't stylize UI so heavily that players can't tell what it does; clarity matters more than looks.
- 2023-03-16 Roblox Creators [1:27](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=87): Avoid spreading the world too far from where gameplay actually happens; designers get carried away building and drift away from the core mechanics.
- 2023-04-12 Mulch [8:58](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=538): Ads returned about 34k net on 100k Robux spend here. Prove retention and monetisation before spending on ads.
- 2023-04-14 B Ricey [0:43](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=43): Don't scale AI behaviour with isolated if-statements; model behaviours as explicit states and transitions in a diagram first.
- 2023-04-27 Roblox Creators [2:07](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=127): Avoid overhyping; high prerelease expectations make an MVP-then-iterate release harder to pull off.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [49:37](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2977): Avoid flashing lights, especially red, and large screen-filling flashes; avoid autoplay trailers with fast flashing content that users cannot turn off.
- 2023-05-18 GnomeCode [15:52](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=952): Avoid copy-pasting repeated script blocks; use loops and functions instead. Early repeated if-blocks over 1000 lines were a sign of learning, but refactor early.
- 2023-05-18 GnomeCode [8:51](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=531): Don't leave large empty spaces in a map; more stuff does not make a map better. Plan where the player goes before adding content.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [10:32](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=632): Don't rely on hover-only tooltips. Show the same info on focus/select so mouse, keyboard, and controller users get it too, and make button states and actions clear.
- 2023-06-01 Roblox Creators [0:48](https://youtu.be/yn4V-fBdUm8?t=48): Monitor developer products for failures; broken products between time windows cause compensation issues.
- 2023-06-19 SmartyRBX [1:37](https://youtu.be/74VNexodi10?t=97): Game development needs real skills in Studio, scripting, building and game design; it is not the easiest path.
- 2023-06-22 Suphi Kaner [5:05](https://youtu.be/ARPfIt16sXI?t=305): Misconception: UpdateAsync is not inherently more secure than SetAsync; it only helps when you read and modify the previous value.
- 2023-08-03 Amora [9:23](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=563): Enable Game Settings > Security > Studio Access to API Services, or Studio cannot access DataStores and saving will fail.
- 2023-08-03 Amora [2:26](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=146): Name the leaderstats folder exactly 'leaderstats' in lowercase, or the leaderboard will not display it.
- 2023-08-18 SimTek Game Development [3:27](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=207): Creating a gamepass makes you own it, so delete it from your inventory before testing the purchase flow.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Do not assume spending Robux alone guarantees success; ads cannot replace an engaging, original game.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [3:18](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=198): Make the most important element the brightest, most eye-catching thing on screen so players look at it first.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [5:21](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=321): Group related items with close proximity and size differences so players instantly see which items belong together.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [5:37](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=337): Limit the UI color palette to a handful of theme-fitting colors; too many colors makes the UI noisy and hard to scan.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [8:21](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=501): Research existing genre and device conventions before designing controls, since players bring habits from other games.
- 2023-08-29 janisjanis01 [0:00](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=0): Don't expect a 5-day game to go viral; a rushed build with no experience rarely reaches the front page.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Don't build features before planning; unused systems (e.g. a shop for weapons not yet designed) get deleted later and waste work.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [44:11](https://youtu.be/8R136Y_T5G4?t=2651): New UGC creators will only be allowed to make limiteds at launch; ID verification and a strike system are required for Marketplace creation.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [30:08](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1808): Decline brands that come for the wrong reason or do not fit the community; a logo slap or opportunistic moment does not work.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [30:36](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1836): Don't force players through portals in gameplay (e.g. racing game where a teleport blocks the racer); Roblox monitors implementations and underpays gamed placement.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [30:46](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1846): Don't mislead users into accidentally teleporting; Roblox is building intentional-teleport confirmation.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-and-manage-subscriptions-within-your-experience/2600664): Ensure subscription descriptions clearly state benefits; benefits not delivered due to unclear descriptions or removing the subscription may be refunded.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-and-manage-subscriptions-within-your-experience/2600664): Subscriptions are not a fit for every experience; cancel or delete subscriptions that are not gaining traction.
- 2023-10-07 CORSO [1:40](https://youtu.be/H9MoA94zKdc?t=100): Avoid making rarity-gated power where top-tier units make lower tiers useless; strategy depth suffers. Balance towers to similar strength, as BTD6 does with 23 towers.
- 2023-10-15 SmartyRBX [0:37](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=37): Don't start with a huge dream game as a first project; the speaker's large 'camping simulator' project with a huge map was never finished.
- 2023-10-25 TheDankDeveloper [1:30](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=90): Anchor placed objects and map parts; unanchored towers and props fall or collide unexpectedly during testing.
- 2023-11-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585): Do not put scripts directly under StarterGui; wrap local scripts in a ScreenGui.
- 2023-11-17 TheDankDeveloper [0:00](https://youtu.be/fqMj_b2m-Ik?t=0): Don't clone or republish other people's games; the speaker warns against downloading and copying uncopylocked games.
- 2023-11-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983): Don't call SetAsync on a shared key from many servers without care; the author suggests UpdateAsync for safer host-data writes.
- 2023-12-12 TheDankDeveloper [2:46](https://youtu.be/1jRaSYhrEcU?t=166): Don't leave pet/egg purchase or pet-count limits unbounded; the speaker found infinite pets by spamming the egg button, causing lag and pets flying off-map.
- 2023-12-17 Last Level Studios [6:07](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=367): Keep the game's identity focused on the core experience rather than polishing niche details or visuals; SCR kept blocky rails and 2D ballast by choice.
- 2023-12-25 VuukStudios [7:35](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=455): Expect to get scammed early on; he says it is part of the process to reach paid-on-completion work.
- 2023-12-25 VuukStudios [10:49](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=649): Do not take percentage-of-game deals from unproven developers early; he says these often never pay out.
- 2023-12-30 ItsZyonic [7:33](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=453): Stress-test extreme scale early: max-scaled characters and huge baseplates caused rendering failures and game pauses in Studio.
- 2024-01-23 Crusherfire [12:20](https://youtu.be/xz_G2n1Q8-U?t=740): Don't rely on CharacterRemoving or DescendantRemoving to read the instance's parent; under deferred mode it may already be nil by the time the callback runs.
- 2024-02-11 Crusherfire [12:40](https://youtu.be/28z_064N-lc?t=760): Don't store local scripts in ServerScriptService or server scripts in StarterPlayerScripts; these are misuses of the services.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [12:54](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=774): Don't leave parts unanchored by default; unanchored physics parts cause lag and chaos with more players.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [3:08](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=188): Vary repetitive building layouts and reduce clutter; visual variety looks more polished.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [2:27](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=147): Make tool visuals match their function; a match looked like dynamite and a punch looked like a throw, confusing players.
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [5:11](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=311): Do not spam ads everywhere; irrational ad placement annoys players and hurts retention.
- 2024-02-21 Crusherfire [17:48](https://youtu.be/9VHThbipoFs?t=1068): Don't mutate self inside methods that return values; return a new object instead (const correctness) to avoid aliasing bugs where two variables share one table.
- 2024-02-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111): Do not rely on tags alone; Discovery weighs engagement (play-through, session length, retention, friend invites) and monetisation, not just metadata.
- 2024-03-08 janisjanis01 [0:00](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=0): A game with many moving parts driven by physics constraints (e.g. Prismatic constraints) lags the server. Avoid physics-driven movement for many platforms.
- 2024-03-14 1Coal [3:36](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=216): Cut shop items players won't buy and re-price the rest; a bloated shop dilutes purchases.
- 2024-03-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/mass-uncopylocked-35-free-games-and-projects/2880269): Sounds and animations do not carry over from uncopylocked places; re-upload them under your own account before expecting them to work.
- 2024-03-25 PolarBear [0:24](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=24): Don't sponsor a game that isn't polished; the creator admits a better game before sponsoring would have helped. Fix core experience first.
- 2024-04-14 janisjanis01 [0:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=0): Check the console for errors early; a single line of code caused 51,000 errors in this game.
- 2024-05-10 ByteBlox [3:09](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=189): Uncleaned particles or effects added on character respawn cause steady lag and eventual crashes; delete old effects on reset.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/viewportframe-masking/2964839): Decals, Textures, mesh textures and part transparency do not inherit 0-alpha mesh masking; use SurfaceAppearance instead if you need masking behaviour.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [13:38](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=818): Test scripts in Studio after each change to confirm the code still works before moving on.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [13:38](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=818): Script descriptions that only say where to put code are unhelpful; explain why the code works and design decisions.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [0:58](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=58): Developer accounts must be verified by joining the Social Seed Roblox group and pasting your profile number; the speaker frames this as required setup.
- 2024-06-03 PineappleDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/BOMfj0bSjf0?t=0): This simple script lacks session locking and retries; for production games use a library like ProfileStore to avoid data loss.
- 2024-06-07 ByteBlox [1:22](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=82): A while true loop with no yield will exhaust execution time and hang the game; games cannot run true infinity, so simulate it instead.
- 2024-06-07 ByteBlox [18:33](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=1113): Ensure triggers have CanCollide off and transparency off so they do not block the player while remaining invisible when intended.
- 2024-06-16 ItsZyonic [7:39](https://youtu.be/b7qEitHbhUA?t=459): Unverified third-party promo services (paid streamer/Discord outreach, fake-view TikTok promos) produced almost no results; don't rely on them.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [13:20](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=800): Parenting a cloned item to the wrong container or forgetting to connect the button's Activated event on every clone causes bugs; verify each step.
- 2024-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-ban-api-and-alt-account-detection/3039740): Write experience rules that follow Community Standards and apply them fairly. Rules excluding users by protected traits violate policy, and arbitrary targeting is not allowed.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [1:02](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=62): Test for duplication exploits; a luck potion once gave infinite best pets. Fix bugs found by players quickly.
- 2024-07-16 TheDankDeveloper [8:57](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=537): Don't assume a sponsored ad campaign will pay back; the creator spent about 3,135 Robux and recovered only 182 Robux from the game.
- 2024-07-19 Mulch [6:51](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=411): Check that the respawn/reset popup does not reappear after each death; a bug here can make the game unplayable.
- 2024-07-23 VuukStudios [3:06](https://youtu.be/NfXvHlAdIsI?t=186): Do not accept percentage-only deals from unproven teams; many never release, leaving you with unpaid work.
- 2024-07-30 ItsZyonic [2:14](https://youtu.be/Cd6mpD4doGs?t=134): Don't rely on a low-effort cash grab with gamepass spam; the creator's version earned 28 Robux from 3,000 Robux of sponsored ads over 72 hours.
- 2024-08-04 PineappleDev [3:05](https://youtu.be/b5PR-4hw2co?t=185): Avoid AFK-farming or pure gambling designs to chase Premium payouts; the speaker notes these are common but risky for long-term player quality.
- 2024-08-07 Roblox Creators [24:57](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1497): Before a UGC item event, prepare for exploiters: rename remote events and place bait coins in unreachable spots so cheaters auto-ban.
- 2024-08-07 Roblox Creators [27:00](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1620): UGC events bring many users who only collect items and do not spend, lowering long-term monetisation and recommendation metrics afterwards.
- 2024-08-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-when-to-use-different-cases-in-luau-scripting/3113987): Keep naming consistent across the whole project; if the team picks camelCase for variables, stick to it. In existing messy code, follow the existing style rather than refactoring it all.
- 2024-08-12 GameMakers [55:36](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3336): Do not assume rolling out cosmetics creates demand; they need a social universe where players care about status and each other.
- 2024-08-18 janisjanis01 [7:12](https://youtu.be/RECJIE0_CzE?t=432): Don't chase quick greed at the expense of player experience; it damages your reputation for future games.
- 2024-09-05 CORSO [10:33](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=633): Admin/cheat-like tools can destroy the core experience; the developer found a large share of players quit right after gaining admin commands, so gate them carefully.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [7:26](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=446): Don't assume a sale is final: items can have zero sales at launch and trend weeks later; keep listings up and promote them.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [35:42](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=2142): Assume generated code is not free of IP risk without checking; per the speaker, Roblox ingests only opted-in code and shreds it into tokens, so content with copyright headers is excluded.
- 2024-10-12 SimTek Game Development [6:01](https://youtu.be/i2_7zT36Jeg?t=361): Use a debounce on mode switching during testing (5s), but use a long cooldown in production (~300s, as in WoW) so players can't rapidly toggle to take shots.
- 2024-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replica-server-to-client-state-replication-module/3216980): Don't rely on the old ReplicaService names; Replica renamed members (use Replica.Maid:Add instead of AddCleanupTask, SetParent after construction instead of Parent arg).
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [42:24](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2544): Avoid UA-vs-product blame games; set expectations before soft launch (expected low LTV, retention, crash rate by market and device) so bad news is not a surprise.
- 2024-10-27 janisjanis01 [3:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=189): Building with a plugin that generates the game drew player backlash and the game died off; avoid relying on tools players dislike.
- 2024-10-27 janisjanis01 [6:41](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=401): Shipping a buggy update (broken data saving) hurt growth; test data handling before releasing a holiday update.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [13:22](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=802): Touched events on HumanoidRootPart miss by a small gap; add an invisible, non-collidable buffer part slightly larger than the wall to fire the trigger earlier.
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [24:10](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=1450): Verify that pet models are actually inserted into the game after import; the speaker forgot them and only noticed when testing eggs.
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [24:53](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=1493): Hoverboard physics were hard to get right; expect levitation and movement bugs and test point-to-point traversal before release.
- 2024-11-13 Roblox Creators [2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122): Prevent exploits: let each invitee reward a referrer only once, and track who has already been invited.
- 2024-11-13 Roblox Creators [2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122): Add a cooldown before the same player can submit another referral to block repeated invite-link abuse.
- 2024-11-13 Roblox Creators [2:02](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=122): Monitor for groups of players repeatedly inviting each other; ban abusers or nullify their rewards.
- 2024-11-17 ByteBlox [26:54](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=1614): Don't treat mobile as an afterthought; developers who build for PC first get bad mobile experiences, while top games cater to mobile heavily.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [0:22](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=22): Check progression gating: a single missing line let players skip areas and collect rewards, so test that areas must be completed in order.
- 2024-11-20 ByteBlox [16:06](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=966): Do not gate basic movement or camera behind purchases. The creator says this breaks Roblox ToS and risks account suspension.
- 2024-11-28 Suphi Kaner [39:31](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=2371): Never put infinite loops in the script or initialize stages; they block all later stages. Only loop inside the start stage.
- 2024-12-01 janisjanis01 [13:08](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=788): Re-test newly configured drops and eggs after setup; a single missed code line and un-anchored objects broke them.
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [27:17](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=1637): Leaving and rejoining a busy public server put the creator in a queue and into a broken update state; server stability during launches matters to players.
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [30:27](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=1827): Shiny relic hunts and hidden collectibles frustrated players when hard to find; the creator repeatedly couldn't locate them, so make collectibles discoverable.
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [32:28](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=1948): The creator reports a rare-pet exploit (luck boosts plus Titanic pets) prompted a reset; balance rare-drop odds before release.
- 2024-12-01 Mulch [34:54](https://youtu.be/tdnhCXx3srA?t=2094): Rarity labels inconsistent with actual drop rates (e.g. a 95% pet appearing more often than a 90% one) erode player trust; verify displayed odds.
- 2024-12-15 janisjanis01 [12:58](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=778): Test every developer product purchase in Studio; a wrong product ID granted a potion instead of eggs and had to be fixed.
- 2025-01-15 CallMeDogey [5:58](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=358): Don't rely on one thumbnail or upload without checking moderation; a rejected image got the thumbnail removed and a one-day ban.
- 2025-01-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865): Prior spherical-gravity attempts looked janky because they were never properly polished; polish the feel (movement, lighting, visuals) rather than just shipping the mechanic.
- 2025-01-22 ByteBlox [2:04](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=124): Players rarely see clipping or out-of-bounds junk, but unpolished hidden scaffolding is normal; don't assume a clean solution exists, pick the simplest working method.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [18:56](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=1136): Keep scripts outside the object folder they manage; a script inside the berries folder was destroyed when the folder was cleared.
- 2025-02-09 Daiorg [2:17](https://youtu.be/nutv8RT5UrQ?t=137): Don't rely on monetisation-heavy design with little gameplay; the speaker criticises RNG and cash-grab games for lacking fun, which he says hurts long-term quality.
- 2025-02-18 GnomeCode [4:19](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=259): Scattering teleport logic across many functions causes mistakes; centralise all teleport-to-room logic in one function so it resets state (e.g. walk speed) consistently.
- 2025-03-02 SmartyRBX [23:49](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1429): Don't make your dream or complex game as your first project; long builds often lose motivation and fail to match players' tastes.
- 2025-03-02 SmartyRBX [26:40](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1600): Avoid forming a group and managing a team before you have a playable game; the speaker's group lost years to this.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [2:29](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=149): Give models and parts a PrimaryPart before scripting moves on them; trees without a PrimaryPart broke the tree-moving script.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [4:29](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=269): Anchor parts when placing map assets; unanchored parts fall or drift and must be fixed later.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [7:11](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=431): Check item orientation in-game; gloves were rendered backwards on join and had to be fixed late.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [9:36](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=576): Do not sink time into custom character models before confirming they function; a glitchy custom rig wasted work.
- 2025-03-22 AlvinBlox [8:31](https://youtu.be/dtRGRx-2WNg?t=511): Vet clients and buyers, check reputations, and secure clear written terms before committing to avoid scams.
- 2025-03-22 CallMeDogey [3:50](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=230): Don't pay an editor upfront without vetting; the creator lost $70 to a scammer and delayed the upload by over a month.
- 2025-03-23 SmartyRBX [1:17](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=77): Don't spend months building a passion project with custom systems and handcrafted assets before validating demand; it often gets 0-2 players.
- 2025-03-30 SmartyRBX [0:00](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=0): Don't spend weeks building a game based only on what you find fun; validate demand first, since unplayed polished games are the common failure.
- 2025-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441): Don't assume larger experiences win: signals are per-user averages, so small experiences with highly engaged users are not disadvantaged.
- 2025-04-04 AlvinBlox [5:55](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=355): Don't cram in many systems (battle, clan, pets, story) at once; new players get confused and leave.
- 2025-04-06 Cumin [8:39](https://youtu.be/YmAwFbqUA0U?t=519): Paying developers poorly and crunching them damages publisher reputation; reviewer says GameFam is widely disliked for this despite heavy monetisation.
- 2025-04-17 Roblox Creators [7:04](https://youtu.be/Zb1BJow0NV4?t=424): Don't assume you can predict sales; no one knows what will sell. Make what you like, and expect some items to sell slowly.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [35:26](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=2126): Don't use buffers as a default optimization; the speaker says the likelihood of needing them is low unless throughput is high.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [3:18](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=198): Don't spend Robux on ads to fix a weak game; ad spikes without retention burn the budget and return players to zero.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [3:45](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=225): Don't spend Robux on ads to fix a weak game; ad spikes without retention burn the budget and players drop back to zero.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [2:31](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=151): Don't build purely because you think it will make money; games made for love of play perform better.
- 2025-04-30 PineappleDev [3:32](https://youtu.be/S43gUJTZWUk?t=212): Reskinning a successful game into a sequel with the same mechanics did not carry over the original's success; the creator concluded viral hits are not guaranteed to repeat.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [11:25](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=685): Don't rely on Roblox physics for core gameplay hits without checking that collisions register correctly; hits that look like misses cause negative reviews.
- 2025-05-11 Cumin [5:47](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=347): Avoid level-locked progression with huge gaps and repeating the same quest template; the speaker says it makes players grind one dungeon for hours.
- 2025-05-11 janisjanis01 [6:21](https://youtu.be/sM9btPAvb28?t=381): Don't rely on a joke or reverse-psychology channel banner to drive subscribes; it did not convert (5 subs from 6,000 views).
- 2025-05-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/expanding-how-commerce-can-help-you-earn-and-grow-your-communities/3651181): Ensure all listed products comply with Roblox Commerce Standards, Community Standards and Advertising Standards to avoid moderation issues.
- 2025-05-25 GnomeCode [12:02](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=722): When copy-pasting setup functions, check you call the right one; a wrong call silently hides the prompt.
- 2025-05-28 Arow [0:45](https://youtu.be/JeBDqH7INjY?t=45): Ask the client many questions before starting so the order is specified correctly.
- 2025-05-30 Perstile [0:40](https://youtu.be/ET3fRqaFmFc?t=40): Don't assume 1 Robux per visit; creator says that rule is false, with this game earning far more per visit from purchases.
- 2025-06-01 janisjanis01 [4:25](https://youtu.be/B409dMcW-pw?t=265): Make sure every seed has a matching crop model before testing, or planting a seed breaks gameplay.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [15:33](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=933): Don't mistake challenge for cheap, unfair design; hard games need fair, readable mechanics and clear failure causes.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [7:45](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=465): Test selling after changes; a missing function silently stopped gold from being credited. Verify each economy path end-to-end before release.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [13:16](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=796): Running physics on the server made gameplay look like slow motion. Keep physics feel and simulation ownership in mind before polishing content.
- 2025-06-22 SmartyRBX [20:23](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=1223): Set firm commission boundaries and terms of service; cap revisions to avoid clients stretching a job indefinitely.
- 2025-06-23 SmartyRBX [19:46](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=1186): Do not start with a complex passion project; the speaker's complex games failed because they were not built up to gradually.
- 2025-06-24 SimTek Game Development [22:03](https://youtu.be/4tGM1Ac5fcI?t=1323): Don't leave a follow state without an exit condition; add a timeout or distance check, since the NPC otherwise keeps following through death and respawn.
- 2025-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628): Do not encourage existing players to create alt accounts, and avoid bots, automation, or manipulated teleports; these lead to forfeited rewards or account termination.
- 2025-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628): Do not rely on long, passive or AFK sessions; under EBP these were rewarded but Creator Rewards removes that advantage, so experiences with unusually long sessions may earn less.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [9:57](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=597): Don't upload UGC to the catalog and hope it sells; research demand first, since the catalog is now open to anyone.
- 2025-07-07 SimTek Game Development [55:45](https://youtu.be/DcLba0yVLHs?t=3345): Avoid mixing follow and attack behaviour: an NPC following one target while attacking another produces odd facing results, so clear the follow flag on attack.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [37:39](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=2259): Moving objects in object mode shifts their origin. Fix it by right-clicking to cancel, then applying location with Ctrl+A.
- 2025-07-17 Roblox [29:41](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=1781): Expect bad actors on any platform at scale; safety is never done, so plan for continuous iteration against bypasses like coded speech.
- 2025-07-18 #7Phantom [11:16](https://youtu.be/xjZXsHxKerE?t=676): Don't upload on a brand-new TikTok account's first day; spend it engaging (follow, like, favorite) to avoid being shadowbanned as a bot.
- 2025-07-21 SmartyRBX [3:45](https://youtu.be/exOJsFcer1Q?t=225): Don't post commission or dev work once and forget it; show actual images and reply to big accounts in the community to build reach.
- 2025-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678): Eligibility list refreshes every Monday; if still missing after a week, contact the Roblox team. Ads are not game-specific and sound settings are respected.
- 2025-08-02 BloxxedDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/od95x-afmBU?t=0): Don't import Toolbox models with low ratings or unverified creators without reviewing them; the video's imports broke spawns, shift lock, camera and lag.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): Actors must be parented to an object, and workers placed under StarterCharacterScripts or the character, or they will never run.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): EditableImage coordinates start at (0,0), so the bottom-right pixel is (width-1, height-1), not (width, height).
- 2025-08-04 waveshine [6:36](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=396): Do not blame players or the algorithm for a flop; taking full responsibility gives you control over the outcome.
- 2025-08-04 waveshine [1:35](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=95): Do not rely on a single account or copied open-source projects; account termination can wipe out all progress (he lost about 100,000 Robux of progress).
- 2025-08-08 bubDEV [1:00](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=60): Avoid crowded clone themes; the creator abandoned Steal Anime Characters because many identical anime-stealing games appeared.
- 2025-08-08 Xaiyne [14:39](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=879): Don't assume a pending review means a ban; moderation queues can exceed 5 hours, so submit early and wait.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [3:14](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=194): Don't use the Roblox name or brand in UGC items; Roblox doesn't allow it. Rename the text instead.
- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [3:21](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=201): Don't expect early items to succeed; Rush's first low-quality items drew hate, but the instant feedback showed what worked. Iterate fast.
- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [5:11](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=311): Don't judge items only against your own catalog. Compare against the marketplace quality bar, which keeps rising, and against a hero creator's work.
- 2025-08-24 janisjanis01 [7:53](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=473): Check that each upload is set to Public. Two videos were left Unlisted and got almost no views; verify visibility right after publishing.
- 2025-08-24 janisjanis01 [9:47](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=587): Don't rely on a single 'sure-hit' idea. A video the creator was confident in got 8 views; hits are unpredictable, so keep posting.
- 2025-08-24 janisjanis01 [2:24](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=144): Keep audio levels normal when editing; the creator noticed the volume was too loud only after publishing.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [17:45](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1065): Rewards must be deterministic and clearly described; randomized rewards violate compliance (tickets for a later random draw are allowed).
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [18:26](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1106): Test rewarded ad flows in Studio before release to catch compliance issues; moderation reacts to user reports and reviews new implementations.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [12:03](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=723): Spamming ads makes players leave; if exposure is limited, you may have room to raise ad load and test the trade-off.
- 2025-08-31 Daiorg [12:03](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=723): Ship social features like player-hosted parties only with moderation in place; Meep City's unmoderated parties caused safety issues and a decline.
- 2025-08-31 Daiorg [5:34](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=334): Cloning existing video games (e.g. Murder Mystery from Garry's Mod) can drive early growth but hurts the platform's reputation with gamers.
- 2025-09-01 Roox4 [9:34](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=574): Don't over-simulate: the solution was an enemy-scale problem, not a car simulation. Ask what the object actually needs to do before modelling full systems.
- 2025-09-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628): Don't use infinite raycast length for suspension; it applies huge forces when airborne and launches the car.
- 2025-09-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628): Don't keep wheels collidable if they are only visual; they will hit the floor and make the car bounce incorrectly.
- 2025-09-06 Cumin [0:00](https://youtu.be/Jf2RvsWMPes?t=0): Copying a viral low-effort meta verbatim or making it lazier is the pattern the speaker describes; these metas saturate and fade.
- 2025-09-07 janisjanis01 [13:05](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=785): Bugs such as merged plots can wipe out player traction; check and fix game-breaking bugs quickly after updates.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Do not assume compiler-level optimizations are free; code at optimize level 2 (inlining) is harder to debug, so use -g2 or lower-optimize builds when debugging.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [2:57](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=177): Don't use third-party IP without permission: violations can get your whole experience removed via DMCA takedown, not just the infringing part.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:37](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=577): Repeat IP infringement reports can lead to permanent loss of your entire Roblox account.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [11:00](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=660): IP enforcement depends on rights holders reporting; similar content staying up does not mean it is allowed.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [14:24](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=864): File a counter notice only if a takedown was truly mistaken; content restores after 10-14 business days if the reporter shows no lawsuit.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [24:40](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=1480): If the IP scanner flags your experience, you have 7 days to dispute or remove the IP, or the license activates automatically.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [26:02](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=1562): Fix content standards deviations within 10 business days or the IP holder may terminate the license and take down the experience.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [34:22](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=2062): AI-generated player items are not persisted between sessions as of this talk; plan storage yourself if items must return tomorrow.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [29:58](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=1798): Avoid exclusive units in hyper-casual games; they can hurt longevity and engagement, while sellable items in trading games add perceived value.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [27:27](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1647): Avoid assuming device from capabilities: a PC can have touch, and a console may have keyboard and mouse attached. Don't hard-code device-specific control schemes.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [3:46](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=226): Do not write bespoke keyboard-only code; hotkeys that do not map to gamepad or touch break on other devices. Test portrait mode layouts, which often collapse.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:34](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=574): Don't rely on custom cheat detection alone; cheat-vs-defence cycles never end. Enable server authority so authority-based cheats fail regardless of method.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [1:36](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=96): Avoid one-sided matches; getting stomped by pros is a bad onboarding experience, so use matchmaking to balance players.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [17:45](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=1065): Avoid chasing short-term trends; games that copy trend-of-the-week updates become hollow. Build trust by releasing what you think players will want long-term.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [34:07](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=2047): Avoid bloat: when most of the game is irrelevant to new players, they get rushed to late content and get overwhelmed and leave.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [12:55](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=775): Get contracts with all collaborators (IP ownership, contractor vs. revshare vs. owner status) before seeking a sale; messy arrangements hurt deals.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [13:38](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=818): Sell while a game is established and stable; brand-new games are valued much lower and declining games attract few buyers.
- 2025-09-26 Erthlord [3:28](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=208): Buying a pre-made game and reskinning it still left bugs and UI problems; the hired scripter had not fixed them by mid-week.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [16:07](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=967): Consider segmenting data (new vs veteran, player types); Adopt Me's trade-delay test looked negative only because bots/alts were concentrated in control.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [25:32](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=1532): Test even obvious changes: popups on locked items can drive new players away, so measure retention alongside revenue.
