- 2025-10-17 Duckable [0:29](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=29): Broken, unplayable or private submissions get eliminated immediately; make sure your game is public and runs before submitting.
- 2025-10-25 Creators on the Blox [1:23:49](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=5029): Do not rely on AI auto-translation for game text; it overrode manual Spanish translations and mishandles nuance, so use manual translators.
- 2025-10-25 Creators on the Blox [1:29:59](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=5399): Do not rely on AI moderation alone; automod misses contractions and evasion like 'who are' spellings, which need human nuance.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [22:29](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1349): Myth: games are quarantined after weak early days. Algorithm never permanently removes a game; revived games with new plays can re-enter home recommendations.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): Keep scope small and work quickly; no developer has a 100% hit rate, so stay flexible and follow what is fun.
- 2025-11-02 janisjanis01 [14:23](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=863): Do not wait for player data inside PlayerRemoving handlers; a wait there left the plot system stuck and kicked over 700 players.
- 2025-11-02 janisjanis01 [14:23](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=863): Stabilise the game before buying ads; bugs wasted the Robux spent on campaigns.
- 2025-11-04 Possible Panda [17:57](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=1077): The most common Fusion error is a stray or extra comma, or forgetting to return the instance from the component function.
- 2025-11-05 Cumin [8:10](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=490): Speaker's ethics framing: heavy monetization aimed at children draws backlash; avoid aggressive FOMO and gotcha mechanics if targeting young players.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [16:50](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=1010): Avoid low-effort template clones of a popular game; some studios reskin one template and get multiple games with thousands of CCU.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Do not act on results that swing between significant and not significant in early days; fluctuations settle only as enrolled traffic grows.
- 2025-11-09 BrawlDev [6:10](https://youtu.be/YGmO7BXSwVg?t=370): Heavy-spend developer products can be spammed and break the server; he had to disable the nuke product mid-session because the game became unplayable.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [54:52](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=3292): Do not build content you dislike; Zeal says his burnout came from working on features he did not like, and it grows the more you ship.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [30:26](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=1826): Avoid making a game too similar to a hit like Doors; Zeal regrets that Pressure felt too close to it and got boring two years later.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [24:59](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=1499): Do not accept rushed event deadlines blindly; Zeal says the Graveyard event was rushed, too ambitious, and he is still not proud of it.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): Do not add rewarded ads to experiences with free-form user creation or AI interaction. They are ineligible.
- 2025-11-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-on-verified-badge-requirements-for-experiences/4070915): Keep your display name identical to the one you had when first granted the badge; changing it removes eligibility until reverted and Support is contacted.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [1:04:38](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=3878): Expect games and creators to drop out late; RB Battles lost multiple integrations, so plan replacements and tell partners a project can take up to a year.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [22:33](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=1353): Don't assume a partner will deliver just because they agreed. Working one-on-one with each game and tracking deadlines was needed; some devs went quiet for months.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [19:08](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=1148): Don't rely on one channel for a niche audience; early growth came from community-driven intros and covers that others adopted.
- 2025-11-23 Cumin [8:58](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=538): Assuming fans will explain your game via wikis is a mistake; poor communication, not complexity, is the problem.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856): Check server max player size before launch; forgetting to set it to 6 broke the rollout.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [14:58](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=898): Despawn or respawn resource objects like trees; otherwise players run out of resources on some servers.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [13:51](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=831): Config snapshots are static by default; listen for update events and reload them manually or changes won't apply.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [14:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=872): Don't peek at experiment results after 24 hours; novelty effects mislead. Wait for the planned duration.
- 2025-12-03 Lnor Roblox [1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91): Scanning all world objects every second to find players burned ~30% of server resources. Use event-driven detection or cached lists instead.
- 2025-12-03 {EISABOOS} "RBX" [4:53](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=293): Avoid random games, no story or goal, long boring intros, clickbait titles that don't deliver, monthly uploads, no thumbnail strategy, ignoring viewers.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Do not publish a Server Authority-enabled game; player clients lack support and the game will not work correctly. Studio Beta changes only apply to local Studio play.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Avoid relying on BasePart.Touched/TouchEnded and InputAction.StateChanged for gameplay; use GetTouchingParts() and InputAction:GetState() instead, since rollbacks can fire them inaccurately.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185): Don't expect zero mispredictions; design so corrections are small and imperceptible, since mispredictions are normal.
- 2025-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-requirements-to-publish-and-update-public-experiences/4143953): Eligibility also applies to updates of existing public experiences and Open Cloud publishing, so plan for it before pushing live changes.
- 2025-12-12 Poke [2:44](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=164): Don't rely on ads alone: after the ad ended, player count dropped to zero, so paid traffic must be backed by a game players return to.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [1:03:00](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=3780): Be careful changing a successful game; he is scared to alter things like music because the game may be succeeding despite or because of its current state.
- 2025-12-24 1_Bruce [8:25](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=505): Avoid over-restraint in monetisation: Energy Incremental earned about a third of the revenue of a similar-visit game with lighter monetisation.
- 2025-12-25 Creators on the Blox [20:33](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=1233): Leaving a group that owns your game can cost you ownership permanently; keep ownership of published games safe.
- 2026-00-00 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026): Expect copycat accusations if you clone a top game; the article notes Fish It! openly credited Fisch and Fishing Simulator in its description.
- 2026-00-00 GosuGamers [page](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items): Make one-shot mission actions clearly communicated; the article notes players fail Liz's quest by wasting a photo and must restart the game, a frustrating punishment.
- 2026-01-23 Erthlord [14:45](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=885): Twitter/X posts with memes and jokes got almost no engagement (about 100 views total) for a small account.
- 2026-01-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561): Don't rely on opt-out long-term: it is temporary; migrate experiences before Phase 2 (H2 2026) when the default flips to Enabled and Phase 3 removes the property.
- 2026-01-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561): Upgraded joints apply only to players spawned via player/HumanoidDescription flows; existing NPC models in the place are not upgraded.
- 2026-02-03 ThePurpleGuy [0:00](https://youtu.be/VEkuM5ZG6G4?t=0): Fix unfinished bugs before running paid ads; the creator's beta bugs cost six dislikes and forced a pause and relaunch.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [13:17](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=797): Don't publish with non-functional monetisation. Shop buttons that do nothing make an 'alpha' look abandoned and kill first impressions.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [13:17](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=797): Make a public release presentable. First impressions decide whether players stay for the full release.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [5:21](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=321): Don't ship a game with only one or two repeated mission templates. The reviewer finished all four maps, which look identical, in one session.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [12:07](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=727): Check progression for impossible points; a wave-76 wall was caused by a missing configuration.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [11:20](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=680): Anchor parts and check placement after building; the speaker had an unanchored plot and chests that fell.
- 2026-02-10 VuukStudios [5:43](https://youtu.be/JQqhUWZUGDQ?t=343): Do not rely on one server. The speaker recommends using several hiring servers at once to widen reach.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Do not assume existing experiences will get this controller; it will not be backported and only targets new experiences. Keep Humanoid-based code for existing games.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Library currently assumes R15 rigs and is not fully supported for NPCs or R6 characters yet.
- 2026-02-18 RoWatcher (tracker blog) [page](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts): Pre-made clone kits (BuiltByBit, Creator Store) produce asset flips that draw community backlash; differentiate with original art and animation, e.g. critics cite missing character animations.
- 2026-02-20 1_Bruce [4:35](https://youtu.be/i3RQXxZeVYc?t=275): Don't quit because a game underperforms. If stats stay weak, treat it as a dud and apply the lessons to a new game rather than endlessly patching.
- 2026-03-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606): Don't assume clean architecture or optimized networking will get you recommended; a core loop misaligned with player psychology will still fail.
- 2026-03-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374): Do not rely on CSG-created MeshParts persisting: they are lost on Studio restart or publish until Roblox lifts this restriction.
- 2026-03-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374): Textures on tool MeshParts are discarded in CSG; only geometry and vertex colors carry over, so the main part's texture is preserved.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [4:51](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=291): Starting with a simple scoped game is hard to keep to; the author's 'small' project grew into a 200-day build because of feature creep.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [42:45](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2565): Smaller partner games are better collab targets than massive ones; big games rarely accept what a small game can offer.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [5:44](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=344): Avoid working for a revenue percentage on someone else's game; take a fixed paid amount instead.
- 2026-04-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788): Graphs do not play in-experience yet (Studio only); granting graph access does not grant access to its clip animations, so grant those separately.
- 2026-04-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788): Negative values are unsupported for Clip/Speed Speed properties and Add/Subtract Weight; avoid relying on them. AnimationId of ClipNode cannot be wired to a parameter.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [22:40](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=1360): Do not skip console at launch. Wonder Works regretted dropping console support at release because players expect cross-device friend connection.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [42:22](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=2542): Expect failure: Zach says for every success there are a couple dozen failures. Release often and learn from each project.
- 2026-04-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575): Old FilterDescendantsInstances will eventually trigger a script analysis warning; migrate before deprecation rather than relying on it.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Do not rely on Humanoid:MoveTo, which the new system does not yet support; swimming also breaks with the Single-Collider avatar setting.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Updating HumanoidDescription can kill characters when the Dead ability has RequiresNeck checked; uncheck it if this matters for your game.
- 2026-04-11 TheDankDeveloper [4:44](https://youtu.be/i5sldtdF8m0?t=284): AI-generated UI may not match your game's style; the creator rejected all variants for a complex rewards frame, so check fit before committing.
- 2026-04-12 janisjanis01 [2:42](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=162): Ship a stable build before scaling ad spend; bugs drove players away and killed retention on earlier games, and the damage could not be undone by later fixes.
- 2026-04-17 1_Bruce [0:20](https://youtu.be/iGng-GC_xFk?t=20): Avoid brain rot and flop obbys: he says they make no money and hurt the platform's reputation.
- 2026-04-17 Roblox Creators [0:41](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=41): Set account uploads to Restricted privacy before selling; otherwise other creators can use your asset without permission, including purchasers.
- 2026-04-17 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=0): Group-owned assets cannot be distributed or priced on the Creator Store; paid content must be uploaded by an individual account.
- 2026-04-19 TheDankDeveloper [8:31](https://youtu.be/Ufr0SVq-o98?t=511): AI-generated code often throws errors on first run; budget time to fix errors, since fixing one can surface two more.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Don't expect generators to modify existing model contents; copy a template model under the generator and clone it during generation.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Scale tool can leave a ProceduralModel stuck at zero size; fix by editing the Size property in Properties.
- 2026-04-25 CallMeDogey [5:29](https://youtu.be/SjjPGduApZU?t=329): Don't generate thumbnails without a reference image; the creator says that was their original mistake.
- 2026-04-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091): Do not gate core gameplay behind AgeChecked; console and VR cannot complete age check, so gate optional rewards or zones instead.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203): Don't stack manipulative mechanics on a weak game. Speaker's claim: players stay but leave hate, rather than praise and growth.
- 2026-05-01 LovJak [2:03](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=123): Don't publish a template unchanged; the creator judged it 'not unique' and unlikely to go viral without changing levels, models, prices and gameplay.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [13:32](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=812): Test purchase handling thoroughly: developer products that re-grant previous purchases on each buy (stacking bug) can be exploited and break the economy.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [12:37](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=757): Watch for sudden player drops after updates; he saw CCU fall to near zero overnight after an update, so check logs and roll back quickly.
- 2026-05-03 rasdev [9:04](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=544): Avoid overcomplicating the game, adding tutorials early, copying other games one-to-one, and spam-selling purchases.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [9:19](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=559): Check ad performance early; a low click-through rate (about 1.9%) means the creative needs replacing, not more spend.
- 2026-05-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944): Do not rely on Private for playtesters; Private is now limited to Edit-permission creators. Use Limited>Playtesters instead, which is hidden from search and home page.
- 2026-05-12 Yann [0:41](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=41): Do not assume your first game will succeed; the first game took 3 months and lost money as a learning experience.
- 2026-05-12 Yann [5:29](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=329): Copying a core loop without understanding why it works leads to low-quality products; add meaningful monetisation rather than filler.
- 2026-05-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/protect-your-games-with-script-capabilities-sandboxing/4634642): Inserting Store assets that rely on banned capabilities will now break at runtime; replace them with self-contained code rather than expecting them to work.
- 2026-05-16 Tizzy [7:27](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=447): Don't judge your game on green overview stats early; they're compared against the whole genre and fall once you're benchmarked against similar-audience games.
- 2026-05-25 MoseyTube [2:44](https://youtu.be/JG37MoDpOTw?t=164): Avoid the three common failures: players leave too fast, weak monetisation, and building one game then giving up when it doesn't blow up immediately.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [3:25](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=205): Do not think you can buy a hit by throwing money at devs; a reported $4,000 first game was never launched.
- 2026-05-30 Cumin [1:40](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=100): Reskinning a popular game with minimal changes (swapping in PNG characters) reads as lazy and does not hold players past the first few minutes.
- 2026-05-30 Cumin [9:46](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=586): Limiting a desirable reward to one player at a time, then selling a paid way around it, is a design flaw that feels predatory to players.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [5:51](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=351): Don't rely on tutorial copy-paste; the creator learned nothing from blindly following tutorials and felt no progress.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [3:38](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=218): Avoid copy-pasting repeated code blocks for each mode; it made the early game hard to maintain.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [2:57](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=177): Don't use free models you don't understand as a substitute for writing code; it stalls learning.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [10:09](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=609): Don't spend Robux on ads before the game has basic bugs fixed; bugs wasted an ad spend of 32,000 Robux.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [26:44](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1604): Check giveaway and contest rules with legal before launch; restrictions on tangible prizes vary by country and wording must be specific.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [43:13](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2593): Do not judge influencer marketing on one campaign; expect the first few campaigns to be learning spend and budget for that explicitly.
- 2026-06-05 Creators on the Blox [55:46](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=3346): Check the profile of harsh critics; fans often write negative feedback out of passion. Treat even harshly-worded feedback as applicable to the game.
- 2026-06-05 Creators on the Blox [36:34](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=2194): Making a puzzle too hard risks it going unsolved; Asimo worried about this with Ready Player One style hidden events. Balance difficulty and discoverability.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [5:18](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=318): Anchor all map parts; unanchored parts caused heavy lag.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [11:27](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=687): Players bounce when updates launch; expect a CCU dip post-update and watch it over several days before judging.
- 2026-06-06 Tizzy [1:21](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=81): Don't use templates or clone existing hits; Roblox docs say near-duplicate experiences with similar metadata/place files rank lower in recommendations and search.
- 2026-06-07 ArTheDev [1:42](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=102): Don't avoid small projects because they seem unimpressive; most beginners never ship anything at all.
- 2026-06-09 rasdev [6:19](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=379): Don't clone top-earning games (e.g. the 15th pet simulator with a brainrot skin); clones are not a niche and won't get players.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [4:43](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=283): Don't launch updates willy-nilly or hotfix without research; each change is an educated guess, not a certainty.
- 2026-06-10 MoseyTube [2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127): Don't leave non-functional UI like a 'join event' board with no event behind it; it makes the game feel dead.
- 2026-06-15 Raul [2:30](https://youtu.be/dAL8ohgzN28?t=150): Cross-promoting from a secondary YouTube channel did not produce players for this game within two days, so don't count on video promo alone.
- 2026-06-16 Alex Builds Games [7:22](https://youtu.be/x_SflcuSi1w?t=442): Avoid stacking too many monetization methods; extra cheap offers distract from your best sellers.
- 2026-06-18 Cumin [8:04](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=484): Don't rely on a small viewer base to clear the trial threshold for kids' games; audiences of 5-year-olds won't meet the 16+ engagement requirement.
- 2026-06-20 Tizzy [5:06](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=306): Don't ship clickbait titles like 'Mysterious Game' over zero-CCU clones; bounce rate now kills that exploit.
- 2026-06-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211): Do not assume legacy PD overshoot or wobble is preserved; games relying on it may change behaviour, so test before Phase 2.
- 2026-06-29 Tizzy [13:41](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=821): Don't overlook small friction points; many devs never test on phone even though about 80% of Roblox players are on mobile.
- 2026-06-29 Tizzy [15:02](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=902): Don't ship a complex map with boats if a proven level design works; the speaker simplified his map to a proven layout.
- 2026-07-03 Generalist Programmer [8:07](https://youtu.be/9mV423C2aXU?t=487): Never give passwords, card details or upfront fees to sites promising instant Robux-to-cash conversion; Roblox has no withdrawal API, so these are scams.
- 2026-07-04 Duckable [19:09](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=1149): Avoid visually near-identical boards or models of other games; a similar-looking model led to a DMCA threat, so change distinctive assets early.
- 2026-07-05 janisjanis01 [8:06](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=486): Avoid overhauling core systems (framework rewrites, many simultaneous features) right before a live push; it caused repeated breakage.
- 2026-07-05 janisjanis01 [12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730): Do not pour heavy investment into a game with weak income and conversion metrics without a plan to fix them.
- 2026-07-06 Tizzy [13:39](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=819): Don't spend months polishing before launch; launch and get real data, since the real work starts once players arrive.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [3:24](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=204): Don't obsess over graphics before retention; pretty games with low player counts don't keep players.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [3:24](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=204): Don't build in a vacuum; build what players already want rather than what you think is cool.
- 2026-07-09 LovJak [0:00](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=0): Fix obvious economy bugs before spending on ads, e.g. selling price equal to purchase price leaves no profit.
- 2026-07-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993): Do not script against Player.InputContexts.CameraContext.CameraAction; it stops synchronizing and games relying on it will break. Migrate to Player:GetCameraState().
- 2026-07-14 MoseyTube [0:50](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=50): Don't make everything a game pass; one-time purchases require constant new-player acquisition to keep earning.
- 2026-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905): Do not write Scribe values with assignment (data.Coins = 5); it is a type error and only shadows the accessor.
- 2026-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905): Do not mix a mixed-version deploy: protocol changes in v2.0.0 and schema hash changes in v2.1.0 and v2.4.0 require deploying server and client together.
- 2026-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905): Do not combine Scribe.ServerOnly and Scribe.Shared on one field; it is a startup error. Pairing Session with either is allowed.
- 2026-07-16 Raul [5:56](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=356): Finishing very fast with little content may signal thin gameplay; plan enough content before release rather than rushing.
- 2026-07-16 Deconstructor of Fun [40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403): Don't surprise players or influencers with monetization; hidden paid progression in a premium game caused a backlash that EA reversed within two days.
- 2026-07-20 Tizzy [4:33](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=273): Don't buy game templates or reskin top games. He bought a $600 template that flopped and reskinned Steal a Cookie into a failed game.
- 2026-07-20 Tizzy [8:36](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=516): Ignoring how the algorithm works sank some past projects, he says. Account for it when designing and launching.
- 2026-07-20 rasdev [0:40](https://youtu.be/t3i54NTcDi8?t=40): Don't assume the algorithm is punishing you or that adding features alone will fix a game with no players.
- 2026-07-23 Erthlord [9:32](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=572): Don't expect fast sales from paid UGC ads; 200 Robux per item on 17 items produced no measurable lift the next day.
- 2026-07-23 Yann [0:57](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=57): Don't copy a popular game's loop too closely; the creator thinks originality was the main risk. Add a meaningful mechanic that differentiates the game.
- 2026-07-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/reference-instances-directly-with-attributes/4753441): Don't treat a nil GetAttribute result as 'target missing': nil means the attribute doesn't exist; a handle whose Get() returns nil means the target isn't available locally.
- 2026-07-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/reference-instances-directly-with-attributes/4753441): GetAttributeChangedSignal does not fire when the referenced Instance streams in/out or is destroyed; only when the attribute itself is set or deleted.
- 2026-07-29 Raul [3:05](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=185): Do not ship a game you are not proud of; the creator held back a game they found bland, which cost a full day.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Investor Relations [page](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm): Traffic and Hours growth can mask falling per-hour monetization. Track monetization per hour and by age cohort, not just DAUs and Hours.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [8:23](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=503): Adapt content to each platform's language; the same short video often works on one platform and flops on another.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [22:16](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=1336): Don't make content only for the algorithm; ask how to keep these viewers and involve them, and keep the tone matching the game.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [24:23](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=1463): Don't put the play call-to-action at the start; first give a reason to watch, then ask people to play at the end.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [31:00](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=1860): Don't spam game links everywhere hoping people play; earn their interest first.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [31:42](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=1902): Don't constantly change your identity or genre to follow trends; audiences won't form around an inconsistent creator.
- 2026-08-04 Duckable [14:09](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=849): A single missing word in a script caused hours of debugging; re-read the exact error line and check for typos before rewriting systems.
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [14:35](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=875): Don't prioritise monetisation over gameplay; making a game super pay-to-win kills it. Add monetisation that doesn't hurt core gameplay.
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [15:16](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=916): Don't implement every community suggestion or give players everything they ask for; it can break your economy (e.g. giving away the rarest pet).
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [15:57](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=957): Don't stop improving a game once it makes money; neglecting updates is how games die.
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [16:38](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=998): Don't push cosmetic-only updates (e.g. one skin) that add no engagement or content.
- 2026-08-06 VuukStudios [11:28](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=688): Do not rely on a peak alone; a game with no loyal fanbase dies when its hype ends, as their 5K CCU game fell to about 500 players.
- 2026-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-avatar-backgrounds-on-marketplace/4778993): A non-response to a pilot application does not mean decline; reviews are rolling and accepted creators are contacted via DevForum in waves.
- 2026-08-07 LovJak [1:20](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=80): Don't publish a template unchanged; if the concept already exists (swing obby for brain rots), it won't go viral. Change the core theme first.
- 2026-08-07 rasdev [4:40](https://youtu.be/Ox5pwUuzF-M?t=280): Do not make monetization scummy: avoid paywalling basics like sprinting or teleporting and cluttering the screen with purchase prompts, since it turns players off.
- 2026-08-07 RoyalDev [5:28](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=328): Assume players know nothing; creators underestimate how much of their obvious mechanics new players need explained.
- 2026-08-07 RoyalDev [13:36](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=816): Don't assume obvious mechanics are obvious; a whiteboard instruction the developer thought obvious confused players.
- 2026-08-09 JustDucksey [8:12](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=492): Rushing features leaves many bugs; ship a smaller working core rather than many half-working systems.
- 2026-08-12 RoyalDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=205): Don't ship rough polish: placeholder UI and hastily written scripts hurt the game; polish is needed to stick to the algorithm.
- 2026-08-12 RoyalDev [6:09](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=369): A good idea can still fail if poorly executed; execution determines how the game performs with the Roblox algorithm.
- 2026-08-15 RoyalDev [2:03](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=123): Don't blame ads for poor results. Strong ads deliver the right players, but a game that isn't good won't keep them.
- 2026-08-15 RoyalDev [0:40](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=40): Ignore popular 'secret' ad setting recipes (specific budgets, start days, days of week); the speaker says none are proven.
- 2026-08-18 MonarchDev [3:41](https://youtu.be/MANBsA1MTzI?t=221): Don't over-invest in originality; proven structures from working games can be reused and reskinned, as the speaker did with Grow a Garden and Sell Lemons UI.
- 2026-08-18 RoyalDev [4:50](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=290): A dead game may be an execution problem (polish, friction) rather than a bad idea; audit friction before scrapping the concept.
- 2026-08-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656): Do not panic-buy bots or chase player counts that were inflated by bot traffic; those numbers are being corrected.
- 2026-08-21 LovJak [7:14](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=434): Check for Z-fighting floors and animation glitches on NPC sitting before release; the creator shipped these visible bugs in a showcase.
- 2026-08-21 Business of Creators [3:46](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=226): Scaling headcount and overhead fast after venture funding nearly killed the studio: 90+ staff, $600K/month overhead, then cut to 12.
- 2026-08-21 Business of Creators [4:26](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=266): Work-for-hire ad games paid bills but drained creative passion; the studio became more ad agency than game studio.
- 2026-08-23 MonarchDev [3:26](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=206): Shipping bugs and then fixing them can introduce new ones; re-test after each fix before pushing.
- 2026-08-25 AllThingsHow (guide) [page](https://allthings.how/steal-an-egg-roblox-ban-why-it-happened-and-how-it-returned/): Avoid inflated or artificial engagement metrics in in-game features; the Steal An Egg treadmill showed like counts that were not real engagement, which fed the takedown speculation.
- 2026-08-25 eGamers [page](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/): Avoid embedding external video/social feeds in-game; Roblox only allows links from an approved list, and off-platform sharing rules can get a game pulled.
- 2026-08-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188): Do not assume a feed is fine if rewards are only implied; implied requirement of ongoing viewing counts as a violation.
- 2026-08-25 MoseyTube [5:41](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=341): Do not release and walk away; the first two weeks decide whether a game gets players.
- 2026-08-26 Matt The Method [2:37](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=157): Don't build first and plan marketing later; decide how players will find the game before opening Studio.
- 2026-08-29 MonarchDev [2:56](https://youtu.be/_DwOE49GDdo?t=176): Small developers without a large ad budget struggle to get traction; don't expect ads alone to fix a lack of players.
- 2026-08-29 RoyalDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=165): Watch for memory leaks from uncleaned old data and objects, and for AI-compounded bad habits that worsen with each prompt.
- 2026-08-29 RoyalDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=205): Don't mistake separate working systems for a finished game; verify that AI-built systems (flashlight, enemy AI) actually connect to each other.
- 2026-08-30 janisjanis01 [10:10](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=610): Playtest and balance early; creator says many of his games failed because of balancing, and found a useless turret and a wave-168 difficulty spike.
- 2026-08-30 janisjanis01 [15:41](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=941): Add invisible boundary walls at launch; players were falling through the map.
- 2026-08-30 janisjanis01 [16:23](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=983): Guard against infinite-stacking exploits: one player stacked hundreds of turrets on one tile, reaching wave 8,679 and breaking the economy.
- 2026-09-06 kra_DeZ [9:06](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=546): Don't assume a quiet game is bad; most failures come from nobody knowing it exists, so promote before judging the design.
- 2026-09-06 MonarchDev [4:04](https://youtu.be/haYW1hXahOM?t=244): Don't just clone a game; copies can get traffic but usually lose to the original. Add a real twist by merging two games' core concepts or a new concept.
- 2026-09-08 ItsZyonic [6:21](https://youtu.be/RIWPfMJyVhQ?t=381): Do not sell early on a mid-metric game; the speaker's game dropped to 300 CCU and then recovered after changes.
- 2026-09-11 MonarchDev [5:27](https://youtu.be/LD7LGP10dRc?t=327): Cloning a successful game's template is not the same as copying its loop; change the core concept while keeping the proven loop.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505): Do not assume DevEx Robux cash-out is permanent; DevEx ends in US mid-2027 and other regions later. Track the announced end dates.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505): Moderation affects earnings: pending earnings may be held longer or forfeited for Terms of Use violations; keep account in good standing.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): Don't rely on DevEx long-term in the US: it transitions away mid-2027 in the US and remains longer elsewhere.
- 2026-09-12 Duckable [14:12](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=852): Check whether an AI tool's 'generated' systems are actually pre-made marketplace templates before trusting its output.
- 2026-09-12 Duckable [11:30](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=690): Don't pay for AI tool subscriptions on free-trial pressure; the paid tiers still failed to finish a working game in the test.
- 2026-09-12 LovJak [0:15](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=15): Do not pay half upfront for a game transfer. Pay only after ownership transfer is confirmed, or use a trusted escrow; the seller went silent after the first payment.
- 2026-09-13 rasdev [3:24](https://youtu.be/Df5ebFGEOXY?t=204): Don't make another 'Steal a Brainrot'-style clone; competition is lame, so differentiate.
- 2026-09-13 Cumin [0:40](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=40): Don't rely on nostalgia for a re-release; modernise systems like matchmaking and progression, because players judge it against current games.
- 2026-09-14 Tizzy [16:14](https://youtu.be/E_tVm3bc5Xo?t=974): Avoid 'copium' victim mentality: blaming the algorithm, ad costs, or Roblox updates for weak results is counterproductive.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [14:48](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=888): Moderation flags will be handled with warnings during editing rather than takedowns, so fix flagged content before publishing.
- 2026-09-18 RoyalDev [0:01](https://youtu.be/nLuxSFg5viw?t=1): Don't assume a niche will work just because it trends; the speaker's Locus-in-backrooms and word-game experiments underperformed, so validate with small tests.
- 2026-09-19 rasdev [2:27](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=147): Don't copy a game word for word; remix multiple external sources into one distinct concept.
- 2026-09-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/players-can-now-find-in-game-items-outside-your-game/4888425): Unlisted items never appear in Shop or outside your game, so leaving products unlisted forfeits external discovery.
- 2026-09-23 Tizzy [13:02](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=782): Don't claim an original idea. Almost every game is derivative; differentiate on the fantasy and unique mechanic instead.
- 2026-09-23 Lnor Roblox [2:56](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=176): Passive timer-based rewards (e.g., currency every 10 seconds) were hard to understand and easy to exploit with auto clickers; tie rewards to player actions instead.
- 2026-09-26 rasdev [7:27](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=447): Cut unnecessary complexity and extra features; simpler games are easier to understand and play.
- 2026-09-27 janisjanis01 [10:43](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=643): Do not assume stats or marketing fix a game; if the core game is weak, players will leave regardless of thumbnails or ads.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [7:40](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=460): Don't change default Roblox mechanics heavily; Parkour Tower's altered mechanics (portals, see-through viewports) confused players and underperformed.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [34:41](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2081): Don't ship updates untested and don't wipe player data; they admitted rushed weekly pushes and testing gaps caused data loss.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [34:41](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2081): Schedule updates for your players' peak hours; their NZ-based release at 3–5am caused poor timing for the US audience.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Investor Relations [page](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm): Cost of revenue is deferred over average paying-user lifetime (27 months), so bookings-based margins are not comparable to revenue-based ones.
- 2026-10-04 Seibo [3:30](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=210): Verify the correct build before publishing; an accidental bugged release caused a sharp player drop he had to fix.
- 2026-10-05 ArTheDev [34:04](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=2044): Anchoring the character root during an ability blocked knockback; set WalkSpeed and JumpPower to zero instead, then restore them.
- 2026-10-09 Alex Builds Games [4:46](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=286): Don't publish near-copies of a top game with new powers; he claims Roblox's docs say direct copies won't earn home recommendations.
- 2026-10-11 kra_DeZ [6:47](https://youtu.be/dE0njfPNsdY?t=407): Don't bolt a second mode onto an existing game (he added zombies to a horror game). Players want one or the other; build a separate game.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [22:26](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1346): Don't play sound effects or particles inside bind to simulation; rollbacks replay them repeatedly and stack the sounds. Use task.defer for visual effects.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:07](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1387): Keep the predicted simulation small; around 10,000 predicted instances lagged the system and forced input latency, causing death loops.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:48](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1428): Some misprediction is normal, especially with remote players; janky character motion on your own character is not and should be fixed.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [12:14](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=734): Blocking chat messages frustrates players; Roblox's AI rephrase approach suggests an appropriate alternative instead, which players said kept chat more positive and fun.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [6:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=410): Remove flagged instances for everyone, not one player; hiding them per-user leaves other observers still seeing the bad content and re-triggering detection.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:03](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=243): Design out unsafe content from the start in open-ended creation, avatar, and roleplay games; prevention is cheaper than detection and enforcement.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [1:22](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=82): Don't use the highest intelligence layer everywhere. Layers are independent; default behavior handles expected events cheaply, dynamic/human-like only when needed.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:33](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=993): Don't rely only on home recommendations; top games get many players from search, friends and community, so build toward being less algorithm-dependent.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:17](https://youtu.be/oxiaIhN5Ybo?t=257): Using third-party IP without permission risks permanent takedown, account termination, and lawsuits. Get a license first.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-simpler-path-to-publish-plus-invite-early-testers/4930524): Early tester access requires an age check and age 9+; testers must be similar-age friends or Trusted Friends, and under-16 testers can be blocked by parental settings.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:51](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=291): Watch scaling costs: a skill tied to 3 dungeons becomes a per-update burden at 16+ dungeons. Each new class/dungeon multiplies future maintenance, so add content sparingly.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [6:53](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=413): Adding classes early seems cheap but level-cap increases force new weapons, skills and armor per class. Model maintenance cost of each system before expanding it.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [31:20](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1880): Don't copy the process of successful studios because they succeeded; their methods are not what made them successful.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [6:49](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=409): Avoid making generic AI slop; pick something unique that nobody has done before.
- 2026-10-14 Tizzy [12:44](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=764): Balance before launch. A week or two of broken D1 (6-8%) on live hurt scaling and likely cost the game a higher peak.
- 2026-10-18 Duckable [24:23](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=1463): Don't neglect core game design fun while rushing monthly releases; the creator says this sabotaged the game.
- 2026-10-20 rasdev [2:51](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=171): Budget carefully: one dev spent $12,000 building a game and recouped only $3,000; another spent 15,000 Robux on ads and got back 2,000 Robux.
- 2026-10-20 kra_DeZ [1:37](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=97): Don't give up on games after the ad run ends; he says his Dark Rooms game fell off and died after he stopped updating it.
- 2026-10-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/home-thumbnail-a-b-test/4947121): Don't assume a sudden QPTR drop is caused by your thumbnails or game when unchanged thumbnails drop together; check platform-wide attribution first.
- 2026-10-21 MonarchDev [0:44](https://youtu.be/3bUysm7IFxg?t=44): Scope check: a single AI prompt yields a prototype, not a finished game; expect days of manual work to turn generated output into a playable game.
- 2026-10-21 Xaiyne [0:00](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=0): Creator says early old dynamic-face items were a waste; focus on items that match current demand and trends (e.g., seasonal makeup).
- 2026-10-22 Deconstructor of Fun [46:44](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2804): Founders often carry over large past-studio scope into new funded studios; plan demand and market first, not a Christmas-scenario best case.
- 2026-10-23 TheDankDeveloper [12:19](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=739): Don't leave usage unused: the creator ended having not hit the session limit, so plan more prompts per session window.
- 2026-10-23 tef [13:25](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=805): Test multiplayer and servers before promoting; the speaker found bugs only after publishing and said he had not tested multiplayer until late.
- 2026-10-23 rasdev [6:48](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=408): Don't copy existing games: copycats decline (Monkey Escape fell to about 6K players while Keyboard Escape holds about 200K), and Roblox is taking down copies.
- 2026-10-23 kra_DeZ [4:07](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=247): Cleaning/chore sims (PowerWash-style) were hyped by a trend; by the time copies appear, the trend is fading and clones underperform.
- 2026-10-24 Crusherfire [2:02](https://youtu.be/4n7thZAAF-E?t=122): Avoid one-prompt generation of whole games; it produces unpolished, low-effort output that older players spot and avoid.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [6:10](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=370): Check testing server visibility before playtests; a testing server accidentally set public exposed unreleased update content.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [8:57](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=537): Anchor all map parts when building in Studio; unanchored parts caused a bug that took time to fix.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Work in separate standalone place files. Places saved with AnimationPlayer during Early Access aren't guaranteed forward compatible with Studio Beta.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Don't modify or save live games while in the program. They become unplayable to users not in the program. Revert the account setting before changing live or Team Create games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Configs do nothing unless your code reads them; call GetValue in the code that uses the value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Conditional values must match the default value's data type, and rule order is global; verify priority on the Conditions tab before publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Use GetConfigForPlayerAsync for targeted configs; GetConfigAsync does not apply targeting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices): Ask permission before using someone else's work; never vandalise or put down others' builds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset): Map each end state (winner found, timer up, unknown) to its own message branch with an else fallback, so an unhandled state shows an error rather than failing silently.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies): Do not skip the modesty layer over sensitive body regions when texturing; it is required by Community Standards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Renaming a mesh in Blender (e.g. Mesh1 to Mesh2) creates a new MeshPart and leaves the original unchanged; plan renames carefully.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Platform-integrated chat becomes required for all games from May 2027; unsupported chat APIs will be deprecated, so plan migration before then.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Prefixes aren't supported under platform-integrated chat; if your game requires chat prefixes, don't enable it yet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Disable the property (PlatformIntegratedChat) again to revert; it can only be changed in Studio, not from scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Games using default Roblox chat with no customizations need no action and should see no change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Signs, boards, notes and comment systems are not affected by this update; it covers TextChatService only.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer): Do not auto-skin hats, hair or glasses; set AutoSkin to Disabled since they should stay rigid and skinning introduces unwanted deformation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails): Avoid ambiguous or unclear imagery in thumbnails; it causes player confusion and fewer clicks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges): Disabled badges are hidden from the game page and cannot be earned, so check Badge is Enabled before players are expected to earn it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Test edge cases where players push the limits of the game, and design prevention or stop-gap solutions, or deliberately encourage the behaviour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Do not trust paper prototypes for fun alone: some activities feel more engaging with physical assets than on screen (false positives).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Ensure CanHarvest is a child of the touched part; the script only works if the flag sits directly under that part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Put the tool in StarterPack, not Workspace, or it will not be equipped on spawn.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Use canHarvest.Value, not canHarvest = true; spell Transparency and CanCollide exactly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): The leaderstats names in FindFirstChild must match the IntValue names used in PlayerSetup exactly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/connect-the-beam): If a beam's arrow points backwards, check that Attachment0 is on the player and Attachment1 on the goal, or mirror custom textures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Do not make the inner and outer cage identical; the system cannot determine the accessory and deformation breaks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Do not place clothing inside the inner cage; it causes artifacts when layered over other clothing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Do not let the outer cage sit inside the inner cage or leave cages missing heads or limbs; these are rejected during validation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Avoid collapsed or overlapping cage vertices; they cause artifacts and are rejected during validation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Keep the clothing mesh inside the outer cage; mesh outside the outer cage does not deform and is rejected during validation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Avoid oversized gaps between clothing and outer cage; they cause severe deformation when combined with other layers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Do not rely on server-side jump detection; it is inconsistent, so the example package removed it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing): Many user-generated avatars on the Marketplace do not support 2D classic clothing; check for modern 3D cosmetic options instead for rigid accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page): Never share your Roblox password, even with real-life friends; use a password that is not guessable from your username.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page): Roblox employees never ask for passwords; report anyone who does via Report Abuse. There is no free Robux; ignore sites or players claiming otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/dsa-ip-reporting): Link a verified email to your Roblox account; Roblox notifies you by in-platform message and email when content is removed or an account is suspended under the IP Policy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): Violating advertising standards can get your game or account suspended; check content against the questions in the docs before publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Do not manipulate impressions or teleports; Roblox may deduct or reclaim Robux, and may suspend the game or account for fraud.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion): All promotional content must follow Community Standards and Terms of Use; advertising must also follow Advertising Standards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Do not expect rbxgameasset names to load when pasting objects into another game; they will fail or load a different same-named asset.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration): Before reverting a place version, disable Team Create; otherwise other editors' auto-saves can overwrite the revert.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/module): Avoid ModuleScripts requiring each other in a circle; this causes a 'Requested module was required recursively' error.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/roblox-plus): Only the server owner's time counts for paid private server payouts; time spent by invited subscribers does not count toward your earnings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization): Diagnose low conversion: check for expensive items, poor shop visibility, unappealing content, and confusing purchase flow steps.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/main-page): Never share your Roblox password, make it hard to guess, and never trust anyone claiming free Robux or a secret method to get it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds): Do not rely on the default Teams scoring for round-based games, since it ties team score to current players and drops points when a scorer disconnects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/explorer): When searching for names with spaces, wrap the full name in double quotes (e.g. tag:"Light Source") or the search logic treats spaces as a combined query.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/roblox-account-creation): Distrust anyone claiming secret methods for free Robux; never trust such players or sites.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/errors): Don't assume one error shape: v1 endpoints return three different formats, so parse both 'error' and 'code' keys and the validation-style 'errors' object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/rate-limit-public-text-inputs): All text shown to other players must pass through the text filter (GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync) before broadcasting; wrap the call in pcall.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace): Do not confuse the Marketplace (avatar items for sale) with the Creator Store (models, images, plugins for building games).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Beams cannot be synced to audio via particle systems alone; script the timing when no sequencer exists in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test-blender): Destructive modeling changes or overridden saved animations can corrupt facial or skinning data; reapplying steps or restarting from the original template may be faster than repairing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating): Don't assume game-making is too hard; the docs frame it as approachable, with tutorials walking through building and scripting basics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons): If a bridge is solid at game start, check that its parts are anchored and not touching terrain or other parts, which can fire Touched early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Do not add 17+ content before generating a 17+ age recommendation; the questionnaire must be completed first or the experience risks moderation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Base questionnaire answers on the most mature or extreme content players can encounter, not the typical content.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Any implied violence (e.g. off-screen sounds) automatically counts as mild; any realistic consequence of violence, even once, counts as moderate or strong.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Any depicted blood, even from a distance, counts as light; pools, gushing, organs, dismemberment or up-close spatter count as heavy (17+ only).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Experience guidelines don't cover user-generated avatar items players bring in; only content you create counts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Inaccurate guidelines can lead to guideline removal, restriction to 13+ visibility, and moderation actions; misrepresentation may bring consequences.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): Free-form user creation and social hangouts are restricted to players 13+ regardless of parental controls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model): Do not add Player objects to the Players container manually; the engine creates them automatically on join.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model): Name objects descriptively so they are easy to find and modify later.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design): Avoid scarce or overly effort-intensive reward sources; they discourage economy participation and create perceptions of unfairness.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Avoid purely informational notifications; prompt an immediate action like rejoining or responding to a challenge.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Don't use disguised ads, false time pressure, bait-and-switch free rewards, or pre-loaded purchases; these violate notification rules.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/expressions): Remember Luau truthiness in and/or: only false and nil are falsy, so 0 and empty strings count as true in conditions like IsGrounded and GroundSpeed or AirSpeed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Group game analytics are visible only to the owner and members with sufficient role permissions, since some metrics are sensitive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio/create-account): Never share your password, even with real-life friends; avoid passwords that just append digits to your username.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/landing): Never share your Roblox password, even with real-life friends; choose a password that isn't a trivial variant of your username.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/landing): Roblox employees never ask for your password; report anyone who does via Report Abuse. There is no such thing as free Robux.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/moods): Mood code runs in a hidden internal script, not the Animate LocalScript; you cannot edit it, only interact through the mood StringValue.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-reference): Handle a null picture claim in userinfo; avatar headshots may not be generated yet or may have been moderated.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes): Don't rely on cross-game pass sales: they are disabled starting May 30, 2026; integrate Robux transfers before then if your game depends on them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Touched and TouchEnded do not fire when Position or CFrame is set directly to intersect a part; they only fire from physical movement.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Models cannot connect Touched directly; loop over child BaseParts and connect each one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): If the wrong animation plays, confirm the animation was published via the Animation Editor and that WalkAnimationID's Value property is only a number.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Don't let students copy-paste or hack code together; the rubric expects a clearly structured algorithm with abstraction.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups): Don't rely on Roblox to resolve group disputes; Roblox cannot arbitrate conflicts within a group, so agree on roles and splits in writing up front.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson): If a vehicle won't fly, confirm it is inside the Garage folder in Explorer and includes at least one Body part with the flight script.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/referral-system): Guard against abuse: reward each invitee only once (DataStore flag), add inviter cooldowns, and monitor for suspicious activity.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback): Don't treat the dashboard as current: players can change ratings anytime and ratings update only at the end of each day.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy): Do not re-publish an existing item as Limited, or re-publish a Limited item; overly similar items violate policy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy): Do not sell items that obscure the avatar, in-game UI, other users' avatars or view, or make avatar parts disappear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy): Do not miscategorize items (e.g. pants listed as Shirt); complete hairstyles go in Hair, hats in Hat, Tops/Bottoms must match garment type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy): Do not create items that depend on Roblox platform glitches or issues.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles): Do not move package UI objects around in the hierarchy without updating UIController, because the code cannot find them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build): Do not edit a Build game in Roblox Studio: it permanently becomes a standard Studio game and loses Build status. Make all changes in the Build chat.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build): Build games default to 16+ age rating; apply via Roblox Select with content review to allow younger players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build): If a request is refused, rephrase the idea to be more original; content rules, age setting, or IP restrictions may apply.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer): Changes made during an active transfer are not carried over; finish or cancel before editing, and note transfer requests expire after 7 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer): Credit card information is removed on transfer, so the new owner must set up payment details.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Gate score accrual with a per-player alive flag so dead players stop earning points; a score that keeps counting while dead undermines the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/quick-words): Don't write Quick Words that are social chat or open-ended questions; Roblox review will reject them and you must revise and republish.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/classroom-best-practices): Address off-topic questions (favourite game, free Robux) at the start of class by stating that only project-related questions will be answered.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/social-media-links): Do not put social URLs or invite codes inside the game itself; Community Standards only allow links on the main details page. Say 'Links on our game's page' instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits): Do not rely on client-reported hits without server checks; a malicious client can remove walls or fake tags unless the server verifies the raycast path.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): A BindableFunction with no OnInvoke set makes the invoking script yield indefinitely and never resume.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Only the last OnInvoke assigned to a BindableFunction runs; assigning multiple callbacks silently overrides earlier ones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Omitting a return statement in OnInvoke makes Invoke return nil.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Do not pass tables mixing numeric and string keys through bindables; elements can be dropped during transfer. Use a pure array or pure dictionary.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Avoid nil values in tables passed through bindables, whether arrays or dictionaries.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Non-string table keys (Instances, functions, userdata) are converted to strings when passed through bindables.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Tables passed through bindables are copied, so identity checks fail; metatables are also lost in transfer, so methods and fields from metatables disappear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines): Developer menu text, status messages, admin announcements and audit logs are not chat, but any user-generated text shared with others still needs text filtering.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge): Guard trigger handlers with a boolean (timerActive) so repeated Touched events don't restart a running timer.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): Do not skip installing MaterialVariants; meshes that reference uninstalled MaterialVariants render gray instead of textured.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Do not put Humanoid instances inside SLIM models; use SLIM avatars for animated characters instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Avoid models with zero Scale, and models that play animations, since they are excluded from SLIM processing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): R6 avatars, NPCs, custom proportions, and body scaling are not eligible for SLIM avatars and fall back to default rendering.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-local-currency): Paid access can shrink your player count since some users won't pay a fee to join; weigh this before enabling it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-local-currency): Games reported broken by many users may be quarantined and escrowed earnings refunded; make sure the game runs properly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-local-currency): Paid access games cannot have private servers, be copied, or repeat existing games; violations can suspend your account.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/spawn-respawn): Do not confuse SpawnLocation.TeamColor with BrickColor/Color; TeamColor drives team logic, the others are only visual.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions): Do not check subscription status from the client; GetUserSubscriptionStatusAsync must run on the server. Handle UserSubscriptionStatusChanged to catch mid-session changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions): Robux subscription purchases are not refundable, and cancellations do not auto-refund; refunds are handled case by case.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start): Do not disable Running; characters then cannot move along the ground. Keep Getting Up enabled whenever Falling Down is enabled, or characters trip and never recover.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/teams): Do not confuse SpawnLocation.TeamColor with BrickColor or Color; the latter only change visual appearance and do nothing for team functionality.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables): Setting a Luau reference to nil does not free an object while another strong reference exists, so check for lingering references to avoid leaks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables): Clearing a Luau reference to an Instance doesn't delete it; call Instance:Destroy() and clear all remaining references.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization): Downvotes follow monetization users disapprove of; be transparent about chance-based monetization and use it responsibly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization): Do not show fake urgency: no inaccurate stock claims, and no countdown timers that are inaccurate or automatically restart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization): Avoid pushy language with minors: use 'View Item', 'See Price', 'Open Shop' instead of 'GET IT NOW', 'LAST CHANCE', 'BUY BEFORE IT'S GONE'.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Be aware that per-part CustomPhysicalProperties override the part's material variant, which overrides the material override, which overrides the base material's default physics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards): Be aware that players can collect coins before PlayerAdded finishes initialising and get reset to zero; use scheduling or freeze the character until setup completes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-a-health-pickup): Use a state flag (attribute such as Enabled) as a debounce so a touchable pickup cannot be collected repeatedly while on cooldown.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-a-health-pickup): Be aware task.wait inside the touch handler yields only that connection's thread; the example uses it deliberately for the cooldown, and it works because each touch runs in its own thread.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places): When changing start place, use Publish As and choose Overwrite; do not use Publish, which overwrites the place itself.
