- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places): Keep places under 100 MB (104,857,600 bytes); larger places may fail to save or publish, usually due to inefficiencies.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Don't assume a campaign is delivering: Inactive status means no ad sets or ads are running; check the Ad Sets or Ads tab to find why.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Ads rejected for violating advertising standards show Rejected status and will not run; check status after submission.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/private-servers): Changing the private server price cancels all active subscriptions; players get an inbox message. Avoid price changes unless necessary.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/shop): Remember to click Save in the Item catalog tab; changes to Shop aren't visible to players until saved.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/locations): Avoid Starter containers for client code with Script RunContext Client; the original and the cloned copy both run, causing duplicate behaviour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/locations): Default Script RunContext is Legacy: it runs server-side only inside server containers like ServerScriptService or Workspace.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/micro-gamepad): In ContextActionService handlers, return Pass for unhandled input states and Sink once the input is handled so other bound actions still run.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Don't assume Player objects are destroyed on leave; connections to Player and CharacterAdded keep leaking on servers. Enable PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior or destroy manually on PlayerRemoving.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/import): Ensure the model meets Roblox rigid accessory specifications before selling or using it; otherwise errors appear at different workflow points.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/create-with-module-scripts): Misspelled module names cause 'Infinite yield possible'. A missing require line causes 'attempt to index global'. Match names exactly and include the require.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): Changing a Shopify item's price takes all associated bundles off sale and resubmits them for bundling fee processing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): Deleting an imported catalog item automatically deletes all commerce products containing it; you must re-import and recreate them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): Roblox does not auto-revoke developer products granted as digital benefits on refund; handle revocation via CommerceProductOrderRefunded webhook.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): Two identical catalog items cannot be on sale at once; take the existing bundle off sale before listing another with the same item.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): PromptCommerceProductPurchaseFinished fires when the prompt closes and does not indicate a successful purchase; confirm via receipts or order events.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products): Shopify content edits (name, images, description, variants) go through moderation; the live listing keeps showing the previously approved version until approval.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/tax-information): Payouts processed on or after Nov 1, 2026 are subject to withholding even if the request was submitted earlier.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/tax-information): Never send tax forms or sensitive info by email; official Roblox emails come from [email] or [email].
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/make-changes-to-arrays): Don't forward-iterate while removing; shifted indexes cause skipped elements, so iterate backward instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Name/health display requires the Humanoid to sit inside a Model with a BasePart named Head, both at the same hierarchy level; otherwise nothing shows.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Occlusion does not occur if the occluding part has Transparency above 0.99, or when hidden behind another character model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs): Restore does not publish. After restoring a revision you must call publish with the returned draftHash or the rollback never goes live.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Time reward progress resets to 0 each session, so players lose all progress toward Time rewards when they leave.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Do not ship the example rewardClaimedHandlerFunction; it only prints, so replace it with your real reward-granting logic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Do not leave ad campaigns Pending: the owner's invoicing info must be complete before the campaign can run.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Do not delete vertices or alter UVs on inner/outer cages; this causes import errors in Studio or failures when equipping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/emotes): Disabling the emotes menu does not block chat-command emotes like /e cheer; block those separately if needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/pickup-system): Pickup models must be visual only; do not put functional weapon Tools in ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Pickups.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines): Copyright infringement still applies even if you don't monetize, credit the creator, downloaded it free, bought a physical copy, or believed it was fair use.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines): Don't submit knowingly false copyright or counter notices; this is illegal under DMCA 512(f) and violates Roblox policy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines): Using AI to modify someone's work to avoid an exact copy still counts as infringement if it reproduces their protected expression.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines): Off-platform infringement (other sites) is generally not actioned by Roblox; report it through that platform's own process.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines): If your content is removed by mistake, submit a counter notice (copyright) or appeal by email to [email] (trademark), or contact the other party to withdraw the notice.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-context-menu): ACM cannot be edited while open. Re-verify an action is still valid when selected, e.g. both users still in the same area, since options may have been removed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/creators): Publishing content using someone else's IP without permission violates the Terms of Use and can lead to content removal and account termination.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/creators): Do not ignore a standard license offer expecting it to lapse; it auto-activates after 14 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/control-structures): Always include a delay such as task.wait() inside infinite while loops; omitting it freezes the game and crashes Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/event-sequencer): Do not place Environment folder assets in scenes used with inline mode; inline mode expects existing place assets and global lighting settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/event-sequencer): Scenes must be tagged SequencerScene to appear in the Scene Manager plugin; if a new scene is missing, tag it, then save and reopen the place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Do not call GetConfigAsync for experiment players; use GetConfigForPlayerAsync per player, otherwise experiments don't apply.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Delay calling GetValue until the player reaches the feature being tested, otherwise you enroll players who never see it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Avoid content changes during an experiment, even unrelated-seeming ones, as they can invalidate results. Don't run overlapping experiments unless confident they don't interact.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Don't rely on the Details tab early for decisions; it's only for checking the experiment runs properly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/add-to-the-garage): If the speeder disappears after Cut and Paste Into, use undo (Ctrl+Z / Cmd+Z) and repeat Cut and Paste Into rather than recreating it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/supporting-systems): Disable seating before teleporting players: a CFrame coordinate change while seated will not work correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques): Don't assume ChildAdded, ChildRemoved or CollectionService added/removed signals mean real spawns; they also fire on stream in/out. Guard effects with an attribute like Spawned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques): Persistent models and Touched, ProximityPrompt, ClickDetector and DragDetector interactions don't work for players who don't have the part streamed in; use persistent models or another mechanism.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust): Don't assume Unknown means untrusted. Roblox only says it can't tell, so decide per feature or per part of the game how to treat it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Don't have multiple people sync the same script and edit files simultaneously; collaborators can overwrite each other's changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Don't delete a top-level synced instance on disk; first Stop Sync in Studio, then delete the file or folder, or Studio throws an error.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Keep script names compatible with the file system: no duplicate names or unsupported characters, or sync will error until resolved.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Don't disable Auto resume sync on place open; it dramatically reduces Script Sync's usefulness.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages): Never delete or move a PackageLink object; doing so converts that copy back to a normal object and loses package capabilities like self-updating.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-fees-and-commissions): Upload fees are not refunded if moderation rejects the item, so check guidelines before paying the 80 Robux (500 for emissive) fee.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-fees-and-commissions): Free Limited items cost a per-unit fee that Roblox keeps entirely; listing 200 hats at 100 Robux per unit costs 20,000 Robux.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/place-files): Unreferenced or hidden assets accumulate over time and silently inflate place size; audit them periodically rather than assuming the place is clean.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/construct-your-world): Limit combat pockets to at most three entrance/exit points to avoid giving players choice overload while navigating.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/glow-lights-with-for-loops): Keep loop body statements between do and end, and use exactly two commas in the for-loop header, or the effect won't show.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/activity-history): Check Activity History after unexpected changes to paid access, price, or private server settings to trace who made them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Do not change the shared Roblox avatar identity without a good reason; users invest heavily in avatars and want them seen by friends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Do not just port a game from another platform; adjust for Roblox by slimming upfront tutorials and emphasising social gameplay.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Test UI regularly so it does not conflict with core Roblox UI like chat and player list, or disable what you do not need.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap): Don't leave Touched-connected functions unguarded; without a debounce, running across the platform resets the fade loop each time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators): Don't rely only on Roblox's notifications: you remain responsible for permanently deleting all personal data covered by an RTBF request, even when automation runs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators): Verify that personal data stored outside configured RTBF templates or custom automation is also deleted; review the daily RTBF message against your data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing): Hard-coded prices in your UI won't update for regional pricing, so players see the wrong price while being charged the correct regional one. Fix by using MarketplaceService.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation): HumanoidDescription for creation must not contain pre-existing asset IDs for body parts or accessories; only new assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/change-the-animation): If the animation you edited does not play, confirm you published it through the Animation Editor; unpublished changes won't load.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/emote-bar): Module modifies the local user's emotes via HumanoidDescription:SetEmotes, so GetEmotes returns the module-modified values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/story-games-lesson): String variables show everything literally between quotes, so stray spaces or missing spaces between joined words appear in output.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/story-games-lesson): Remind learners to press Stop after each playtest in Roblox Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Do not start makeup in one template and transfer it to the other; decide on the template before creating any textures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange): Direct Robux purchases, subscription monthly grants, traded items, transfer-received Robux, gift cards, and template-game passes with no real visits do not count as Earned Robux.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/kids-and-select): Reaching Kids/Select requires 2FA, age/ID verification, and the full Maturity & Compliance Questionnaire; answering only the first question limits you to 16+.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/greybox-your-environment): Do not run a single flat floor with no elevation change; it leaves players little strategy beyond hiding from line of sight.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/designing-studio-templates): Do not let students join the template's group; a student may accidentally make unintended modifications to the template.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/laser-traps-with-beams): Scripts and parts must match exact names (e.g. CollisionBox, script parented to the blaster model) or the trap silently fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores): Do not use memory stores for data that must persist beyond 45 days; their maximum lifetime is 45 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores): Do not use memory stores to frequently read a single key or partition; configs are more performant for that read-only case.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding): Do not rely on default shortest paths: they route through swamp water when it is geometrically shorter; set material costs to avoid this.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding): Keep path requests under 3,000 studs straight-line distance and expect failures in large open worlds or mazes that exceed 20,000 nodes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding): Set AgentHeight and AgentCanJump to match the agent; computations fail if the only route needs jumping while AgentCanJump is false.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/webhooks/webhook-notifications): Don't rely on third-party tools beyond Discord and Slack for webhooks; Roblox says other tools risk missing notifications.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred): Don't assume Destroying or ancestry/property-change handlers see the state at the moment of change; by the time they run, the instance may already be destroyed or changed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/type-coercion): Arithmetic on a non-numeric string ("hello" + 234) throws a runtime error and stops the rest of the script; validate input types first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq): Don't assume ads hurt RFY; a drop in RFY impressions after ads is not a penalty, because ad-acquired users are excluded from RFY signals.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq): Avoid leading with giveaways, misleading or mismatched metadata, and non-unique games; these can reduce reach.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/multiple-parameters-and-arguments): Passing fewer arguments than parameters leaves the missing ones nil; extra arguments are silently ignored.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/avatar-creation-token): Verify the token owner is ID-verified with Roblox Plus or Premium, and keep meeting Marketplace creator requirements, or the token and in-game creation APIs stop working.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/avatar-creation-token): Makeup items can be composited into one outfit with PromptCreateMakeupAsync, but this requires a separate token per layer matching that layer's asset type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Disable the chat window for console games regardless of custom chat setup.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Console games always need content maturity information set up to ensure smooth releases and avoid removal from consoles.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/defensive-design): Don't rely on complex detection code to catch exploiters; they will find a way around it. Change the design so the abuse yields no advantage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines): Do not build alphabet wheels, number-based encoding, or question/answer structures; these are explicitly disallowed preset patterns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines): Do not let presets simulate free-form chat, flattery unrelated to gameplay, or back-and-forth dialogue across age groups.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines): Avoid slang or words with evolving meanings, real-world contact or identity references, and emotional pressure in presets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/save-player-data): Without data stores, player score and position reset every time they leave, so persistence must be added explicitly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/memory-usage): Disconnect and destroy instances you no longer need; check Unique References and Unparented Instances views for leaked, unparented instances still pinned by scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Do not rely on the client crash rate when the chart shows a dashed line (low sample size); check the total crash count instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/greybox-a-playable-area): Names are case-sensitive in Studio; misspelled World or Blockout_Parts will break later scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/speech-to-text): Enable microphone usage in Experience Settings > Communication and publish the game before STT will work; it cannot be tested otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-place-publishing): Publish from Studio if your game contains EditableImage, EditableMesh, PartOperation, SurfaceAppearance, or BaseWrap instances; the API does not update them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-assets): Metadata-only Update Asset calls are not subject to the content upload limits; only content uploads are restricted.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/intro-to-scripting): Do not test a while true loop before adding a wait; the Studio session will hang.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store): Robux cannot be used to price or buy Creator Store models; sales are USD only.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store): Do not sell composites containing Restricted assets you did not create; Roblox will not list them on the Creator Store.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store): Enable Asset Privacy before importing meshes, images and decals you plan to sell, so child assets default to Restricted.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store): Avoid obfuscated code, loadstring/require of remote assets, and bloated scripts in assets, as they break Creator Store rules and risk moderation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux): Refunds are not available for paid access purchases. Launch the price carefully, since buyers cannot get their Robux back.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux): If many users report the game as broken, it can be quarantined and escrowed earnings refunded to users. Make sure the game runs properly before charging.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-essentials): Don't stop updating: without LiveOps, even dedicated players lose interest after completing challenges and consuming all content.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching): Don't assume a data store instance's cache is shared; separate instances (different scope or AllScopes) each keep their own cache and can diverge.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching): Unsupported types may save silently as nil; debug with HttpService:JSONEncode to preview what gets stored.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Avoid violence or direct confrontation in games for classrooms; use cartoonish conflict such as spell-casting wands instead of swords.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/robux-transfers): Do not return Processed before finishing the handler's work; Processed marks the receipt complete and confirms the transfer.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/moderation): Ensure assets adhere to the Marketplace Policy before upload; non-compliant items can be rejected or removed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Do not reward random outcomes; state the exact reward, e.g., 'Watch to receive [Specific Item]'. RNG rewards are not allowed under 2026 standards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Make ad rewards account-bound. Tradable currency from ads enables alt-account farming that destroys the player marketplace.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1): If parts fall or disappear during playtesting, turn on Anchored for those parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1): Most common beginner script errors are capitalization mistakes and typos; have students check their own code first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor): Automatic mode: other clients see parameter updates with a small delay (one send interval plus network latency); do not rely on instant remote parameter state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service): Only x-api-key and content-type headers are allowed; only HTTPS works, and ports below 1024 (except 80 and 443) plus port 1194 are blocked.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service): Do not use '..' in URL path parameters; data stores or entries containing that string are inaccessible from HttpService.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/standout-games): Copies or reskins of popular Roblox games are a hard sell for Standout Games. Make something with distinct mechanics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/intro-to-digital-civility): Griefing (players disrupting others' play or destroying collaborative builds) should be countered by setting strict expectations upfront and reinforcing them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace): Test all assets thoroughly before uploading; they must meet Marketplace policy. Fixing after upload requires an update via Update an existing item.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace): Avoid using AssetIDs or TextureIDs that belong to others, existing Marketplace assets, or assets pending moderation; these cause validation failures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/skin-a-humanoid-model): Selecting the wrong bone while weight painting is a common error; confirm the active bone name shown in the viewport before painting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies): Anchoring more than one part in an assembly splits it into separate assemblies and deactivates welds between them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/voice-chat): Non-spatial voice scripts route all players to themselves too; add a LocalScript that destroys Wires under your own player to avoid hearing your own voice.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts): Omitting breakdown-dimension from filter, or sending periodOffsetMultiplier with Absolute mode, returns 400 errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/right-to-be-forgotten): Do not rely on RTBF automation if user ID is absent from key names or scopes; such entries are ineligible for automated deletion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority/techniques): Do not hold references to speculative instances and parent them in a later frame; parent them into the DataModel before the frame ends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority/techniques): Do not cache AnimationTrack objects under server authority; rollback invalidates them. Store animation IDs and fetch tracks via Animator:GetTrackByAnimationId.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/beginner-landing-page): Never share your Roblox password, even with real-life friends; choose a password that isn't a trivial variant of your username.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/variables): Extra values in a multiple assignment are silently dropped; missing values become nil.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson): Warn users that Roblox employees never ask for passwords, and that no legitimate source offers free Robux; report anyone who asks via Report Abuse.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores): Do not design against per-partition limits as fixed numbers; they are estimates set on the backend and may change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores): Flushing memory stores with Open Cloud clears all queues, sorted maps and hash maps in the scope, including live matchmaking or trading data. Flush only if the game can run without it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Avoid setting alert duration to 1 on volatile metrics; it fires on a single data point and creates noise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/experience-genres): Do not submit a new genre selection while a genre appeal is pending, as it cancels the original appeal review.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Do not reward with Robux; rewards must be developer products. Do not let ads hurt the character; pause damage or show ads only in safe zones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Resubmitting the Maturity questionnaire removes ads eligibility during review; only resubmit when a game update changes prior answers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/rights-manager): After submitting a removal request, you cannot edit it while Roblox reviews it; double-check links and details first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/rights-manager): For assets inside a game, include a screenshot and location details so Roblox can find the reported asset.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity): Base questionnaire answers on the most mature or extreme content players can encounter, not the average. Inaccurate answers can lead to game and account moderation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity): Do not add age-restricted content before receiving the Moderate or Restricted label; the label must be granted first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity): Title or description containing social hangout references will classify the game as a social hangout; keep titles and descriptions accurate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity): Genre selection does not determine maturity. Answer the questionnaire from actual in-game content even when it overlaps a genre.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity): Asset uploads (images, meshes, audio) are subject to moderation regardless of the maturity label if they show realistic violence, blood, crude humor or romantic themes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/nil): Clearing an instance's Parent to nil does not free memory while a variable still references it; also set the variable to nil.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property): Do not assume publishing a design found elsewhere gives you ownership of it on Roblox; others may still make similar designs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/playtest-the-map): Always click Stop to end a playtest; if you don't, any changes made during the playtest are lost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/cross-server-chat): Don't expect the Global tab in private servers; it is disabled there regardless of the setting, so design private-server social features around the Here tab.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/player-data-purchasing): Don't use the sample code as-is in production; it is reference only and untested over a long period in a popular game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products): Do not process purchases with PromptProductPurchaseFinished; the event firing does not mean the purchase succeeded.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products): If your game relies on cross-game developer product sales, integrate Robux transfers before May 30, 2026; cross-game sales are disabled after that date.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products): If using price optimization, run the price-showing script on the client so users see personalized prices.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/intro-to-roblox): If parts fall or disappear during playtest, turn on Anchoring for those parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): Changed also fires when attributes change, with the property name 'Attributes'; filter that out if you only want real property changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/version-history): Restoring a place does not publish it. For a live game, open the restored place, publish it, and restart servers so players get the change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/account-verification): Don't use High verification for everyday features; use Enum.VerifiedLevel.Low (the default) for standard features like in-game trading to deter alternate accounts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Do not use bots or large engagement campaigns to fake ad impressions, misclassify metadata to boost ad delivery, or obscure ad disclosures; these risk suspension and payout revocation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Do not include phone numbers or other PII in ad content that leads users off-platform; this violates ad standards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/frequently-asked-questions-education): Do not alter the Roblox logo (color, transparency, drop-shadow); type Roblox, not ROBLOX, in external communications.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/merch-booth): Wrap addItemAsync in pcall; it is a network call that can fail occasionally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2): Check capitalization carefully; one wrong letter in long API names like FindFirstChildWhichIsA causes errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits): Don't test rate limits in Studio Run mode; it uses lower static limits. Use Studio Team Create instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/roblox-user-base): Do not assume Roblox audiences match other platforms; Roblox is closer to YouTube than the App Store in how users browse and switch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models): Procedural models can make permanent edit-time changes to your game; review Creator Store generators before trusting them, since they are sandboxed by default for safety.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): MessageReceived fires twice per message: once when sending and once when the server confirms. Check for an existing label by MessageId before adding a new one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Invalid cage configurations fail validation and can be subject to moderation; fix caging issues before uploading.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Don't assume API members are parallel-safe; members without a stated thread safety level are Unsafe and can't be read or written in parallel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): Open Use is irreversible: once an asset is set to Open Use it cannot go back to Restricted. Confirm before switching.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): Game grants are permanent. Once a game is granted a restricted asset, that access cannot be revoked.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): Removing a creator as a friend does not revoke their permission on restricted assets. Revoke explicitly on the asset's Permissions page.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): Asset Privacy does not affect Audio, Video, Models, MeshParts, Animations, or Packages. If those fail to load, look elsewhere for the cause.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): A Decal can only be set to Open Use if its Image dependency is Open Use. Set the Image first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): Avatar items need all dependent textures and meshes set to Open Use before Marketplace upload. Convert restricted primitives first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy): If a game uses a restricted asset in a script or saves a template place with it, the game itself must have permission. Otherwise the asset is invisible or silent at runtime.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/avatar-settings): Avatar Settings changes hidden game defaults not visible in scripts; test with preview and playtesting before publishing to a live game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Resolve warning and error icons in the hierarchy before importing; warnings flag issues that cause errors or unexpected behavior.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): paymentType and budget.type are fixed after creation; choose CREDIT_CARD/ADS_CREDIT/INVOICE and DAILY/LIFETIME deliberately.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/stacks): Guard pop() with an isEmpty() check; popping an empty stack returns nil and can cause errors downstream.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/create-teams): Without custom spawn logic, team players spawn at the same random spawn zone and end up next to each other; restrict spawns per team.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup): Makeup only works on R15 characters with MeshPart heads; other rigs will not support it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-filtering): Games that display user text without filtering can be removed by Roblox until filtering is added.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-filtering): Do not filter per character while typing; filtered output only shows to the typing user. Filter after submit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding): Most common beginner Luau errors are improper capitalization and typos; check these first when debugging.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies): Launching an event without estimating economic impact causes Gold surplus, players stop buying, and revenue drops after the event ends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): Do not yield inside TextChatService callbacks (ShouldDeliverCallback, OnIncomingMessage, OnBubbleAdded, OnChatWindowAdded); yielding blocks the chat system.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): Avoid multiple TextChannels with the same name; it can cause unintended behavior with the default chat window.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): When platform-integrated chat is enabled, custom chat must use Roblox native systems; message content is not accessible in code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/gltf-export): Check asset permissions before exporting: Avatar Marketplace, acquired Creator Store, and unshared collaborator or public Open Use assets cannot be exported.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-dcc): Expect moderation: Roblox moderates every asset on upload and can moderate assets again after the initial upload.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Folders must be empty before deletion; move or delete contents first or the delete action fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization): Manual translations always display to players, while automatic translations only fill in strings that are missing, so review manual entries carefully.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/specifications): Remove extraneous Scripts and additional Parts from the Model; they violate Marketplace body requirements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Don't highlight every interactable: the team's Highlight on all objects caused visual noise; restrict highlights to corrupted or dangerous items.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Don't attach lore to everything: players couldn't tell important story from flavor, so the team removed much of it and relied on visual cues.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/pairs-and-ipairs): Dictionary iteration order with pairs() is not guaranteed; use an array with ipairs() when order matters, such as leaderboards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery): Use a custom thumbnail; default-thumbnail games are ineligible for Home recommendation impressions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery): Avoid metadata and visuals that closely copy existing games; non-unique games rank lower.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/capabilities): Disabling a script or setting Sandboxed=false on a caller weakens security; only do it when strictly necessary.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Avoid cluttering mobile UIs with excessive buttons, screens and text; small screens get overwhelmed quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Apply UI decisions consistently everywhere (e.g. same green for Health in text, icons and bar; red price when unaffordable). Inconsistency causes confusion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/use-parameters-and-events): Anchor the trap part and keep it from touching anything when placed; otherwise it may fire too early or fall and destroy things unintentionally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops): Place all loop instructions between 'while ... do' and 'end'; code outside that block will not run in the loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops): Ensure RGB values are numbers from 0 to 255 separated by commas, otherwise the part stays gray or does not change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops): Remember a local variable only exists in the script where it was created; it cannot be read from another script.
- 2026-10-29 Deconstructor of Fun [26:33](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=1593): Be aware AI disclosure labels may reduce Steam unit sales in western markets; weigh before checking the box, though the data is confounded by top-game skew.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-2d-isometric-and-top-down-games-with-orthographic-camera/4966772): Do not ship orthographic cameras yet: published games cannot use them during beta, and the API may change.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization/auto-translate-dynamic-content): Don't expect to test the translation API in Studio locally; it only supports RCC auth, so deploy to a test instance via collaborative testing.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers): ReserveServerAsync() does not actually reserve a server; it only generates a random GUID. Migrate to HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId.
